    local function GetBullet()
        if workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored") and workspace.Ignored:FindFirstChild("Siren") and workspace.Ignored.Siren:FindFirstChild("Radius") then
            return {
                BulletPath = workspace.Ignored.Siren.Radius,
                BulletName = "BULLET_RAYS",
                BulletBeamName = "GunBeam"
            }
        elseif workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored") then
            return {
                BulletPath = workspace.Ignored,
                BulletName = "BULLET_RAYS",
                BulletBeamName = "GunBeam"
            }
        elseif workspace then
            return {
                BulletPath = workspace,
                BulletName = "Part",
                BulletBeamName = "gb"
            }
        end
        return nil
    end
    
    local Support = GetBullet()
    local BulletBeamName = (Support.BulletBeamName) or nil
    local BulletName = (Support.BulletName) or nil
    local BulletPath = (Support.BulletPath) or nil
    
    local SoundsOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Table.Sounds) do
        table.insert(SoundsOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local SkyboxOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Skybox) do
        table.insert(SkyboxOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local ChatSpamOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Table.ChatSpam) do
        table.insert(ChatSpamOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local AnimationPlayerOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Table.Animations) do
        table.insert(AnimationPlayerOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local HitEffectOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Table.HitEffect) do
        table.insert(HitEffectOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local EmoteSpamOptions = {}
    for Name, _ in pairs(Table.Emotes) do
        table.insert(EmoteSpamOptions, Name)
    end
    
    local MainEvent = nil
	(function()
		for _, Remote in pairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetDescendants()) do
			if (Remote.Name == "MainEvent" or Remote.Name == "MainRemote" or Remote.Name == "MAIN_EVENT" or Remote.Name == "MAINEVENT") and Remote.ClassName == "RemoteEvent" then
				MainEvent = Remote
			end 
		end
	end)()
	
	-- // Utility
	local Utility = {} do
	    Utility.IsVisible = function(Start, Result, Other)
            return #Camera:GetPartsObscuringTarget({Start, Result}, Other) == 0
        end
        
        Utility.WorldToScreen = function(Position)
	    	local Position, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Position)

		    return {Position = NewVector2(Position.X, Position.Y), OnScreen = OnScreen}
	    end
	
	    Utility.IsAlive = function(Player)
            return (Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")) and true or false
        end
        
        Utility.IsCharacterLoaded = function()
			local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
			if Character then 
				return Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			end
		end
	
	    Utility.IsInAir = function(Player)
            if (not (Utility.IsAlive(Player))) then return false end

	    	local RootPart = Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart

		    return RootPart.Velocity.Y ~= 0
        end
	
        Utility.IsFriendsWith = function(Player)
            return Player:IsFriendsWith(LocalPlayer.UserId) and true or false
        end
        
        Utility.GetTool = function()
            if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") then 
                return LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") 
            end
        end
        
	    Utility.NewConnection = function(Type, Callback)
            local Connection = Type:Connect(Callback)
            
    		table.insert(Table.Connections, Connection)
            
    		return Connection
        end
        
        Utility.NewDrawing = function(Class, Properties)
            local Surge = Drawing.new(Class)
            Surge.Visible = false
            for Property, Value in pairs(Properties) do
                Surge[Property] = Value
            end
            return Surge
        end
        
        Utility.NewObject = function(InstanceType, Properties)
            local Instance = Instance.new(InstanceType)
        
            for Property, Value in pairs(Properties) do
                Instance[Property] = Value
            end
        
            return Instance
        end
        
        Utility.PlaySound = function(Volume, SoundID)
            local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		    Sound.Parent = Workspace
		    Sound.SoundId = SoundID
		    Sound.Volume = Volume

		    Sound:Play()

		    Utility.NewConnection(Sound.Ended, function()
			    Sound:Destroy()
		    end)
        end
        
        -- Background Noise System
        local BackgroundSound = nil
        local BackgroundSounds = {
            Ocean = "rbxassetid://1841647093",
            Night = "rbxassetid://1837803098", 
            Rain = "rbxassetid://1837811146",
            Thunderstorm = "rbxassetid://3149250700",
            Forest = "rbxassetid://1837804589"
        }
        
        Utility.PlayBackgroundNoise = function(NoiseType, Volume)
            if BackgroundSound then
                BackgroundSound:Stop()
                BackgroundSound:Destroy()
                BackgroundSound = nil
            end
            
            if NoiseType and BackgroundSounds[NoiseType] then
                BackgroundSound = Instance.new("Sound")
                BackgroundSound.Name = "freemium.lolBackgroundNoise"
                BackgroundSound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                BackgroundSound.SoundId = BackgroundSounds[NoiseType]
                BackgroundSound.Volume = Volume or 0.5
                BackgroundSound.Looped = true
                BackgroundSound:Play()
            end
        end
        
        Utility.StopBackgroundNoise = function()
            if BackgroundSound then
                BackgroundSound:Stop()
                BackgroundSound:Destroy()
                BackgroundSound = nil
            end
        end
        
        Utility.UpdateBackgroundVolume = function(Volume)
            if BackgroundSound and BackgroundSound.Playing then
                BackgroundSound.Volume = Volume
            end
        end
        
        -- Custom Music System
        local CustomMusicSound = nil
        
        Utility.PlayCustomMusic = function(SoundID, Volume)
            if CustomMusicSound then
                CustomMusicSound:Stop()
                CustomMusicSound:Destroy()
                CustomMusicSound = nil
            end
            
            if SoundID and SoundID ~= "" then
                CustomMusicSound = Instance.new("Sound")
                CustomMusicSound.Name = "freemium.lolCustomMusic"
                CustomMusicSound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                -- Add rbxassetid:// if not already present
                if not string.find(SoundID, "rbxassetid://") then
                    SoundID = "rbxassetid://" .. SoundID
                end
                CustomMusicSound.SoundId = SoundID
                CustomMusicSound.Volume = Volume or 0.5
                CustomMusicSound.Looped = true
                CustomMusicSound:Play()
            end
        end
        
        Utility.StopCustomMusic = function()
            if CustomMusicSound then
                CustomMusicSound:Stop()
                CustomMusicSound:Destroy()
                CustomMusicSound = nil
            end
        end
        
        Utility.UpdateCustomMusicVolume = function(Volume)
            if CustomMusicSound and CustomMusicSound.Playing then
                CustomMusicSound.Volume = Volume
            end
        end
        
        Utility.CreateBeam = function(From, To, Color1, Color2, Texture, Duration, FadeEnabled, FadeDuration, Material)
            local Tween, TotalTime = nil, 0
            Material = Material or "SmoothPlastic"
        
            local MainPart = Utility.NewObject("Part", {
                Parent = Workspace,
                Size = NewVector3(0, 0, 0),
                Massless = true,
                Transparency = 1,
                CanCollide = false,
                Position = From,
                Anchored = true
            })
        
            local Part0 = Utility.NewObject("Part", {
                Parent = MainPart,
                Size = NewVector3(0, 0, 0),
                Massless = true,
                Transparency = 1,
                CanCollide = false,
                Position = From,
                Anchored = true,
                Material = Material == "Neon" and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            })
        
            local Part1 = Utility.NewObject("Part", {
                Parent = MainPart,
                Size = NewVector3(0, 0, 0),
                Massless = true,
                Transparency = 1,
                CanCollide = false,
                Position = To,
                Anchored = true,
                Material = Material == "Neon" and Enum.Material.Neon or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
            })
        
            local Attachment0 = Utility.NewObject("Attachment", {
                Parent = Part0
            })
        
            local Attachment1 = Utility.NewObject("Attachment", {
                Parent = Part1
            })
            
            -- Determine if we should use neon texture or normal texture
            local BeamTexture = Table.Textures[Texture]
            if Material == "Neon" then
                BeamTexture = "rbxasset://textures/whiteCircle.png"
            end
            
            local Beam = Utility.NewObject("Beam", {
                Texture = BeamTexture,
                TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Wrap,
                TextureLength = Material == "Neon" and 5 or 10,
                LightEmission = Material == "Neon" and 5 or 0.5,
                Segments = 10,
                LightInfluence = 0,
                Brightness = Material == "Neon" and 15 or 5,
                TextureSpeed = 3,
                Width0 = Material == "Neon" and 2.5 or 1.7,
                Width1 = Material == "Neon" and 2.5 or 1.7,
                FaceCamera = true,
                ZOffset = -1,
                Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Material == "Neon" and 0.2 or 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
                }),
                Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color1),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color2),
                }),
                Attachment0 = Attachment0,
                Attachment1 = Attachment1,
                Enabled = true,
                Parent = MainPart
            })
        
            if FadeEnabled then
                Tween = Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, function(DeltaTime)
                    TotalTime += DeltaTime
                    Beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(TweenService:GetValue((TotalTime / FadeDuration), Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In))
                end)
            end
        
            task.delay(Duration, function()
                MainPart:Destroy()
                if Tween then Tween:Disconnect() end
            end)
        end
        
        Utility.CreateDrawingBeam = function(StartPos, EndPos, Color1, Color2, Duration, FadeEnabled, FadeDuration, Neon)
            local Line = Drawing.new("Line")
            local LineOutline = Drawing.new("Line")
            local NeonGlow = nil
            
            Line.Visible = true
            Line.Thickness = Neon and 2 or 1
            Line.Color = Color1
            Line.ZIndex = 9999
            
            LineOutline.Visible = true
            LineOutline.Thickness = Neon and 5 or 3
            LineOutline.Color = Color2
            
            -- Add neon glow layer
            if Neon then
                NeonGlow = Drawing.new("Line")
                NeonGlow.Visible = true
                NeonGlow.Thickness = 8
                NeonGlow.Color = Color1
                NeonGlow.Transparency = 0.3
                NeonGlow.ZIndex = 9998
            end
            
            local StartTime = tick()
        
            local function UpdateLine()
                local StartViewport = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(StartPos)
                local EndViewport = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(EndPos)
                local IsVisible = StartViewport.Z > 0 and EndViewport.Z > 0
        
                if IsVisible then
                    Line.From = NewVector2(StartViewport.X, StartViewport.Y)
                    Line.To = NewVector2(EndViewport.X, EndViewport.Y)
                    Line.Visible = true
                    
                    LineOutline.From = Line.From
                    LineOutline.To = Line.To
                    LineOutline.Visible = true
                    
                    if NeonGlow then
                        NeonGlow.From = Line.From
                        NeonGlow.To = Line.To
                        NeonGlow.Visible = true
                    end
                else
                    Line.Visible = false
                    LineOutline.Visible = false
                    if NeonGlow then NeonGlow.Visible = false end
                end
            end
        
            local TweenConnection
            TweenConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(DeltaTime)
                UpdateLine()
        
                local ElapsedTime = tick() - StartTime
        
                if FadeEnabled then
                    local Alpha = 0
                    if ElapsedTime <= FadeDuration then
                        Alpha = TweenService:GetValue(ElapsedTime / FadeDuration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
                    else
                        Alpha = 1
                    end
                    LineOutline.Transparency = Alpha
                    if NeonGlow then
                        NeonGlow.Transparency = Clamp(0.3 + (Alpha * 0.7), 0, 1)
                    end
                else
                    Line.Transparency = 1
                    LineOutline.Transparency = 1
                    if NeonGlow then NeonGlow.Transparency = 0.3 end
                end
        
                if ElapsedTime >= Duration then
                    TweenConnection:Disconnect()
                    Line:Remove()
                    LineOutline:Remove()
                    if NeonGlow then NeonGlow:Remove() end
                end
            end)
        end        
        
        Utility.CreateImpact = function(Color, Size, FadeEnabled, FadeDuration, Duration, Position)
            local Impact = Utility.NewObject("Part", {
                CanCollide = false,
                Material = Enum.Material.Neon,
                Size = NewVector3(Size,Size,Size),
                Color = Color,
                Position = Position,
                Anchored = true,
                Parent = workspace
            })
        
            local Outline = Utility.NewObject("SelectionBox", { --// credits to xander
                LineThickness = 0.01,
                Color3 = Color,
                SurfaceTransparency = 1,
                Adornee = Impact,
                Visible = true,
                Parent = Impact
            })
        
            if FadeEnabled then
                local TweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(Duration)
                local Tween = TweenService:Create(Impact, TweenInfo, {Transparency = 1})
                local TweenOutline = TweenService:Create(Outline, TweenInfo, {Transparency = 1})
        
                Tween:Play()
                TweenOutline:Play()
            end
        
            task.delay(Duration, function()
                Impact:Destroy()  
            end)
        end
        
        Utility.CreateButton = function(Name, Callback, SizeMultiply, TextOnly)
            do
                local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
                local IconImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                local ButtonText = Instance.new("TextButton")
                local TextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
                SizeMultiply = SizeMultiply or 1
        
                ScreenGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
                ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        
                Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                Frame.BackgroundColor3 = TextOnly and NewRGB(255,255,255) or NewRGB(0,0,0)
                Frame.BackgroundTransparency = TextOnly and 0 or 0.3
                Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, Random(-128,128), 0, 0)
                Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, (TextOnly and 150 or 90)*SizeMultiply, 0, (TextOnly and 36 or 32)*SizeMultiply)
                Frame.Active = true
                Frame.BorderSizePixel = TextOnly and 1 or 0
                Frame.BorderColor3 = NewRGB(0,0,0)
        
                IconImage.Parent = Frame
                IconImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                IconImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26*SizeMultiply, 0, 26*SizeMultiply)
                IconImage.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0,0.5)
                IconImage.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
                IconImage.Image = ""
                IconImage.Visible = not TextOnly
        
                ButtonText.Parent = Frame
                ButtonText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ButtonText.Size = TextOnly and UDim2.new(1, -8*SizeMultiply, 1, -4*SizeMultiply) or UDim2.new(0, 52*SizeMultiply, 0, 26*SizeMultiply)
                ButtonText.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0.5,0.5)
                ButtonText.Position = TextOnly and UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0.5, 0)
                ButtonText.Font = Enum.Font.Arimo
                ButtonText.Text = Name
                ButtonText.TextColor3 = TextOnly and NewRGB(0,0,0) or NewRGB(255,255,255)
                ButtonText.TextScaled = true
        
                local UiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", Frame)
                UiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)
        
                local ButtonState = false
                Utility.NewConnection(ButtonText.MouseButton1Down, function()
                    ButtonState = not ButtonState
                    Callback(ButtonState)
                    IconImage.Image = ButtonState and "rbxassetid://133131442644680" or "rbxassetid://133131442644680"
                end)
        
                local Dragging = false
                local DragInput, DragStart, StartPos
        
                local function Update(Input)
                    if not DragStart then return end
                    local Delta = Input.Position - DragStart
                    Frame.Position = UDim2.new(StartPos.X.Scale, StartPos.X.Offset + Delta.X, StartPos.Y.Scale, StartPos.Y.Offset + Delta.Y)
                end
        
                Utility.NewConnection(Frame.InputBegan, function(Input)
                    if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        Dragging = true
                        DragStart = Input.Position
                        StartPos = Frame.Position
        
                        Utility.NewConnection(Input.Changed, function()
                            if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                                Dragging = false
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
        
                Utility.NewConnection(Frame.InputChanged, function(Input)
                    if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        DragInput = Input
                    end
                end)
        
                Utility.NewConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged, function(Input)
                    if Dragging and Input == DragInput then
                        Update(Input)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        
        Utility.SetCenterPanelText = function(Text)
            local New = Table.Status[Text]
            if not New then
                return
            end
            if not CenterPanelText or New < (Table.Status[CenterPanelText] or math.huge) then
                CenterPanelText = Text
            end
        end
        
        -- Rainbow Color Function
        Utility.GetRainbowColor = function()
            RainbowHue = (RainbowHue + 0.01) % 1
            return Color3.fromHSV(RainbowHue, 1, 1)
        end
        
        -- Kill Screen Effect (Hit Stacking)
        Utility.CreateHitEffect = function(Color, Intensity, Duration)
            if KillScreenEffect then
                -- Don't destroy immediately, let the fade complete
                local OldEffect = KillScreenEffect
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(Duration + 0.1)
                    if OldEffect and OldEffect.Parent then
                        OldEffect.Parent:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
            end
            
            -- Clamp intensity between 0 and 1
            Intensity = Clamp(Intensity, 0, 1)
            Duration = Duration or 0.3
            
            local ScreenGui = Utility.NewObject("ScreenGui", {
                Parent = gethui(),
                IgnoreGuiInset = true,
                DisplayOrder = 10000
            })
            
            KillScreenEffect = Utility.NewObject("Frame", {
                Parent = ScreenGui,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color,
                BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (Intensity * 0.5), -- Max 50% opacity at full intensity
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                ZIndex = 10000
            })
            
            task.spawn(function()
                -- Wait a tiny bit before starting fade
                task.wait(0.05)
                -- Fade out over the duration
                TweenService:Create(KillScreenEffect, TweenInfo.new(Duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1
                }):Play()
                
                -- Clean up after fade completes
                task.wait(Duration + 0.1)
                if ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent then
                    ScreenGui:Destroy()
                end
                if KillScreenEffect == ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
                    KillScreenEffect = nil
                end
            end)
        end
	end
	
	-- // Custom Math
	local CustomMath = {} do
	    CustomMath.CFrameToOffset = function(Origin, Target)
            local ActualOrigin = Origin * NewCFrame(0, -0.25, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, -1, 0)
            return ActualOrigin:ToObjectSpace(Target):inverse()
        end
        
        CustomMath.RandomVector3 = function(Randomization)
            return NewVector3(Random(-Randomization, Randomization), Random(-Randomization, Randomization), Random(-Randomization, Randomization))
        end
        
        CustomMath.RecalculateVelocity = function(Part, UpdateTime)
            local CurrentPosition = Part.Position
            local CurrentTime = tick()
            task.wait(1 / UpdateTime)
            local NewPosition = Part.Position
            local NewTime = tick()
            local DistanceTraveled = NewPosition - CurrentPosition
            local TimeInterval = NewTime - CurrentTime
            local Velocity = DistanceTraveled / TimeInterval
            return Velocity
        end
        
        CustomMath.SafeUnit = function(Unit)
            if Unit.Magnitude > 1e-8 then
                return Unit
            end
            
            return Vector3.zero
        end
        
        CustomMath.DeviceInFrame = function(Frame)
            local BoxPosition = Frame.Position
            local BoxSize = Frame.Size
                
            return DevicePositionX >= BoxPosition.X and DevicePositionX <= BoxPosition.X + BoxSize.X and DevicePositionY >= BoxPosition.Y and DevicePositionY <= BoxPosition.Y + BoxSize.Y
        end
	end
	
	-- // Custom DaHood Functions
    local DaHood = {} do
        DaHood.IsKnocked = function(Player)
            if not Player then return false end
    
            local Character = Player.Character
            if not Character then return false end
    
            local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not Humanoid then return false end
    
            local Value
    
            if GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" then
                local BodyEffects = Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                local KO = BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
                Value = KO and KO.Value or false
    
            else
                Value = Humanoid.Health <= 10
            end
    
            return Value
        end
        
        DaHood.IsGrabbed = function(Player)
            return Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("GRABBING_CONSTRAINT") ~= nil
        end
        
        DaHood.IsCrew = function(Player)
            return Player and Player.DataFolder and Player.DataFolder.Information.Crew.Value == LocalPlayer.DataFolder.Information.Crew.Value or false
        end
    end
	
	-- // Combat
	local Combat = {} do
	    Combat.GetClosestPoint = function(Part)
            local ScreenCenter = Camera.ViewportSize / 2
            local MouseRay = IsMobile and Camera.ViewportPointToRay(Camera, ScreenCenter.X, ScreenCenter.Y) or LocalPlayer.Mouse
            MouseRay = MouseRay.Origin + (MouseRay.Direction * (Part.Position - MouseRay.Origin).Magnitude)
        
            local PartCF = Part.CFrame
            local HalfSize = Part.Size / 2
            local LocalPos = PartCF.PointToObjectSpace(PartCF, MouseRay)
        
            local ClampedLocal = Vector3.new(
                math.clamp(LocalPos.X, -HalfSize.X, HalfSize.X),
                math.clamp(LocalPos.Y, -HalfSize.Y, HalfSize.Y),
                math.clamp(LocalPos.Z, -HalfSize.Z, HalfSize.Z)
            )
        
            local ClosestPoint = PartCF.PointToWorldSpace(PartCF, ClampedLocal)
        
            local Params = RaycastParams.new()
            Params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
            Params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Part}
        
            local Ray = workspace.Raycast(workspace, Camera.CFrame.Position, (ClosestPoint - Camera.CFrame.Position), Params)
            if Ray then
                return Ray.Position
            else
                return ClosestPoint
            end
        end
        
        Combat.GetClosestPart = function(Player)
            local ClosestCharacterPart
            local RealPosition = IsMobile and (Camera.ViewportSize / 2) or UserInputService.GetMouseLocation(UserInputService)
            local ClosestDistance = math.huge
        
            for _, Part in ipairs(Player.Character.GetChildren(Player.Character)) do
                if not Part.IsA(Part, "BasePart") then continue end
                if Part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then continue end
        
                local screenPos, onScreen = Camera.WorldToViewportPoint(Camera, Part.Position)
                if not onScreen then continue end
        
                local Distance = (RealPosition - Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)).Magnitude
        
                if Distance <= ClosestDistance then
                    ClosestDistance = Distance
                    ClosestCharacterPart = Part
                end
            end
        
            return ClosestCharacterPart
        end
        
	    Combat.GetClosestPlayer = function(Position, FovEnabled, FovRadius, ChecksEnabled, ChecksFlags)
            local Target, Closest = nil, FovEnabled and FovRadius or math.huge
            local Origin = Position == "Mouse" and NewVector2(Mouse.X, Mouse.Y) or NewVector2(DevicePositionX, DevicePositionY)
        
            for _, v in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if v ~= LocalPlayer and v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    local RootPart = Utility.WorldToScreen(v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                    if not RootPart.OnScreen then continue end
                    local Distance = (RootPart.Position - Origin).Magnitude
                    if Distance < Closest then
                        if ChecksEnabled then
                            if Find(ChecksFlags, "Knocked") and DaHood.IsKnocked(v, 10) then continue end
                            if Find(ChecksFlags, "Grabbed") and DaHood.IsGrabbed(v) then continue end
                            if Find(ChecksFlags, "Crew") and DaHood.IsCrew(v) then continue end
                            if Find(ChecksFlags, "Friend") and Utility.IsFriendsWith(v) then continue end
                            if Find(ChecksFlags, "Not Visible") and not Utility.IsVisible(v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, {v.Character.HumanoidRootPart, v.Character, LocalPlayer.Character, Workspace.Ignored}) then continue end
                        end
                        Closest = Distance
                        Target = v
                    end
                end
            end
            return Target
        end
        
        Combat.CalculateResolverOffset = function(Player, Method, UpdateTime)
            if not Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
        
            local ActualUpdateTime = UpdateTime or 100
            local Velocity
        
            if Method == "Recalculate Velocity" then
                Velocity = CustomMath.RecalculateVelocity(Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart, ActualUpdateTime)
            elseif Method == "Suppress Velocity" then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.X, 0, Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Z)
            elseif Method == "Move Direction" then
                Velocity = Player.Character.Humanoid.MoveDirection * Player.Character.Humanoid.WalkSpeed
            end
        
            return Velocity
        end
        
        Combat.CalculateAimViewerEndPoint = function()
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiAimViewer and Table.SilentAim.Target and Table.SilentAim.AbleToShoot then
                local EndPoint = Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemoteArgs
                
                EndPoint[Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemotePositionIndex] = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
                Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemote:FireServer(unpack(EndPoint))
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- // Advanced Resolver (Multi-Mode: Cluster, Predict, Exponential, Defensive, Auto)
    local AdvancedResolver = {} do
        -- Per-target resolver state table, keyed by player instance
        local ResolverState = {}

        local _abs   = math.abs
        local _clamp = math.clamp
        local _Vec3  = Vector3.new
        local _ins   = table.insert
        local _rem   = table.remove

        -- Lazily create state for each player
        local function GetState(player)
            if not ResolverState[player] then
                ResolverState[player] = {
                    -- Cluster (pattern detection)
                    positionLog  = {},
                    foundPattern = nil,
                    lastRefresh  = tick(),
                    -- Predict (velocity lead)
                    lastPos      = nil,
                    lastTime     = nil,
                    -- Exponential (bounce offset)
                    expDist      = 0,
                    expDir       = 1,
                    -- Defensive (void-spam weight tracker)
                    defensivePos   = {},
                    lastDefUpdate  = tick(),
                    -- Shared velocity
                    velocity     = _Vec3(0, 0, 0),
                    lastVelPos   = nil,
                    lastVelTime  = tick(),
                }
            end
            return ResolverState[player]
        end

        -- Clean up state on player leave
        Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
            ResolverState[p] = nil
        end)

        -- Track real velocity for any target
        local function TrackVelocity(state, pos)
            local now = tick()
            local dt  = now - state.lastVelTime
            if dt > 0 and state.lastVelPos then
                state.velocity = (pos - state.lastVelPos) / dt
            end
            state.lastVelPos  = pos
            state.lastVelTime = now
        end

        -- ── MODE 1: Cluster (Pattern Detection) ──────────────────────────
        -- Finds the spatial cluster where the target most often sits.
        -- Best vs jitter bots, randomised AAs, position cycling.
        local function ResolveCluster(state, root, pos)
            local now         = tick()
            local refreshTime = getgenv().ue_refreshtime or 3
            local forgiveness = getgenv().ue_forgiveness or 14.4
            local distPenalty = getgenv().ue_distpenalty or 2
            local voidBonus   = getgenv().ue_voidbonus   or 5
            local minCluster  = 4

            -- Periodic reset so old data doesn't corrupt aim
            if now - state.lastRefresh >= refreshTime then
                state.positionLog  = {}
                state.foundPattern = nil
                state.lastRefresh  = now
            end

            -- Void proximity: loosen forgiveness at map edge
            local flatDist = _abs(pos.X) + _abs(pos.Z)
            if flatDist < 8955 then
                forgiveness = forgiveness + voidBonus
            end

            -- Distance-based tightening
            local lchar = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            if lchar and lchar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local dist    = (pos - lchar.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                local penalty = (dist / 100) * distPenalty
                forgiveness   = _clamp(forgiveness - penalty, 1, 100)
            end

            -- Record sample
            _ins(state.positionLog, { pos = pos, time = now })
            if #state.positionLog > 500 then _rem(state.positionLog, 1) end
            if #state.positionLog < 10 then return pos end

            -- Build density clusters
            local log      = state.positionLog
            local clusters = {}
            for i = 1, #log do
                local base  = log[i].pos
                local count = 0
                local sum   = _Vec3(0, 0, 0)
                for j = 1, #log do
                    local tp = log[j].pos
                    if (base - tp).Magnitude <= forgiveness then
                        count += 1
                        sum   += tp
                    end
                end
                if count >= minCluster then
                    _ins(clusters, { pos = sum / count, count = count })
                end
            end

            -- Pick the densest cluster
            local best = nil
            for _, c in ipairs(clusters) do
                if not best or c.count > best.count then best = c end
            end

            if best then
                state.foundPattern = best.pos
                return best.pos
            end
            return pos
        end

        -- ── MODE 2: Predict (Velocity Lead) ──────────────────────────────
        -- Extrapolates target's future position using movement delta.
        -- Best vs strafe bots and slow predictable AAs.
        local function ResolvePredict(state, root, pos)
            local now    = tick()
            local result = pos

            local predMode = getgenv().ue_pred_mode or "Custom"
            local predMult = getgenv().ue_pred_mult or 2.0

            if state.lastPos and state.lastTime then
                local dt = now - state.lastTime
                if dt > 0 and dt < 0.5 then
                    local vel   = (pos - state.lastPos) / dt
                    local speed = vel.Magnitude
                    if speed > 0.001 then
                        local strength = predMult * 0.01
                        if predMode == "Custom" then
                            local dist = (pos - state.lastPos).Magnitude
                            local pred = (dist / dt) * strength
                            result = pos + vel.Unit * pred
                        else -- Regular
                            result = pos + vel * strength
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            state.lastPos  = root.Position
            state.lastTime = now
            return result
        end

        -- ── MODE 3: Exponential (Bounce Offset) ───────────────────────────
        -- Oscillates a vertical offset on the target's up-vector.
        -- Best vs flip AAs that alternate between two fixed positions.
        local function ResolveExponential(state, root, pos)
            local minD = getgenv().ue_exp_min or 0
            local maxD = getgenv().ue_exp_max or 10
            local step = (maxD - minD) / 10

            state.expDist = state.expDist + step * state.expDir
            if state.expDist >= maxD then
                state.expDist = maxD
                state.expDir  = -1
            elseif state.expDist <= minD then
                state.expDist = minD
                state.expDir  = 1
            end

            return pos + root.CFrame.UpVector * state.expDist
        end

        -- ── MODE 4: Defensive (Void-Spam Weight Tracker) ──────────────────
        -- Accumulates weighted position history. Decays stale entries.
        -- Commits aim to the highest-trust position.
        -- Best vs void-spammers and defensive anti-aim.
        local function ResolveDefensive(state, root, pos)
            local now        = tick()
            local posWeight  = getgenv().ue_def_pos_weight  or 1.5
            local voidWeight = getgenv().ue_def_void_weight or 0.2
            local forgetRate = getgenv().ue_def_forget_rate or 80
            local accuracy   = getgenv().ue_def_accuracy    or 1.35
            local lerp       = getgenv().ue_def_lerp        or 0.10

            local inVoid  = (_abs(pos.X) + _abs(pos.Z)) >= 8955
            local wToAdd  = inVoid and voidWeight or posWeight

            -- Decay and prune old positions
            local toRemove = {}
            for p, data in pairs(state.defensivePos) do
                local dt   = now - data.lastUpdate
                local rate = forgetRate / 20
                data.weight -= ((p - pos).Magnitude > 200 and dt * (rate * 2.5) or dt * rate)
                data.lastUpdate = now
                if data.weight < 0.1 then toRemove[#toRemove + 1] = p end
            end
            for _, p in ipairs(toRemove) do state.defensivePos[p] = nil end

            -- Merge close positions or insert new
            local merged = false
            for p, data in pairs(state.defensivePos) do
                if (p - pos).Magnitude <= 200 then
                    local newP = p:Lerp(pos, lerp)
                    state.defensivePos[newP] = {
                        weight     = _clamp(data.weight + wToAdd, -1, 18),
                        lastUpdate = now
                    }
                    state.defensivePos[p] = nil
                    merged = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not merged then
                state.defensivePos[pos] = { weight = wToAdd, lastUpdate = now }
            end

            -- Commit to highest-weight candidate
            local bestP, bestW = nil, 0
            for p, data in pairs(state.defensivePos) do
                if data.weight > bestW then
                    bestW = data.weight
                    bestP = p
                end
            end

            if bestP and bestW > accuracy then
                return bestP
            end
            return pos
        end

        -- ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        -- Returns a resolver-corrected position for a player target.
        AdvancedResolver.GetResolvedPosition = function(player)
            if not player or not player.Character then return nil end
            local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root then return nil end

            local pos   = root.Position
            local state = GetState(player)
            TrackVelocity(state, pos)

            local mode = getgenv().ue_resolver_mode or "auto"

            if mode == "cluster" then
                return ResolveCluster(state, root, pos)
            elseif mode == "predict" then
                return ResolvePredict(state, root, pos)
            elseif mode == "exponential" then
                return ResolveExponential(state, root, pos)
            elseif mode == "defensive" then
                return ResolveDefensive(state, root, pos)
            else
                -- "auto": cluster first, fall back to predict if no pattern locked
                local clustered = ResolveCluster(state, root, pos)
                if state.foundPattern then
                    return clustered
                end
                return ResolvePredict(state, root, pos)
            end
        end

        -- Legacy wrappers so nothing else in the script breaks
        AdvancedResolver.GetResolvedTarget = AdvancedResolver.GetResolvedPosition
        AdvancedResolver.ResolvePosition   = function(root, pos)
            return pos -- passthrough; real logic is per-player above
        end

        -- Expose state for the indicator to read resolver status
        AdvancedResolver.State = ResolverState
    end

    -- // Ragebot
	local Ragebot = {} do
	    -- // Spam Resolver state (void_spam_resolver pattern)
	    local defensive_positions = {}
	    local void_spam_resolver_accuracy      = 1.35   -- computed from accuracy slider
	    local void_spam_resolver_lerp          = 0.1
	    local void_spam_resolver_void_weight   = 0.2
	    local void_spam_resolver_position_weight = 1.5
	    -- forget_rate is read from Settings each frame

	    -- Clear positions on target change
	    local _sr_last_target = nil
	    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	        local cur = Table.Ragebot.Target
	        if cur ~= _sr_last_target then
	            defensive_positions = {}
	            _sr_last_target = cur
	        end
	    end)

	    -- Setters exposed to UI (upvalue writes into this do-block)
	    Ragebot.SetSpamResolverPositionWeight = function(v) void_spam_resolver_position_weight = v end
	    Ragebot.SetSpamResolverVoidWeight     = function(v) void_spam_resolver_void_weight = v end
	    Ragebot.SetSpamResolverAccuracy       = function(v) void_spam_resolver_accuracy = 1 + (0.6525 * ((-50 + v) / 50)) end
	    Ragebot.SetSpamResolverLerp           = function(v) void_spam_resolver_lerp = v / 100 end

	    Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        -- 1) Advanced multi-mode resolver (highest priority when enabled)
	        if getgenv().ue_resolver_enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	            local ResolvedPosition = AdvancedResolver.GetResolvedPosition(Table.Ragebot.Target)
	            if ResolvedPosition then
	                return ResolvedPosition
	            end
	        end

	        -- 2) Random-teleport / Network resolver forced position
	        if (Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.RandomTeleport or Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver) and Table.Ragebot.ForcePos then
	            return Table.Ragebot.ForcePos
	        end

	        -- 3) Standard velocity resolver or raw HRP velocity
	        local Velocity
	        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.RandomTeleport or Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver then
	            Velocity = Table.Ragebot.Velocity or NewVector3()
	        else
	            Velocity = Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.Ragebot.Target, Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.Method, Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
	        end

	        -- 4) Hit-part selection
            local HitPart = (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Root" and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Head" and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.Ragebot.Target).Position) or (Settings.Ragebot.General.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.Ragebot.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500

            if Settings.Ragebot.General.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and InAir then
                Velocity *= NewVector3(1, Settings.Ragebot.General.AntiGroundShots.Factor, 1)
            end

            if Settings.Ragebot.General.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Ragebot.General.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end

            if Settings.Ragebot.General.JumpOffset.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.Ragebot.Target) then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Ragebot.General.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end

            -- // Spam Resolver (void_spam_resolver pattern)
            if Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.Enabled then
                local hitbox_position = Position
                local current_time = tick()

                local positions_to_remove = {}
                for position, data in defensive_positions do
                    local time_delta = current_time - data["last_update_time"]
                    if time_delta > 0 then
                        local rate = Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.ForgetRate / 20
                        data["weight"] -= ((position - hitbox_position).Magnitude > 200 and time_delta * (rate * 2.5) or time_delta * rate)
                        data["last_update_time"] = current_time
                    end
                    if data["weight"] < 0.1 then
                        positions_to_remove[#positions_to_remove + 1] = position
                    end
                end
                for _, position in positions_to_remove do
                    defensive_positions[position] = nil
                end

                local weight_to_add = (hitbox_position.Magnitude < 9e5 and void_spam_resolver_position_weight or void_spam_resolver_void_weight)
                local done = false
                for position, data in defensive_positions do
                    if (position - hitbox_position).Magnitude <= 200 then
                        local new_position = position:Lerp(hitbox_position, void_spam_resolver_lerp)
                        defensive_positions[new_position] = {
                            ["weight"] = math.clamp(data["weight"] + weight_to_add, -1, 18),
                            ["last_update_time"] = current_time
                        }
                        defensive_positions[position] = nil
                        done = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not done then
                    defensive_positions[hitbox_position] = { ["weight"] = weight_to_add, ["last_update_time"] = current_time }
                end

                local highest = nil
                local highest_weight = 0
                for position, data in defensive_positions do
                    if data["weight"] > highest_weight then
                        highest_weight = data["weight"]
                        highest = position
                    end
                end

                if highest and highest_weight > void_spam_resolver_accuracy then
                    PredictedPosition = highest
                end
            end

            return PredictedPosition
	    end

	    -- Used by MagicBullet & AutoKill to get spam-resolved aim position
	    Ragebot.GetSpamResolverPosition = function(hitbox_position)
	        if not Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.Enabled then return nil end
	        local highest = nil
	        local highest_weight = 0
	        for position, data in defensive_positions do
	            if data["weight"] > highest_weight then
	                highest_weight = data["weight"]
	                highest = position
	            end
	        end
	        if highest and highest_weight > void_spam_resolver_accuracy then
	            return highest
	        end
	        return nil
	    end
	end
	
	-- // Teleport & Network Resolver Runtime
	-- Shared state used by both resolvers (mirrors Table.Ragebot fields)
	local ResolverClosestShot = nil
	local ResolverConnection  = nil

	-- Returns whichever target is active: ragebot OR autokill
	local function GetActiveResolverTarget()
		if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target then
			return Table.Ragebot.Target
		end
		if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled and Settings.Misc.Players.Target then
			return Settings.Misc.Players.Target
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- Per-target resolver heartbeat: runs on RunService.Heartbeat when the
	-- random-teleport resolver OR network resolver is toggled on.
	local function ResolverHeartbeatStep(dt)
		local target = GetActiveResolverTarget()
		if not target then
			Table.Ragebot.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 0, 0)
			return
		end

		local char = target.Character
		local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then
			Table.Ragebot.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 0, 0)
			return
		end

		local refreshTime   = Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.RefreshTime or 0.095
		local elapsed       = tick() - Table.Ragebot.LastRefresh
		local doRefresh     = elapsed > refreshTime

		if doRefresh then
			if not Table.Ragebot.LastPosition then
				Table.Ragebot.LastPosition = hrp.Position
			else
				local offset        = hrp.Position - Table.Ragebot.LastPosition
				local lastPosition  = Table.Ragebot.LastPosition
				Table.Ragebot.LastPosition = hrp.Position

				-- // Random Teleport Resolver
				if Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.RandomTeleport then
					local humanoid      = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
					local moveDir       = humanoid and humanoid.MoveDirection or NewVector3()
					local validOff      = offset.Unit * (Table.Ragebot.LastMoveDirection or moveDir)
					local validOffset   = validOff.Magnitude > 0 and (validOff * offset) or offset
					local highestOffset = Table.Ragebot.HighestOffset
					local mag           = validOffset.Magnitude

					if mag > highestOffset then
						Table.Ragebot.HighestOffset = mag
						highestOffset = mag
					end

					local placeholder = highestOffset * 100
					local loDiv = math.floor(placeholder / 8)
					local hiDiv = math.floor(placeholder)
					if loDiv < 1 then loDiv = 1 end
					if hiDiv < loDiv then hiDiv = loDiv end

					local divisor = math.random(loDiv, hiDiv) / 100
					if divisor == 0 then divisor = 1 end

					local newPos = hrp.Position - (validOffset * (highestOffset ~= 0 and (mag / divisor) or 1))
					Table.Ragebot.ForcePos = newPos
					Table.Ragebot.Velocity = NewVector3()
					Table.Ragebot.LastMoveDirection = moveDir

					task.delay(0.03, function()
						if Table.Ragebot.ForcePos == newPos then
							Table.Ragebot.ForcePos = nil
						end
					end)
				end

				-- // Network Resolver
				if Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver then
					local isNetSleeping = pcall(function()
						return gethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping")
					end) and gethiddenproperty(hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping") or false

					if isNetSleeping then
						Table.Ragebot.LastSleeping = tick()
					end
					if tick() - Table.Ragebot.LastSleeping < 0.3 then
						local oldShot = ResolverClosestShot
						Table.Ragebot.Velocity  = NewVector3()
						Table.Ragebot.ForcePos  = oldShot or nil
						task.delay(0.3, function()
							if Table.Ragebot.ForcePos == oldShot then
								Table.Ragebot.ForcePos = nil
							end
						end)
					end
				end

				-- teleport detection guard
				if (hrp.Position - (lastPosition or NewVector3())).Magnitude >= 9e9 then
					Table.Ragebot.Velocity  = NewVector3()
					Table.Ragebot.ForcePos  = lastPosition
					return
				end

				if not Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver then
					Table.Ragebot.ForcePos = nil
				end

				Table.Ragebot.Velocity = offset / elapsed
			end
			Table.Ragebot.LastRefresh = tick()
		end
	end

	-- Network Resolver: detect enemy bullets near their HRP to track their server position
	local DoNetworkResolver = function(bulletPart)
		local target = GetActiveResolverTarget()
		if not target then return end

		local char = target.Character
		local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp then return end

		local lhrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not lhrp then return end

		-- Estimate server position from bullet spawn offset
		local old = bulletPart.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * NewVector3(-6, 0, -6)

		if (lhrp.Position - old).Magnitude > 250 then return end

		ResolverClosestShot = old

		task.delay(1, function()
			if ResolverClosestShot == old then
				ResolverClosestShot = nil
				Table.Ragebot.ForcePos = nil
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Store for UI toggle callback to reconnect
	Table.DoNetworkResolver = DoNetworkResolver

	-- Watch BulletPath for new bullet parts to feed network resolver
	local function WatchBullets()
		if BulletPath then
			ResolverConnection = BulletPath.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
				if Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver then
					task.spawn(DoNetworkResolver, child)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	WatchBullets()

	-- Heartbeat connection for both random-teleport and network resolver
	Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
		if Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.RandomTeleport or Settings.Ragebot.General.Resolver.NetworkResolver then
			ResolverHeartbeatStep(dt)
		else
			-- Fall back to raw HRP velocity when both are off
			local target = GetActiveResolverTarget()
			local hrp = target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			Table.Ragebot.Velocity = hrp and hrp.Velocity or NewVector3()
		end
	end))

	-- // PredictAutoArmor source
	-- Variables shared by PredictAutoArmor
	local PredictAutoArmorReady = true
	local PredictAutoArmorShopNames = nil  -- populated lazily from workspace shop data
	local PredictAutoArmorArmorNames = {"Level 1", "Level 2", "Level 3", "Level 4", "Level 5"}

	local function RunPredictAutoArmor(dt)
		-- Determine target: prefer ragebot target, fall back to autokill target
		local target = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target)
			or (Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled and Settings.Misc.Players.Target)
			or nil

		if not target or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end

		local lhrp = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not lhrp then return end

		-- Don't run if we have forcefield or haven't fired yet
		if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then return end

		local char = target.Character
		local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp or (hrp.Position - lhrp.Position).Magnitude <= 25.1 then return end

		-- Find RPG in backpack
		local rpg = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
		if not rpg then return end

		-- Check if target is near an armor shop
		local lastPos       = hrp.Position
		local isInPosition  = nil
		local shopArmorData = workspace:FindFirstChild("Shops") -- adjust path as needed

		if shopArmorData then
			for _, shopModel in ipairs(shopArmorData:GetChildren()) do
				local button = shopModel:FindFirstChild("Button") or shopModel:FindFirstChild("button")
				if button then
					if (lastPos - button.Position).Magnitude <= 9 then
						isInPosition = button.Position
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- If target has forcefield, don't aim for armor positions
		local targetBodyEffects = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
		local targetFF = targetBodyEffects and targetBodyEffects:FindFirstChild("ForceField") and targetBodyEffects.ForceField.Value
		if targetFF then
			isInPosition = nil
		end

		-- Control RPG projectiles already in world
		local ignored = workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
		local has_one = false
		if ignored then
			for _, instance in ipairs(ignored:GetChildren()) do
				if instance.Name == "Model" then
					local part = instance:FindFirstChild("Launcher")
					if part then
						-- Check ownership
						local ok, owned = pcall(isnetworkowner, part)
						if ok and owned then
							has_one = true
						end
						-- Remove physics manipulators so we can steer it
						local bv = part:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity")
						if bv then bv:Destroy() end
						local ti = part:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest")
						if ti then ti:Destroy() end
						-- Steer toward armor position or up in the air
						if isInPosition then
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(isInPosition + NewVector3(0, math.random(-185, -150) / 100, 0))
						else
							-- Get predicted aim position from ragebot if available
							local aimPos = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
								or (hrp.Position)
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(aimPos + NewVector3(0, 100, 0))
						end
						part.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 1.85, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		-- Auto-fire RPG when no projectile is owned and target is in position
		if not has_one and PredictAutoArmorReady and not targetFF then
			PredictAutoArmorReady = false
			task.spawn(function()
				local oldTool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
				if oldTool then
					oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					task.wait(2)
				end
				local rpgRef = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
				if rpgRef then
					rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					task.wait(0.03)
					if rpgRef.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then
						rpgRef:Activate()
					end
					task.delay(2.5, function()
						PredictAutoArmorReady = true
					end)
					task.wait(0.03)
					if MainEvent then
						MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", rpgRef)
					end
					if rpgRef and rpgRef.Parent then
						rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					end
					task.wait(0.03)
					if oldTool and oldTool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Backpack then
						oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					end
				else
					PredictAutoArmorReady = true
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	-- Hook into heartbeat: run PredictAutoArmor when enabled
	Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
		if Settings.Misc.Other.PredictAutoArmor.Enabled then
			pcall(RunPredictAutoArmor, dt)
		end
	end))

	-- // Silent Aim
	local SilentAim = {} do
	    SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.SilentAim.Target, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Root" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Head" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and InAir then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Value * 0.01)
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.SilentAim.Target) then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	    
	    local RoundMultiple = function(...) 
            local A = {} 
            for I, V in next, table.pack(...) do 
                A[I] = Round(V) 
            end 
            return unpack(A) 
        end
        
        local WorldToViewport = function(...) 
            local A, B = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(...) 
            return NewVector2(A.X, A.Y), B, A.Z 
        end
        
        SilentAim.BoxFov = function(Player)
            local Character = Player and Player.Character
            if Character then
                local CFrame = Character:GetModelCFrame()
                local Position, Visible, Depth = WorldToViewport(Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
        
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
        
                if Visible then
                    local ScaleFactor = 1 / (Depth * Tan(Rad(Camera.FieldOfView / 2)) * 2) * 1000
                    local Width, Height = RoundMultiple(4 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum, 7 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum)
                    local X, Y = RoundMultiple(Position.X, Position.Y)
        
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size = NewVector2(Width, Height)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position = NewVector2(RoundMultiple(X - Width / 2, Y - Height / 2))
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Color = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Color
        
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position
        
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size - NewVector2(2, 2)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position + NewVector2(1, 1)
                end
            else
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = false
            end
        end
	end
	
	-- // Aim Assist
	local AimAssist = {} do
	    AimAssist.MouseMoveRel = function(Position, Smoothing)
		    local MousePosition = UserInputServive:GetMouseLocation()

	    	mousemoverel((Position.X - MousePosition.X) / Smoothing, (Position.Y - MousePosition.Y) / Smoothing)
	    end
	    
	    AimAssist.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.AimAssist.Target, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Root" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Head" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.AimAssist.Target) then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Amount)
            end
            
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Enabled and InAir then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	end
