        HitDetection.HitEffect = function(Part, WhichEffect, Color)
            local Clone = Table.HitEffect[WhichEffect]:Clone()
            Clone.Parent = Part

            for _, Effect in pairs(Clone:GetChildren()) do
                if ( Effect:IsA('ParticleEmitter') ) then
                    Effect.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color)
                    Effect:Emit()
                end
            end

            task.delay(3, function()
                Clone:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        
        HitDetection.HitMarker2D = function(Color, Time)
        	local Lines = {}
            local LinesOutline = {}
            local Hitmarker = true
        
            for i = 1, 4 do
                Lines[i] = Utility.NewDrawing("Line", { Color = Color, Visible = true, Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1, ZIndex = 9999 })
                LinesOutline[i] = Utility.NewDrawing("Line", { Color = NewRGB(0, 0, 0), Visible = true, Transparency = 1, Thickness = 3 })
            end
        
            local function SetMarkerPos()
                local X, Y = DevicePositionX, DevicePositionY
        
                Lines[1].From = NewVector2(X + 5, Y + 5)
                Lines[1].To = NewVector2(X + (5 * 2.5), Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                Lines[2].From = NewVector2(X + 5, Y - 5)
                Lines[2].To = NewVector2(X + (5 * 2.5), Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                Lines[3].From = NewVector2(X - 5, Y + 5)
                Lines[3].To = NewVector2(X - (5 * 2.5), Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                Lines[4].From = NewVector2(X - 5, Y - 5)
                Lines[4].To = NewVector2(X - (5 * 2.5), Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                LinesOutline[1].From = NewVector2(X + 5, Y + 5)
                LinesOutline[1].To = NewVector2(X + (5 * 2.5), Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                LinesOutline[2].From = NewVector2(X + 5, Y - 5)
                LinesOutline[2].To = NewVector2(X + (5 * 2.5), Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                LinesOutline[3].From = NewVector2(X - 5, Y + 5)
                LinesOutline[3].To = NewVector2(X - (5 * 2.5), Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                LinesOutline[4].From = NewVector2(X - 5, Y - 5)
                LinesOutline[4].To = NewVector2(X - (5 * 2.5), Y - (5 * 2.5))
            end
        
            SetMarkerPos()
        
            delay(Time, function()
                for i = 5, 0, -0.1 do
                    task.wait()
                    for _, v in next, Lines do
                        v.Transparency = i
                    end
                    for _, v in next, LinesOutline do
                        v.Transparency = i
                    end
                end
        
                for _, v in next, Lines do
                    v:Remove()
                end
                for _, v in next, LinesOutline do
                    v:Remove()
                end
        
                Hitmarker = false
            end)
        end
        
        HitDetection.HitMarker3D = function(Color, Time, Hit) 
        	local Lines = {}
            local LinesOutline = {}
            local Hitmarker = true
        
            for i = 1, 4 do
                Lines[i] = Utility.NewDrawing("Line", { Color = Color, Visible = true, Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1, ZIndex = 9999 })
                LinesOutline[i] = Utility.NewDrawing("Line", { Color = NewRGB(0, 0, 0), Visible = true, Transparency = 1, Thickness = 3 })
            end
        
            task.spawn(function()
                while Hitmarker do
                    local Position3D, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Hit)
        
                    Lines[1].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X + 5, Position3D.Y + 5)
                    Lines[1].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X + (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                    Lines[2].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X + 5, Position3D.Y - 5)
                    Lines[2].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X + (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                    Lines[3].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X - 5, Position3D.Y + 5)
                    Lines[3].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X - (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                    Lines[4].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X - 5, Position3D.Y - 5)
                    Lines[4].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X - (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                    LinesOutline[1].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X + 5, Position3D.Y + 5)
                    LinesOutline[1].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X + (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                    LinesOutline[2].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X + 5, Position3D.Y - 5)
                    LinesOutline[2].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X + (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                    LinesOutline[3].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X - 5, Position3D.Y + 5)
                    LinesOutline[3].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X - (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y + (5 * 2.5))
        
                    LinesOutline[4].From = NewVector2(Position3D.X - 5, Position3D.Y - 5)
                    LinesOutline[4].To = NewVector2(Position3D.X - (5 * 2.5), Position3D.Y - (5 * 2.5))
        
                    for _, Value in next, Lines do
                        Value.Visible = OnScreen
                    end
                    for _, Value in next, LinesOutline do
                        Value.Visible = OnScreen
                    end
        
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
        
            delay(Time, function()
                for i = 5, 0, -0.1 do
                    task.wait()
                    for _, Value in next, Lines do
                        Value.Transparency = i
                    end
                    for _, Value in next, LinesOutline do
                        Value.Transparency = i
                    end
                end
        
                for _, Value in next, Lines do
                    Value:Remove()
                end
                for _, Value in next, LinesOutline do
                    Value:Remove()
                end
        
                Hitmarker = false
            end)
        end        
        
        local HitChamsFolder = Instance.new("Folder") HitChamsFolder.Name = "HitChamsFolder" HitChamsFolder.Parent = Workspace
        HitDetection.HitChams = function(Color, Material, Duration, Player)
            if Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                Player.Character.Archivable = true
                local Cloned = Player.Character:Clone()
                Cloned.Name = "Player Clone"
        
                local BodyParts = {
                    "Head", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
                    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
                    "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
                    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
                    "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"
                }
        
                for _, Part in ipairs(Cloned:GetChildren()) do
                    if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local PartValid = false
                        for _, ValidPart in ipairs(BodyParts) do
                            if Part.Name == ValidPart then
                                PartValid = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        
                        if not PartValid then
                            Part:Destroy()
                        end
                    elseif Part:IsA("Accessory") or Part:IsA("Tool") or Part.Name == "face" or Part:IsA("Shirt") or Part:IsA("Pants") or Part:IsA("Hat") then
                        Part:Destroy()
                    end
                end
        
                if Cloned:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                    Cloned.Humanoid:Destroy()
                end
        
                for _, BodyPart in ipairs(Cloned:GetChildren()) do
                    if BodyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                        BodyPart.CanCollide = false
                        BodyPart.Anchored = true
                        BodyPart.Transparency = 0
                        BodyPart.Color = Color
                        BodyPart.Material = Material
                    end
                end
        
                if Cloned:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                    local Head = Cloned.Head
                    Head.Transparency = 0
                    Head.Color = Color
                    Head.Material = Material
        
                    if Head:FindFirstChild("face") then
                        Head.face:Destroy()
                    end
                end
        
                Cloned.Parent = game.Workspace
        
                local SuicideInfo = TweenInfo.new(Duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, 0, true)
        
                for _, BodyPart in ipairs(Cloned:GetChildren()) do
                    if BodyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local Tween = TweenService:Create(BodyPart, SuicideInfo, { Transparency = 1 })
                        Tween:Play()
                    end
                end
        
                task.delay(Duration, function()
                    if Cloned and Cloned.Parent then
                        Cloned:Destroy()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        
        HitDetection.HitSkeleton = function(Color, Time, Player)
            if Player and Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local BodyParts = {
                    {"Head", "UpperTorso"},
                    {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
                    {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
                    {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"},
                    {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
                    {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"},
                    {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
                    {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
                    {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
                    {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"},
                    {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
                    {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"},
                    {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
                    {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"}
                }
                
                local Lines = {}
        
                for _, BonePair in ipairs(BodyParts) do
                    local ParentBone = Player.Character:FindFirstChild(BonePair[1])
                    local ChildBone = Player.Character:FindFirstChild(BonePair[2])
        
                    if ParentBone and ChildBone then
                        local Line = Instance.new("Part")
                        Line.Size = NewVector3(0.02, 0.02, (ParentBone.Position - ChildBone.Position).Magnitude)
                        Line.CFrame = NewCFrame(ParentBone.Position, ChildBone.Position) * NewCFrame(0, 0, -Line.Size.Z / 2)
                        Line.Anchored = true
                        Line.CanCollide = false
                        Line.Transparency = 0
                        Line.Color = Color
                        Line.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        Line.Parent = workspace
        
                        local TweenInfoObject = TweenInfo.new(5 / 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                        local Tween = TweenService:Create(Line, TweenInfoObject, { Transparency = 1 })
                        Tween:Play()
        
                        table.insert(Lines, Line)
                    end
                end
        
                task.delay(Time, function()
                    for _, Line in ipairs(Lines) do
                        if Line and Line.Parent then
                            Line:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        
        HitDetection.DamageNumber = function(Color, Damage, Character)
            local DamageValue = tonumber(Damage)
            if not DamageValue then return end
        
            local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not Head then return end
        
            local Part = Instance.new("Part")
            Part.Size = NewVector3(1, 1, 1)
            Part.Position = Head.Position + NewVector3(0, -2, 0)
            Part.Anchored = true
            Part.CanCollide = false
            Part.Transparency = 1
            Part.Parent = Workspace
        
            local Billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            Billboard.Adornee = Part
            Billboard.Size = UDim2.new(4, 0, 4, 0)
            Billboard.StudsOffset = NewVector3(0, 3, 0)
            Billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        
            local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Billboard)
            TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TextLabel.Text = tostring(DamageValue)
            TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.3, 0)
            -- Custom font support
            local FontName = Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Font or "Cartoon"
            TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font[FontName] or Enum.Font.Cartoon
            TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
            TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color
            TextLabel.TextSize = 0
            TextLabel.TextScaled = false
            
            Billboard.Parent = Part
            
            -- Custom size support
            local MaxSize = Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Size or 40
        
            TweenService:Create(TextLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                TextSize = MaxSize
            }):Play()
        
            task.delay(0.2, function()
                local RotateTween = TweenService:Create(TextLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    Rotation = 60
                })
                RotateTween:Play()
            end)
        
            task.delay(1 - 0.3, function()
                local ShrinkTween = TweenService:Create(TextLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    TextSize = 0
                })
                ShrinkTween:Play()
                Utility.NewConnection(ShrinkTween.Completed, function()
                    Part:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end
	end
	
	-- // Misc
	local Misc = {} do
        Misc.ModDetection = function(State, Method, Rank, Plr)
            if State then
                if (game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Creator.CreatorType == "Group" and true or false) == true then
                    local GetId = game:GetService("GroupService"):GetGroupInfoAsync(game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId).Creator.CreatorTargetId).Id
                    local GroupId = tonumber(GetId)
                    
                    if Plr:IsInGroup(GroupId) and Plr:GetRankInGroup(GroupId) > Rank then
                        if Method == "Kick" then 
                            task.wait(3)
                            LocalPlayer:Kick("Detected Moderator / Admin: " .. tostring(Plr))
                        elseif Method == "Notification" then
                            Library:Notify("Detected Moderator / Admin: " .. tostring(Plr))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.CheckMessage = function(Tbl)
            local Msg = Tbl[Random(1, #Tbl)]
            
            if Msg == OldMsg then
                return Misc.CheckMessage(Tbl)
            else
                OldMsg = Msg
                return Msg
            end
        end
        
        Misc.ChatSpam = function(Type, Repeat, Emojis, Symbols)
            local Message = ""
            
            for i = 1, Repeat and Random(2, 5) or 1 do
                for i = 1, Random(1, 5) do
                    local Index = Random(0, 1) == 0 and "Symbols" or "Emojis"
                    
                    if Index == "Symbols" and Symbols then
                        Message ..= tostring(Table[Index][Random(1, #Table[Index])])
                    elseif Index == "Emojis" and Emojis then
                        Message ..= tostring(Table[Index][Random(1, #Table[Index])])
                    end
                end
                
                Message ..= Misc.CheckMessage(Table.ChatSpam[Type])
                
                for i = 1, Random(1, 5) do
                    local Index = Random(0, 1) == 0 and "Symbols" or "Emojis"
                    
                    if Index == "Symbols" and Symbols then
                        Message ..= tostring(Table[Index][Random(1, #Table[Index])])
                    elseif Index == "Emojis" and Emojis then
                        Message ..= tostring(Table[Index][Random(1, #Table[Index])])
                    end
                end
                
                Message ..= " "
            end
            
            return Message
        end
        
        Misc.EnableShiftlock = function()
            if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = NewCFrame(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position, NewVector3(Camera.CFrame.LookVector.X * 10e10, LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Y, Camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z * 10e10))
            end
        end
        
        Misc.DisableShiftlock = function()
            if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
            end
            
            if Connections.Macro then
                Connections.Macro:Disconnect()
                Connections.Macro = nil
            end
        end
        
        Misc.SaveOriginalAnimations = function(Character)
            local Animate = Character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
            if not Animate then return end
            for _,Child in ipairs(Animate:GetDescendants()) do
                if Child:IsA("Animation") then
                    OriginalAnimations[Child] = Child.AnimationId
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.RestoreOriginalAnimations = function()
            for AnimObj,Id in pairs(OriginalAnimations) do
                if AnimObj and AnimObj.Parent then
                    AnimObj.AnimationId = Id
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.ApplyAnimationPack = function(PackName)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local Animate = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
            if not (Animate and Table.Animations[PackName]) then return end
        
            local Pack = Table.Animations[PackName]
            for Index, Map in ipairs(AnimationOrder) do
                local AnimId = Pack[Index]
                if AnimId then
                    local Section = Animate:FindFirstChild(Map.Path[1])
                    if Section then
                        local AnimObj = Section:FindFirstChild(Map.Path[2])
                        if AnimObj then
                            AnimObj.AnimationId = AnimId
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.PlayEmote = function(Track, EmoteName)
            local Speed = Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.PlaybackSpeed or 1
            local FPS = Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.FramePerSecond or 0
        
            if EmoteCoroutine then
                coroutine.close(EmoteCoroutine)
                EmoteCoroutine = nil
            end
        
            if FPS and FPS > 0 then
                local FrameTime = 1 / FPS
                Track:Play()
                Track:AdjustSpeed(0)
        
                EmoteCoroutine = coroutine.create(function()
                    while CurrentPlaying == EmoteName and Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled do
                        task.wait(FrameTime)
                        pcall(function()
                            Track.TimePosition = Track.TimePosition + (FrameTime * Speed)
                        end)
                    end
                end)
                coroutine.resume(EmoteCoroutine)
            else
                Track:Play()
                Track:AdjustSpeed(Speed)
            end
        end
        
        Misc.LoadEmoteTrack = function(Character, EmoteName)
            local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local EmoteId = Table.Emotes[EmoteName]
        
            if EmoteId and Humanoid then
                local Emote = Instance.new("Animation")
                Emote.AnimationId = EmoteId
                local Track = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Emote)
                Track.Looped = true
                Track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                EmoteTracks[EmoteName] = Track
        
                if Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled and Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.WhichEmote == EmoteName then
                    task.wait(0.6)
                    CurrentPlaying = EmoteName
                    Misc.PlayEmote(Track, EmoteName)
                end
            end
        end
        
        -- Custom Emote Spam System
        local CustomEmoteTrack = nil
        local CustomEmoteLoop = nil
        
        Misc.StartCustomEmoteSpam = function(EmoteID)
            if not LocalPlayer.Character or not LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                return
            end
            
            -- Stop existing custom emote
            if CustomEmoteTrack then
                CustomEmoteTrack:Stop()
                CustomEmoteTrack = nil
            end
            
            if CustomEmoteLoop then
                CustomEmoteLoop:Disconnect()
                CustomEmoteLoop = nil
            end
            
            if EmoteID and EmoteID ~= "" then
                local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid
                
                -- Add rbxassetid:// if not present
                if not string.find(EmoteID, "rbxassetid://") then
                    EmoteID = "rbxassetid://" .. EmoteID
                end
                
                local Emote = Instance.new("Animation")
                Emote.AnimationId = EmoteID
                CustomEmoteTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Emote)
                CustomEmoteTrack.Looped = true
                CustomEmoteTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
                CustomEmoteTrack:Play()
                
                -- Keep restarting the emote
                CustomEmoteLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                    if Settings.Misc.EmoteSpam.Enabled and CustomEmoteTrack and not CustomEmoteTrack.IsPlaying then
                        CustomEmoteTrack:Play()
                    end
                end))
            end
        end
        
        Misc.StopCustomEmoteSpam = function()
            if CustomEmoteTrack then
                CustomEmoteTrack:Stop()
                CustomEmoteTrack:Destroy()
                CustomEmoteTrack = nil
            end
            
            if CustomEmoteLoop then
                CustomEmoteLoop:Disconnect()
                CustomEmoteLoop = nil
            end
        end
        
        -- Hood Customs Godmode Button
        -- Hood Customs Godmode System
        local GodmodeActive = false
        local GodmodeTrack = nil
        local GodmodeHeartbeat = nil
        local GodmodeAnimConn = nil
        local GodmodeEmoteID = "rbxassetid://70883871260184"
        local GodmodeFreezeTime = 0.1265
        
        Misc.StartGodmode = function()
            if GodmodeActive or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
            
            local Humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not Humanoid then return end
            
            -- Cleanup existing
            if GodmodeTrack then GodmodeTrack:Stop() GodmodeTrack:Destroy() GodmodeTrack = nil end
            if GodmodeHeartbeat then GodmodeHeartbeat:Disconnect() GodmodeHeartbeat = nil end
            if GodmodeAnimConn then GodmodeAnimConn:Disconnect() GodmodeAnimConn = nil end
            
            GodmodeActive = true
            
            -- Load and play animation
            local Anim = Instance.new("Animation")
            Anim.AnimationId = GodmodeEmoteID
            GodmodeTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Anim)
            GodmodeTrack:Play(0, 1, 1)
            
            -- Freeze animation at specific time
            GodmodeHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                if GodmodeTrack and GodmodeActive then
                    GodmodeTrack.TimePosition = GodmodeFreezeTime
                    GodmodeTrack:AdjustSpeed(0)
                end
            end))
            
            -- Restart if new animation plays
            GodmodeAnimConn = Humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(newtrack)
                if GodmodeActive and GodmodeTrack and newtrack ~= GodmodeTrack then
                    task.delay(0.02 + math.random() * 0.03, function()
                        if GodmodeActive then
                            Misc.StartGodmode()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
        
        Misc.StopGodmode = function()
            GodmodeActive = false
            if GodmodeTrack then GodmodeTrack:Stop() GodmodeTrack:Destroy() GodmodeTrack = nil end
            if GodmodeHeartbeat then GodmodeHeartbeat:Disconnect() GodmodeHeartbeat = nil end
            if GodmodeAnimConn then GodmodeAnimConn:Disconnect() GodmodeAnimConn = nil end
        end
        
        -- Hood Customs Force Hit System
        Misc.DBForceHit = function(Target)
            if not Target or not Target.Character then return end
            
            local TargetPart = Target.Character.SpecialParts and Target.Character.SpecialParts:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if not TargetPart then return end
            if Target.Character:FindFirstChild('ForceField') then return end
            
            local args = {
                [1] = "Shoot",
                [2] = {
                    [1] = {
                        [1] = {
                            Normal = TargetPart.Position,
                            Instance = TargetPart,
                            Position = TargetPart.Position
                        },
                        [2] = {
                            Normal = TargetPart.Position,
                            Instance = TargetPart,
                            Position = TargetPart.Position
                        },
                        [3] = {
                            Normal = TargetPart.Position,
                            Instance = TargetPart,
                            Position = TargetPart.Position
                        },
                        [4] = {
                            Normal = TargetPart.Position,
                            Instance = TargetPart,
                            Position = TargetPart.Position
                        },
                        [5] = {
                            Normal = TargetPart.Position,
                            Instance = TargetPart,
                            Position = TargetPart.Position
                        }
                    },
                    [2] = {
                        [1] = {
                            thePart = TargetPart,
                            theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        },
                        [2] = {
                            thePart = TargetPart,
                            theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        },
                        [3] = {
                            thePart = TargetPart,
                            theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        },
                        [4] = {
                            thePart = TargetPart,
                            theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        },
                        [5] = {
                            thePart = TargetPart,
                            theOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                        }
                    },
                    [3] = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
                    [4] = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position,
                    [5] = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                }
            }
            
            MainEvent:FireServer(unpack(args))
        end
        
        -- Calculate prefire based on target distance and ping
        Misc.CalculatePrefire = function(Target)
            if not Target or not Target.Character then return 50 end
            
            local LocalHRP = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local TargetHRP = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            if not LocalHRP or not TargetHRP then return 50 end
            
            local Distance = (LocalHRP.Position - TargetHRP.Position).Magnitude
            local Ping = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            
            -- Formula: Base prefire + (Distance factor) + (Ping factor)
            local Prefire = 20 + (Distance / 10) + (Ping / 5)
            return Clamp(Prefire, 10, 200)
        end
        
        Misc.Gun = function(Name)
            for Check = 0, 100000 do
                if Workspace.Ignored.Shop:FindFirstChild("["..Name.."] - $"..Check) then
                    return "["..Name.."] - $"..Check
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.Ammo = function(Name)
            for Check1 = 0, 250 do
                for Check2 = 0, 500 do
                    if Workspace.Ignored.Shop:FindFirstChild(Check1.." ["..Name.." Ammo] - $"..Check2) then
                        return Check1.." ["..Name.." Ammo] - $"..Check2
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        Misc.Purchase = function(Obj, Ammo, Amount)
            local Character = LocalPlayer and (LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait())
            local OldCFrame = Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            local ObjectPath = Workspace.Ignored.Shop[Obj]
            local ClickDet   = ObjectPath.ClickDetector
            
            local EquippedTools = {}
            for _, Tool in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
                if Tool:IsA("Tool") then
                    table.insert(EquippedTools, Tool)
                    Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                end
            end
            
            if Character and Ammo then
                for i = 1, Amount do
                    Table.ForceCFrame = ObjectPath.Head.CFrame
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    fireclickdetector(ClickDet)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
        
                Table.ForceCFrame = OldCFrame
            else
                Table.ForceCFrame = ObjectPath.Head.CFrame
                task.wait(0.15)
                fireclickdetector(ClickDet)
                task.wait(0.15)
                Table.ForceCFrame = OldCFrame
            end
            task.delay(0.03, function()
                Table.ForceCFrame = nil
                task.wait(0.1)
                for _, Tool in ipairs(EquippedTools) do
                    if Tool and Tool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Backpack then
                        Tool.Parent = Character
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        
        Misc.PurchaseGunAndAmmo = function(GunName, Times)
            if LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("["..GunName.."]") or LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("["..GunName.."]") then
                Misc.Purchase(Misc.Ammo(GunName), true, Times)
            else
                Misc.Purchase(Misc.Gun(GunName), true, 1)
            end
        end
        
        Misc.PurchaseArmor = function()
            local Armor = Misc.Gun("High-Medium Armor")
        
            if not Armor then
                local Ignored = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
                local Shop = Ignored and Ignored:FindFirstChild("Shop")
                local BestArmor, BestPrice = nil, -1
        
                if Shop then
                    for _, Item in ipairs(Shop:GetChildren()) do
                        if Item:IsA("Model") and Item.Name:lower():find("armor") then
                            local Price = tonumber(Item.Name:match("%$(%d+)")) or 0
                            if Price > BestPrice then
                                BestArmor, BestPrice = Item, Price
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
        
                Armor = BestArmor
            end
        
            if Armor then
                Misc.Purchase(Armor, true, 1)
            end
        end
        
        Misc.PurchaseFireArmor = function()
            local FireArmor = Misc.Gun("Fire Armor")
            if FireArmor then
                 Misc.Purchase(FireArmor, true, 1)
            end
        end

        -- // Safe Purchasing runtime
        -- Wraps Misc.Purchase: checks for nearby enemies/target before allowing the purchase.
        do
            local _original_purchase = Misc.Purchase

            local function safe_purchasing_check(ObjectPath)
                if not Settings.Misc.Other.SafePurchasing.Enabled then return true end
                if not ObjectPath then return true end

                local avoid_enemy  = false
                local avoid_target = false
                local statuses = Settings.Misc.Other.SafePurchasing.AvoidStatuses or {}
                for _, s in ipairs(statuses) do
                    if s == "enemy"  then avoid_enemy  = true end
                    if s == "target" then avoid_target = true end
                end

                -- Determine shop world position (Head part or model PrimaryPart)
                local shop_pos = nil
                if typeof(ObjectPath) == "Instance" then
                    local head = ObjectPath:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    shop_pos = head and head.Position
                    if not shop_pos and ObjectPath.PrimaryPart then
                        shop_pos = ObjectPath.PrimaryPart.Position
                    end
                end
                if not shop_pos then return true end

                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr == LocalPlayer then continue end
                    if not plr.Character then continue end
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then continue end
                    if (shop_pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude >= 50 then continue end

                    -- Check if player qualifies as a threat under selected statuses
                    local is_ragebot_target = (Table.Ragebot.Target == plr or Settings.Misc.Players.Target == plr)
                    local is_enemy = not DaHood.IsCrew(plr) and not Utility.IsFriendsWith(plr)

                    if (avoid_target and is_ragebot_target) or (avoid_enemy and is_enemy) then
                        Library:Notify("Safe Purchasing: blocked — threat within 50 studs of shop", 3)
                        return false
                    end
                end
                return true
            end

            Misc.Purchase = function(Obj, Ammo, Amount)
                local ObjectPath = (typeof(Obj) == "Instance") and Obj or
                    (Workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored") and Workspace.Ignored:FindFirstChild("Shop") and Workspace.Ignored.Shop:FindFirstChild(tostring(Obj)))
                if not safe_purchasing_check(ObjectPath) then
                    return
                end
                return _original_purchase(Obj, Ammo, Amount)
            end
        end
    end
    
                            
    local hi = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                        hi.Parent = gethui()

                        local ImageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                        ImageLabel.Parent = hi
                        ImageLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32)
                        ImageLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                        ImageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        ImageLabel.Image = "rbxassetid://108457834955769"
                        ImageLabel.Visible = false
    -- // Features
	local Features = {} do
	    Features.HighlightBody = function(State, FillColor, OutlineColor)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if Character then
                local Highlight = Character:FindFirstChild("Head ")
                
                if State then
                    if not Highlight then
                        Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
                        Highlight.Name = "Head "
                        Highlight.Adornee = Character
                        Highlight.Parent = Character
                    end
        
                    Highlight.OutlineColor = OutlineColor
                    Highlight.FillColor = FillColor
                    Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.5
                    Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
                else
                    if Highlight then
                        Highlight:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
	    local OriginalParts = {}
        
        Features.ForcefieldBody = function(State, Color)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
        
            if Character then
                for _, Part in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if Part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                        if not OriginalParts[Part] then
                            OriginalParts[Part] = {Color = Part.Color, Material = Part.Material}
                        end
        
                        if State then
                            Part.Color = Color
                            Part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
                        else
                            Part.Color = OriginalParts[Part].Color
                            Part.Material = OriginalParts[Part].Material
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        Features.ForcefieldHats = function(State, Color)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
        
            if Character then
                for _, Accessory in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if Accessory:IsA("Accessory") then
                        local Handle = Accessory:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                        if Handle then
                            if not OriginalParts[Handle] then
                                OriginalParts[Handle] = {Color = Handle.Color, Material = Handle.Material}
                            end
        
                            if State then
                                Handle.Color = Color
                                Handle.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
                            else
                                Handle.Color = OriginalParts[Handle].Color
                                Handle.Material = OriginalParts[Handle].Material
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end        
        
        Features.Trail = function(State, Color, Lifetime)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local Parts = Character:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, #Parts do
                local Part = Parts[i]
                if Part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if State then
                        for _, Child in ipairs(Part:GetChildren()) do
                            if Child:IsA("Trail") and Child.Name == "Trail_Effect" then
                                Child:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                        
                        local Trail = Instance.new("Trail", Part)
                        local A0 = Instance.new("Attachment", Part)
                        local A1 = Instance.new("Attachment", Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                        A0.Name = "Trail_Attachment0"
                        A1.Name = "Trail_Attachment1"
                        Trail.Name = "Trail_Effect"
                        Trail.Texture = "rbxassetid://1390780157"
                        Trail.Attachment0 = A0
                        Trail.Attachment1 = A1
                        Trail.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color)})
                        Trail.Lifetime = Lifetime
                    else
                        for _, Child in ipairs(Part:GetChildren()) do
                            if Child:IsA("Trail") and Child.Name == "Trail_Effect" then
                                Child:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        		Emitter2.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Top
        		Emitter2.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1)
        		Emitter2.Rate = 7
        		Emitter2.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
        		Emitter2.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-400, 400)
        		Emitter2.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.001)
        		Emitter2.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(5, 5)
        		Emitter2.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
        		Emitter2.LockedToPart = true
        		Emitter2.Parent = HaloAttachment
        		table.insert(Emitters, Emitter2)
        	elseif AuraType == "Angel Wings" then
        		local Attachment1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        		Attachment1.Name = "ParticleAttachment1"
        		Attachment1.CFrame = CFrame.new(2, 0.5, 1) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(), Vector3.new(0.965929627, 0, 0.258804798), Vector3.new(0, 1, 0), Vector3.new(-0.258804798, 0, 0.965929627))
        		Attachment1.Axis = Vector3.new(0.9659296274185181, 0, 0.2588047981262207)
        		Attachment1.SecondaryAxis = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)

        Features.UpdateAura = function(State, Color, Type)
        	if not State then
        		if Table.Aura then
        			for _, Cache in pairs(Table.Aura) do
        				if Cache.Emitters then
        					for _, Emitter in ipairs(Cache.Emitters) do
        						Emitter.Enabled = false
        					end
        				end
        			end
        		end
        		return
        	end
        
        	if not LocalPlayer then return end
        	
        	local Char = LocalPlayer.Character
        	if not Char then
        		if Table.Aura then
        			for _, Cache in pairs(Table.Aura) do
        				if Cache.Attachment then
        					Cache.Attachment:Destroy()
        				end
        				if Cache.Attachments then
        					for _, Att in ipairs(Cache.Attachments) do
        						Att:Destroy()
        					end
        				end
        			end
        			Table.Aura = {}
        		end
        		return
        	end
        	
        	local CurrentTypes = Type
        	if type(CurrentTypes) ~= "table" then
        		CurrentTypes = {CurrentTypes}
        	end
        	
        	if not Table.Aura then
        		Table.Aura = {}
        	end
        	
        	local TypesToRemove = {}
        	for CachedType, _ in pairs(Table.Aura) do
        		local Found = false
        		for _, CurrentType in ipairs(CurrentTypes) do
        			if CachedType == CurrentType then
        				Found = true
        				break
        			end
        		end
        		if not Found then
        			table.insert(TypesToRemove, CachedType)
        		end
        	end
        	
        	for _, TypeToRemove in ipairs(TypesToRemove) do
        		local Cache = Table.Aura[TypeToRemove]
        		if Cache then
        			if Cache.Attachment then
        				Cache.Attachment:Destroy()
        			end
        			if Cache.Attachments then
        				for _, Att in ipairs(Cache.Attachments) do
        					Att:Destroy()
        				end
        			end
        			Table.Aura[TypeToRemove] = nil
        		end
        	end
        	
        	for _, CurrentType in ipairs(CurrentTypes) do
        		local UseUpperTorso = (CurrentType == "Angel Wings")
                local UseHead = (CurrentType == "Halo")
        		local UseHumanoidRootPart = (CurrentType == "freemium.lol" or CurrentType == "Electrical" or CurrentType == "Lightning" or CurrentType == "Swirl" or CurrentType == "Wave" or CurrentType == "Air" or CurrentType == "Bubble")
        
        		local TargetPart = nil
        		if UseHumanoidRootPart then
        			TargetPart = Char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                elseif UseHead then
                    TargetPart = Char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        		elseif UseUpperTorso then
        			TargetPart = Char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        		else
        			TargetPart = Char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or Char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        		end
        		
        		if not TargetPart then
        			if Table.Aura[CurrentType] then
        				local Cache = Table.Aura[CurrentType]
        				if Cache.Attachment then
        					Cache.Attachment:Destroy()
        				end
        				if Cache.Attachments then
        					for _, Att in ipairs(Cache.Attachments) do
        						Att:Destroy()
        					end
        				end
        				Table.Aura[CurrentType] = nil
        			end
        			return
        		end
        		
        		if not Table.Aura[CurrentType] then
        			local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
        			if UseUpperTorso then
        				Attachment.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)
        			end
        			Attachment.Parent = TargetPart
        			
        			local Emitters, ExtraAttachments = Features.CreateAura(CurrentType, Attachment, TargetPart)
        			
        			Table.Aura[CurrentType] = {
        				Attachment = Attachment,
        				Emitters = Emitters,
        				Attachments = ExtraAttachments or {}
        			}
        		else
        			local Cache = Table.Aura[CurrentType]
        			if Cache.Attachment.Parent ~= TargetPart then
        				Cache.Attachment.Parent = TargetPart
        				if Cache.Attachments then
        					for _, Att in ipairs(Cache.Attachments) do
        						Att.Parent = TargetPart
        					end
        				end
        			end
        			
        			if Cache.Emitters then
        				for _, Emitter in ipairs(Cache.Emitters) do
        					Emitter.Enabled = true
                            Emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color)
        				end
        			end
        		end
        	end
        end
        
        Features.CustomCharacter = function()
            if not Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.CustomCharacter.Enabled then return end
        
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not OriginalDescription then
                OriginalDescription = Humanoid.HumanoidDescription:Clone()
            end
            
            if Humanoid and Humanoid.HumanoidDescription and Humanoid.HumanoidDescription.RightArm == 0 then
                for _, Item in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if Item:IsA("Shirt") or Item:IsA("Pants") or Item:IsA("Accessory") then
                        Item:Destroy()
                    end
                end
        
                local function GetCustomHumanoidDescription()
                    local ModelType = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.CustomCharacter.Type
                    if ModelType == "User ID" then
                        local UserId = tonumber(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.CustomCharacter.UserID)
                        if UserId then
                            return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(UserId)
                        end
                    elseif ModelType == "Username" then
                        local Username = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.CustomCharacter.Username
                        if Username and Username ~= "" then
                            local Success, UserId = pcall(function()
                                return Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(Username)
                            end)
                            if Success and UserId then
                                return Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(UserId)
                            end
                        end
                    elseif ModelType == "Preset" then
                        local PresetName = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.CustomCharacter.Preset
                        local UserId = Table.Characters[PresetName]
                        if UserId then
                            return game.Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(UserId)
                        end
                    end
                end
        
                local Description = GetCustomHumanoidDescription()
                if Description then
                    Humanoid:ApplyDescriptionClientServer(Description)
                else
                    Library:Notify("Invalid Custom Character input.")
                end
            else
                Library:Notify("'Custom Character' requires Blocky / Default Right Arm.")
            end
        end
    end

                local LastReturnPos = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame or CFrame.new()

                Instances.TargetTeleportButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local Target = Table.Ragebot.Target
                    if Target and Target.Character and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        LastReturnPos = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame or CFrame.new()
                        if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        end
                    end
                end)

                Instances.TargetReturnButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = LastReturnPos
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

    -- * Connections
    
    Utility.NewConnection(RunService.RenderStepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        if (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.General.AutoSelection.Enabled) and (tick() - Table.PreviousTicks.AutoSelection >= Settings.Ragebot.General.AutoSelection.Cooldown.Amount and Settings.Ragebot.General.AutoSelection.Cooldown.Enabled or true) then
            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Device", Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Radius, Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Flags)
            Table.Ragebot.Target = NewTarget or nil
            Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = NewTarget and true or false
            Table.PreviousTicks.AutoSelection = tick()
        end
        
        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.Enabled then
            Instances.RagebotFov.Visible = true
            Instances.RagebotFov.Size = UDim2.new(0, Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Radius * 1.6, 0, Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Radius * 1.6)
            Instances.RagebotFov.Position = UDim2.new(0, DevicePositionX, 0, DevicePositionY)
            Instances.RagebotFovGradient.Rotation = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.Spin.Enabled and Sin(os.clock() * (Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.Spin.Speed / 200)) * 360 or 90
            Instances.RagebotFovStrokeGradient.Rotation = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.Spin.Enabled and Sin(os.clock() * (Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.Spin.Speed / 200)) * 360 or 90
            
            Instances.RagebotFovGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.From.Color),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.To.Color)
            }
            Instances.RagebotFovGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.4),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.7)
            }   
            Instances.RagebotFovStrokeGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.From.Outline),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.ShowFov.To.Outline)
            }
            Instances.RagebotFovStrokeGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new{
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.5)
            }
        else
            if Instances.RagebotFov.Visible then
                Instances.RagebotFov.Visible = false
            end
        end
        
        if Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Spectate.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and not DaHood.IsKnocked(Table.Ragebot.Target, 10) then
            Camera.CameraSubject = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.Humanoid
        else
            local Hum = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if Hum then Camera.CameraSubject = Hum end
        end
        
        if CrosshairFollow and Table.Ragebot.Target then
            local Nadeko = Utility.WorldToScreen(Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
            Crosshair.mode = "Custom"
            Crosshair.position = Nadeko.Position
        else
            Crosshair.mode = CrosshairPosition
        end
        
        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Enabled and LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local Okazaki = (Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Origin == "Device" and {Position = NewVector2(DevicePositionX, DevicePositionY)} or nil)            
			if Okazaki == nil then 
				Okazaki = Utility.WorldToScreen(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) 
			end
			local Akkio = Utility.WorldToScreen(Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.EndPoint == "Character Position" and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position or Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
			Drawings.RagebotLine.Visible = Akkio.OnScreen
			Drawings.RagebotLine.From = Okazaki.Position
			Drawings.RagebotLine.To = Akkio.Position
			Drawings.RagebotLine.Thickness = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Thickness
			Drawings.RagebotLine.Color = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Rainbow and Utility.GetRainbowColor() or Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Color
			
			Drawings.RagebotLineOutline.Visible = Akkio.OnScreen
			Drawings.RagebotLineOutline.From = Drawings.RagebotLine.From
			Drawings.RagebotLineOutline.To = Drawings.RagebotLine.To
			Drawings.RagebotLineOutline.Color = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Line.Outline
		else
		    if Drawings.RagebotLine.Visible then
		        Drawings.RagebotLine.Visible = false
		        Drawings.RagebotLineOutline.Visible = false
		    end
		end
		
		if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Dot.Enabled then
		    local Hachiman = Utility.WorldToScreen(Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
		    Drawings.RagebotDot.Visible = Hachiman.OnScreen
		    Drawings.RagebotDot.Position = Hachiman.Position
		    Drawings.RagebotDot.Color = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Dot.Color
		    Drawings.RagebotDot.Radius = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Dot.Size
		    Drawings.RagebotDot.Filled = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Dot.Filled
		else
		    if Drawings.RagebotDot.Visible then
		        Drawings.RagebotDot.Visible = false
		    end
		end
		
		if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Highlight.Enabled then
		    Instances.RagebotHighlight.Parent = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character
		    Instances.RagebotHighlight.FillColor = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Highlight.Rainbow and Utility.GetRainbowColor() or Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Highlight.Color
		    Instances.RagebotHighlight.OutlineColor = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Highlight.Outline
		else
		    Instances.RagebotHighlight.Parent = nil
		end
		
		if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.BoundingBox.Enabled then
            Instances.RagebotBoundingBox.Adornee = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character
            
            if Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.BoundingBox.Filled then
                local Orientation, Boundary = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:GetBoundingBox()
                
                Instances.RagebotBoundingBoxFill.Material = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.BoundingBox.Material
                Instances.RagebotBoundingBoxFill.Size = Boundary 
                Instances.RagebotBoundingBoxFill.CFrame = Orientation
            else
                Instances.RagebotBoundingBoxFill.CFrame = NewCFrame(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            end
        else
            Instances.RagebotBoundingBox.Adornee = nil
            Instances.RagebotBoundingBoxFill.CFrame = NewCFrame(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        end
		
        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Circle.Enabled then
            local RootPart = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if RootPart then
                if Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Circle.Scan then
                    local Time = tick() * 2
                    local ScanY = Sin(Time) * 2
                    local ScanPosition = RootPart.Position + NewVector3(0, ScanY, 0)
                    Instances.RagebotCircle.CFrame = NewCFrame(ScanPosition)
                else
                    Instances.RagebotCircle.CFrame = RootPart.CFrame
                end
                Instances.RagebotCircle.Color = Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.Circle.Color
                Instances.RagebotCircle.Size = NewVector3(2,2,2)
            end
        else
            Instances.RagebotCircle.CFrame = NewCFrame(9e9,9e9,9e9)
        end
        
        -- Update Target Info UI (adapted from target_hud.txt)
        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and Table.Ragebot.Target and Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.TargetInfo.Enabled then
            local Target = Table.Ragebot.Target
            if Target and Target.Character and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                local Humanoid = Target.Character.Humanoid
                local BodyEffects = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                local ArmorV = BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Armor") or nil
                local WeaponV = Target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

                local LocalHRP = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local TargetHRP = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local DistanceMag = (LocalHRP and TargetHRP) and (TargetHRP.Position - LocalHRP.Position).Magnitude or 0

                local HealthRatio = math.clamp(Humanoid.Health / Humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
                local ArmorRatio = ArmorV and math.clamp(ArmorV.Value / 130, 0, 1) or 0

                -- Name label
                if Target.Name ~= Target.DisplayName then
                    local full = Target.DisplayName .. " (@" .. Target.Name .. ")"
                    Instances.TargetUsername.Text = #full > 30 and string.sub(full, 1, 17) .. "..." or full
                else
                    Instances.TargetUsername.Text = #Target.Name > 30 and string.sub(Target.Name, 1, 17) .. "..." or Target.Name
                end

                -- Distance
                Instances.TargetDistance.Text = math.floor(DistanceMag) .. " studs"

                -- Tool
                if WeaponV then
                    Instances.TargetWeapon.Text = "tool: " .. WeaponV.Name
                elseif Instances.TargetWeapon.Text ~= "tool: none" then
                    Instances.TargetWeapon.Text = "tool: none"
                end

                -- Health bar (cached tween to prevent lag from spawning tweens every frame)
                local CurrHealthRatio = math.floor(HealthRatio * 100)
                if CurrHealthRatio ~= Instances._LastHealthRatio then
                    Instances._LastHealthRatio = CurrHealthRatio
                    if Instances._CachedHealthTween then Instances._CachedHealthTween:Cancel() end
                    Instances._CachedHealthTween = TweenService:Create(Instances.TargetHealthBar, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                        Size = UDim2.new(HealthRatio, 0, 1, 0)
                    })
                    Instances._CachedHealthTween:Play()
                end
                Instances.TargetHealthText.Text = math.floor(Humanoid.Health) .. "/100"

                -- Armor bar
                if ArmorV then
                    local CurrArmorRatio = math.floor(ArmorRatio * 100)
                    if CurrArmorRatio ~= Instances._LastArmorRatio then
                        Instances._LastArmorRatio = CurrArmorRatio
                        if Instances._CachedArmorTween then Instances._CachedArmorTween:Cancel() end
                        Instances._CachedArmorTween = TweenService:Create(Instances.TargetArmorBar, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                            Size = UDim2.new(ArmorRatio, 0, 1, 0)
                        })
                        Instances._CachedArmorTween:Play()
                    end
                    Instances.TargetArmorText.Text = ArmorV.Value .. "/130"
                end

                -- Avatar (only update when target changes to avoid spam)
                local UserId = Target.UserId
                if UserId and Instances._LastUserId ~= UserId then
                    Instances._LastUserId = UserId

        if Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Enabled then
            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Device", Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.Radius, Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Checks.Flags)

            local Hrp = Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character and Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local Tool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        
            if Hrp and Tool then
                local ToolName = Tool.Name
                local ValidTool = ToolName ~= "[Knife]" and ToolName ~= "Wallet" and ToolName ~= "Ragebot Tool" and ToolName ~= "Aim Assist Tool"
        
                if ValidTool then
                    local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.Triggerbot.Target, Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
                    local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Root" and Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Head" and Table.Triggerbot.Target.Character.Head)
                    local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
                    
                    if Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Prediction.Custom then
                        PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Prediction.Amount
                    else
                        PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
                    end
                    
                    local ScreenPosition, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(PredictedPosition)
                    
                    if OnScreen then
                        local MousePositionX = DevicePositionX
                        local MousePositionY = DevicePositionY
                        local Distance = (NewVector2(DevicePositionX, DevicePositionY) - NewVector2(ScreenPosition.X, ScreenPosition.Y)).magnitude
        
                        if Distance <= Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Range then
                            if Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Delay.Enabled then
                                task.delay(Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.Delay.Amount, function()
                                    if IsMobile then
                                        Tool:Activate()
                                    else
                                        mouse1click()
                                    end
                                end)
                            else
                                if IsMobile then
                                    Tool:Activate()
                                else

                                end
                            end
                        end

    local TimeElapsed = 0
    local FPS = 0
    local NeckC0
    Utility.NewConnection(RunService.RenderStepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(DeltaTime)
        local Character = LocalPlayer and (LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait())
        local Gun = Utility.GetTool()
        
        if Gun and Gun:FindFirstChild("Handle") and Settings.Visuals.Game.CustomShootSound.Enabled then
            local HasAmmo = false
            for _,v in pairs(Gun:GetChildren()) do
                if string.lower(v.Name) == "ammo" then
                    HasAmmo = true
                    break
                end
            end
            
            if HasAmmo then
                for _, Value in pairs(Gun.Handle:GetChildren()) do 
                    if Value:IsA("Sound") and Value.Name ~= "NoAmmo" and Gun.Handle ~= nil then 
                        Value.SoundId = Table.Sounds[Settings.Visuals.Game.CustomShootSound.Value]
                        Value.Volume = Settings.Visuals.Game.CustomShootSound.Volume
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        TimeElapsed = TimeElapsed + DeltaTime
        FPS = 1 / DeltaTime
        
        if TimeElapsed >= Settings.Player.Movement.Macro.Delay then
            
            if Settings.Player.Movement.Macro.Enabled and MacroKeybind then
                if not Table.Connections.Macro then
                    Table.Connections.Macro = Connection(RunService.RenderStepped, function()
                        Misc.EnableShiftlock()
                    end)
                elseif Table.Connections.Macro then
                    Misc.DisableShiftlock()
                end
            end
            
            TimeElapsed = 0
        end
        
        pcall(function()
            if Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.Enabled ~= true then return end
            local Character = Game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
            if Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid").RootPart then
                local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.AutoWalkSpeed then
                    Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.WalkSpeed = Humanoid.WalkSpeed
                end
                local Root = Humanoid.RootPart
                Root.Velocity = NewVector3(Humanoid.MoveDirection.X , Root.Velocity.Y , Humanoid.MoveDirection.Z)
                Root.CFrame = Root.CFrame + ((Humanoid.MoveDirection * DeltaTime) * (Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.WalkSpeed * 0.9))
                for _, Animation in next , Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do
                    local AnimationParent = nil
                    local CurrentAnimation = Animation
                    if Animation and Animation.Animation and Animation.Animation.Parent then
                        AnimationParent = Animation.Animation.Parent
                    end
                    if AnimationParent and AnimationParent.Parent and AnimationParent.Parent.Name == "Animate" and 
                        (string.find(CurrentAnimation.Animation.Name:lower() , "run") or string.find(CurrentAnimation.Animation.Name:lower() , "walk")) then
                        CurrentAnimation:AdjustSpeed(Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.WalkSpeed / Settings.Player.Character.AnimationBreaker.AnimationSpeed)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        
        local Neck
        if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
            if LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                Neck = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and LocalPlayer.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("Neck") or LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") and LocalPlayer.Character.UpperTorso:FindFirstChild("Neck")
            else
                Neck = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") and LocalPlayer.Character.Torso:FindFirstChild("Neck")
            end
        end
        
        if Neck and not NeckC0 then
            NeckC0 = Neck.C0
        end
        
        if Settings.Player.Character.ClientSidePitch.Enabled and Neck and NeckC0 then
            Neck.C0 = NeckC0 * Angle(Rad(Settings.Player.Character.ClientSidePitch.Angle), 0, 0)
        elseif Neck and NeckC0 then
            Neck.C0 = NeckC0
        end
        
        Features.UpdateAura(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ParticleAura.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ParticleAura.Color, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ParticleAura.Type)
    end))
    
    local PingValue = nil
    local Split = nil
    local Ping = nil
    local PredictionValue = nil
    local BasePredictionTable = {
        {Ping = 130, Value = 0.51},
        {Ping = 125, Value = 0.49},
        {Ping = 110, Value = 0.46},
        {Ping = 105, Value = 0.38},
        {Ping = 90,  Value = 0.36},
        {Ping = 80,  Value = 0.34},
        {Ping = 70,  Value = 0.31},
        {Ping = 60,  Value = 0.229},
        {Ping = 50,  Value = 0.225},
        {Ping = 40,  Value = 0.256}
    }
    
    Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Stepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
		if Character then
			if Settings.Player.Movement.Noclip then
				local Children = Character:GetChildren()
				for i = 1, #Children do
					local Child = Children[i]
					local ClassName = Child.ClassName
					if ClassName == "Part" or ClassName == "MeshPart" or ClassName == "BasePart" then
						Child.CanCollide = false
					end 
				end 
			end
		end
		
		if Settings.Player.Movement.PreventFling then
            for _, Player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if Player ~= LocalPlayer and Player.Character then
                    local Descendants = Player.Character:GetDescendants()
                    for i = 1, #Descendants do
                        local Descendant = Descendants[i]
                        if Descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                            Descendant.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        PingValue = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString()
        Split = string.split(PingValue, '(')
        Ping = tonumber(Split[1])
    
        for _, Data in ipairs(BasePredictionTable) do
            if Ping < Data.Ping then
                PredictionValue = Data.Value * Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Prediction
                break
            end
        end
    end))
    
    Utility.NewConnection(UserInputService.WindowFocusReleased, function()
        IsTabbedOut = true
    end)

    Utility.NewConnection(UserInputService.WindowFocused, function()
        IsTabbedOut = false
    end)
    
    Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(DeltaTime)
        local Hrp, Hum = Utility.IsCharacterLoaded()
        local ReloadDelay = 0.5
        local LastReload = 0
        local FollowTargetActive = false
        local AutoKillReload = true
        Table.Ping = tonumber(string.split(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString(),'(')[1])
        
        CenterPanelText = "Unsafe"
        
        -- // Quick Check
        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Enabled then
            local Origin = Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            
            if Origin and (Table.Ragebot.Target and Utility.IsAlive(Table.Ragebot.Target)) then
                if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Resolver.SpawnProtection then
                    if Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then
                        return
                    end
                end
                
                FollowTargetActive = true
            end
        end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled or FollowTargetActive then
            AbleToVoid = false
        else
            AbleToVoid = true
        end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoFire and tick() - Table.PreviousTicks.AutoShoot >= 0.1 then
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.MultipleWeapons then
                for _, Tool in next, LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren() do
                    if Tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        Tool:Activate()
                    end
                end
            else
                local Tool = Utility.GetTool()
                if Tool then
                    Tool:Activate()
                end
            end
            
            Table.PreviousTicks.AutoShoot = tick()
        end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoReload then
    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not Character then return end

    if tick() - LastReload < ReloadDelay then return end

    for _, Tool in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
        if Tool:IsA("Tool") then

            local ToolScript

            if GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment" then
                ToolScript = Tool:FindFirstChild("Script")
            else
                ToolScript = Tool
            end

            if ToolScript and ToolScript:FindFirstChild("Ammo") then
                local Ammo = ToolScript.Ammo.Value

                -- reload when empty OR very low (prevents dry firing)
                if Ammo <= 1 then
                    LastReload = tick()
                    AutoKillReload = false

                    MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", Tool)
                    Utility.SetCenterPanelText("Reloading")
                    
                    -- Faster reload with reduced delay
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Other.AntiBag then
            LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Christmas_Sock"):Destroy()
        end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoDropCash.Enabled then
            MainEvent:FireServer("DropMoney", Settings.Misc.Other.AutoDropCash.Amount)
        end
        
        if Settings.Visuals.View.NoFlashbang then
			if LocalPlayer.PlayerGui then
				local MainScreenGui = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
				if MainScreenGui then
					local Flashbang = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("whiteScreen")
					if Flashbang then Flashbang:Destroy() end
				end 
			end
		end            
		
		if Settings.Player.Movement.NoSlowdown then
	    	if LocalPlayer.Character.BodyEffects.Reload.Value then
				LocalPlayer.Character.BodyEffects.Reload.Value = false
			end
			
			local Slowdowns = LocalPlayer.Character.BodyEffects.Movement:FindFirstChild('NoJumping') or LocalPlayer.Character.BodyEffects.Movement:FindFirstChild('NoWalkSpeed') or LocalPlayer.Character.BodyEffects.Movement:FindFirstChild('ReduceWalk')
			if Slowdowns then
				Slowdowns:Destroy()
			end
        end
        
        local Bars = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        Bars = Bars and Bars:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
        Bars = Bars and Bars:FindFirstChild("Bar")
        
        if Bars then
            local Energy = Bars:FindFirstChild("Energy")
            local Armor = Bars:FindFirstChild("Armor")
            local HP = Bars:FindFirstChild("HP")
        
            if Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Enabled then
                if Energy and Energy:FindFirstChild("bar") then Energy.TextLabel.Text = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Text.EnergyBar Energy.bar.BackgroundColor3 = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Color.EnergyBar end
                if Armor and Armor:FindFirstChild("bar") then Armor.TextLabel.Text = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Text.ArmorBar Armor.bar.BackgroundColor3 = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Color.ArmorBar end
                if HP and HP:FindFirstChild("bar") then HP.TextLabel.Text = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Text.HealthBar HP.bar.BackgroundColor3 = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Color.HealthBar end
                if Armor and Armor:FindFirstChild("firebar") then Armor.firebar.BackgroundColor3 = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomStatBars.Color.FireArmorBar end
            else
                if Energy and Energy:FindFirstChild("bar") then Energy.TextLabel.Text = "Energy              " Energy.bar.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(182,182,9) end
