                if Armor and Armor:FindFirstChild("bar") then Armor.TextLabel.Text = "                   Armor" Armor.bar.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(0,136,194) end
                if HP and HP:FindFirstChild("bar") then HP.TextLabel.Text = " Health " HP.bar.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(36,182,3) end
                if Armor and Armor:FindFirstChild("firebar") then Armor.firebar.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(253,121,33) end
            end
        end
			
        if Settings.Visuals.Hud.SpinningCrosshair.Enabled then
            local MainScreen = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
            if MainScreen then
                local Crosshair = MainScreen:FindFirstChild("Aim")
                if Crosshair then
                    local Rotation = Crosshair.Rotation
                    if Rotation == 360 then
                        Table.Rotation = tick()
                    end
            
                    local ElapsedTime = tick() - Table.Rotation
                    local TweenValue = TweenService:GetValue((ElapsedTime / ((130 - Settings.Visuals.Hud.SpinningCrosshair.Speed) / 100)), Enum.EasingStyle[Settings.Visuals.Hud.SpinningCrosshair.Style], Enum.EasingDirection.In)
            
                    local Mode = Settings.Visuals.Hud.SpinningCrosshair.Direction or "Right"
                    local BaseRotation = TweenValue * 360
            
                    if Mode == "Left" then
                        BaseRotation = -BaseRotation
                    elseif Mode == "Pulse" then
                        BaseRotation = Sin(tick() * 2) * 180
                    elseif Mode == "Flip" then
                        BaseRotation = (Floor(tick() * 2) % 2 == 0) and 0 or 180
                    elseif Mode == "Random" then
                        local RandomDirection = (Random(0, 1) == 1) and 1 or -1
                        BaseRotation = BaseRotation * RandomDirection
                    elseif Mode == "Jitter" then
                        BaseRotation = Random(-15, 15)
                    end
            
                    Crosshair.Rotation = BaseRotation
                end
            end
        end
        
        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        local WalkSpeed = Humanoid.WalkSpeed
        local JumpPower = Humanoid.JumpPower
        
        if Settings.Player.Movement.WalkSpeed.Enabled then
            Humanoid.WalkSpeed = Settings.Player.Movement.WalkSpeed.Speed
    
            if not Table.Connections.WalkSpeed then
                Table.Connections.WalkSpeed = Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
                    if Settings.Player.Movement.WalkSpeed.Enabled then
                        Humanoid.WalkSpeed = Settings.Player.Movement.WalkSpeed.Speed
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            Humanoid.WalkSpeed = WalkSpeed
    
            if Table.Connections.WalkSpeed then
                Table.Connections.WalkSpeed:Disconnect()
                Table.Connections.WalkSpeed = nil
            end
        end
        
        if Settings.Player.Movement.JumpPower.Enabled then
            Humanoid.JumpPower = Settings.Player.Movement.JumpPower.Power
    
            if not Table.Connections.JumpPower then
                Table.Connections.JumpPower = Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("JumpPower"):Connect(function()
                    if Settings.Player.Movement.JumpPower.Enabled then
                        Humanoid.JumpPower = Settings.Player.Movement.JumpPower.Power
                    end
                end)
            end
        else
            Humanoid.JumpPower = JumpPower
    
            if Table.Connections.JumpPower then
                Table.Connections.JumpPower:Disconnect()
                Table.Connections.JumpPower = nil
            end
        end
        
        if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled and Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Indicator then
            local target = Settings.Misc.Players.Target
            if target and target.Character then
                local head = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = Utility.WorldToScreen(head.Position + NewVector3(0, 2.5, 0))
                    if onScreen then
                        local txt = tostring(IndicatorText or "Waiting..")
                        Drawings.IndicatorText.Visible  = true
                        Drawings.IndicatorText.Text     = txt
                        Drawings.IndicatorText.Position = NewVector2(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y - 24)
                        -- Colour by state
                        if txt:find("Shooting") or txt:find("Killing") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(255, 80, 80)
                        elseif txt:find("Resolv") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(255, 200, 0)
                        elseif txt:find("Stomp") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(255, 140, 0)
                        elseif txt:find("Void") or txt:find("void") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(160, 0, 255)
                        elseif txt:find("Reload") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(0, 200, 255)
                        elseif txt:find("Spawn") or txt:find("spawn") or txt:find("Protection") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(100, 255, 100)
                        elseif txt:find("dead") or txt:find("Dead") then
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(180, 180, 180)
                        else
                            Drawings.IndicatorText.Color = NewRGB(255, 255, 255)
                        end
                    else
                        Drawings.IndicatorText.Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    Drawings.IndicatorText.Visible = false
                end
            else
                Drawings.IndicatorText.Visible = false
            end
        else
            Drawings.IndicatorText.Visible = false
        end
        
        if Hrp and Hum then
            if Settings.Player.AntiAim.NetworkDesync.Enabled then
                local NetworkDesyncTickInterval = (Settings.Player.AntiAim.NetworkDesync.Amount / Pi) / 10000
                local MoveDirection = Hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0
            
                if (tick() - Table.PreviousTicks.NetworkDesync) >= NetworkDesyncTickInterval or (Settings.Player.AntiAim.NetworkDesync.WalkingCheck and MoveDirection) then
                    Table.NetworkShouldSleep = not Table.NetworkShouldSleep
                    Table.PreviousTicks.NetworkDesync = tick()
                    sethiddenproperty(Hrp, "NetworkIsSleeping", Table.NetworkShouldSleep)
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Utilities.CashAura then
                local DropFolder = Workspace.Ignored:FindFirstChild("Drop")
                if DropFolder then
                    for _, Cash in ipairs(DropFolder:GetChildren()) do
                        if Cash.Name == "MoneyDrop" and (Cash.Position - Hrp.Position).Magnitude < 12 then
                            local ClickDetector = Cash:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")
                            if ClickDetector then
                                fireclickdetector(ClickDetector)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end            
            
            local IsJitter = Settings.Player.Character.Jitter.Enabled
            local IsSpinbot = Settings.Player.Character.Spinbot.Enabled
            local IsFaceBackwards = Settings.Player.Character.FaceBackwards
            Hum.AutoRotate = not (IsJitter or IsSpinbot or IsFaceBackwards)
            
            if IsJitter then
                local Direction = Random(1, 2) == 1 and Settings.Player.Character.Jitter.Speed or -Settings.Player.Character.Jitter.Speed
                Hrp.CFrame = NewCFrame(Hrp.Position) * Angle(0, Rad(Direction), 0)
            elseif IsSpinbot then
                Hrp.CFrame = Hrp.CFrame * Angle(0, Rad(Settings.Player.Character.Spinbot.Speed), 0)
            elseif IsFaceBackwards then
                local LookDirection = NewVector3(Camera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, Camera.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
                Hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Hrp.Position, Hrp.Position - LookDirection)
            end
            
            if Settings.Player.Movement.CFrameFly.Enabled then
                Hrp.Velocity = NewVector3()
                local X, Y, Z = 0,0,0
    
                if not Typing then
                    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
                        local MoveVector = Hum.MoveDirection
                        local RelativeMove = Camera.CFrame:VectorToObjectSpace(MoveVector)
                        X, Z = RelativeMove.X, RelativeMove.Z
                        Y = MoveVector.Y
                    else
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then z -= 1 end
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then z += 1 end
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then x += 1 end
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then x -= 1 end
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then y += 1 end
                        if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then y -= 1 end
                    end
                end
    
                local Direction = CustomMath.SafeUnit(Camera.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(NewVector3(X, Y, Z)).Unit)
                Hrp.CFrame += Direction * (Settings.Player.Movement.CFrameFly.Speed / 10) * (DeltaTime * 60)
            end
            
            if Settings.Player.Movement.CFrameSpeed.Enabled then
                Hrp.CFrame = Hrp.CFrame + ((DeltaTime * Hum.MoveDirection) * Settings.Player.Movement.CFrameSpeed.Speed * 2.5)
            end
            
            local OldVelocity = Hrp.Velocity
            local IsVelocitySpoofing = false
            local IsVelocityDesyncing = false
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoHeal.Enabled then
                if Hum.Health < Settings.Misc.Other.AutoHeal.Health then
                    for i, v in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                        if v:FindFirstChild("Eat") or v:FindFirstChild("Drink") then
                            v:Activate()
                        else
                            Table.Equipable = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                if Table.Equipable then
                    pcall(function()
                        for i, v in pairs(LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                            if v:FindFirstChild("Eat") or v:FindFirstChild("Drink") then
                                Hum:EquipTool(v)
                                Table.Equipable = false
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AntiRPG then
                local PlayerPosition = Hrp.Position
                for _, Model in pairs(game.Workspace.Ignored:GetChildren()) do
                    if Model:IsA("Model") and Model.Name == "GrenadeLauncherAmmo" or Model.Name == "Handle" or Model:FindFirstChild("Launcher") then
                        local Launcher = Model:FindFirstChild("Launcher") or Model:FindFirstChild("Main") or Model
                        if Launcher and (Launcher.Position - PlayerPosition).Magnitude <= 30 then
                            local Offset = Vector3.new(Random(30, 50), 0, Random(30, 50))
                            Hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(PlayerPosition + Offset)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AntiStomp.Enabled and DaHood.IsKnocked(LocalPlayer, Settings.Misc.Other.AntiStomp.Health) then
                Hum:ChangeState("Dead")
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AbuseSpawn.Enabled and not LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then
                Hum:ChangeState("Dead")
                task.wait(Settings.Misc.Other.AbuseSpawn.ExtraTime)
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoLoadout.Enabled and Table.ForceCFrame == nil then
                local LoadoutGuns = Settings.Misc.Other.AutoLoadout.Guns


                if type(LoadoutGuns) == "string" then
                    LoadoutGuns = {LoadoutGuns}
                elseif type(LoadoutGuns) == "table" then

                    local gunArray = {}
                    for gunName, isSelected in pairs(LoadoutGuns) do
                        if isSelected == true then
                            table.insert(gunArray, gunName)
                        end
                    end
                    LoadoutGuns = gunArray
                end

                if LoadoutGuns and type(LoadoutGuns) == "table" and #LoadoutGuns > 0 then
                    for _, LoadoutGun in ipairs(LoadoutGuns) do
                        if not LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("["..LoadoutGun.."]")
                        and not (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("["..LoadoutGun.."]")
                        ) then
                            local GunShopName = Misc.Gun(LoadoutGun)

                            if GunShopName then
                                Misc.Purchase(GunShopName, false, 1)
                                task.wait(0.5)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoAmmo.Enabled and Table.ForceCFrame == nil then
                for _, Tool in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if Tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local GunName = Tool.Name
                        local CleanGunName = GunName:match("%[(.+)%]")
                        
                        if CleanGunName then
                            local Inventory = LocalPlayer.DataFolder.Inventory
                            local AmmoData = Inventory:FindFirstChild(GunName)
                            
                            if AmmoData then
                                local CurrentAmmo = tonumber(AmmoData.Value) or 0
                                
                                if CurrentAmmo <= 0 then
                                    Misc.Purchase(Misc.Ammo(CleanGunName), true, Settings.Misc.Other.AutoAmmo.Amount)
                                    task.wait(0.1) -- Faster ammo purchase
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoArmor.Enabled then
			    local BodyEffects = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
			    local Armor = BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Armor") and BodyEffects["Armor"].Value
			    
			    if Armor / 130 < Settings.Misc.Other.AutoArmor.Threshold / 100 and Table.ForceCFrame == nil then
			        Misc.PurchaseArmor()
		        	task.wait(0.1) -- Faster armor purchase
			    end
			end
			
			if Settings.Misc.Other.AutoFireArmor.Enabled then
			    local BodyEffects = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
			    local FireArmor = BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("FireArmor") and BodyEffects["FireArmor"].Value
			    
			    if FireArmor / 200 < Settings.Misc.Other.AutoFireArmor.Threshold / 100 and Table.ForceCFrame == nil then
			        Misc.PurchaseFireArmor()
			    end
			end
			
            if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Enabled and AbleToVoid then
                local VoidHideActive = false
                
                if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Activation == "Always" then
                    VoidHideActive = true
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Activation == "Not Full Health" and Hum.Health < Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Health then
                    VoidHideActive = true
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Activation == "Tabbed Out" and IsTabbedOut then
                    VoidHideActive = true
                end
                     
                if VoidHideActive then
                    local OldCFrame = Hrp.CFrame
                    local PredictedCFrame
                    
                    if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Spam then
                        if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Type == "Meta" then
                            PredictedCFrame = Table.ShouldHaalfiDestroy and (Hrp.CFrame * NewCFrame(9e9, 0/0, math.huge)) or Hrp.CFrame
                        else
                            PredictedCFrame = Table.ShouldHaalfiDestroy and (Hrp.CFrame + CustomMath.RandomVector3(80000)) or Hrp.CFrame
                        end
                    else
                        if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Type == "Meta" then
                            PredictedCFrame = Hrp.CFrame * NewCFrame(9e9, 0/0, math.huge)
                        else
                            PredictedCFrame = Hrp.CFrame + CustomMath.RandomVector3(80000)
                        end
                    end
                    
                    Utility.SetCenterPanelText("Void Hiding")
                    Hrp.CFrame = PredictedCFrame
                    
                    if Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Enabled then
                        Instances.TrueDepressedLoner:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(PredictedCFrame)
                        Instances.Yabadoo.FillColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                        Instances.Yabadoo.FillTransparency = 0
                        Instances.Yabadoo.OutlineColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                        Instances.Yabadoo.OutlineTransparency = 0
                    else
                        Instances.TrueDepressedLoner:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                        Instances.Yabadoo.FillTransparency = 1
                        Instances.Yabadoo.OutlineTransparency = 1
                    end
                    
                    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("VoidHideRestore")
                    
                    RunService:BindToRenderStep("VoidHideRestore", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                        Hrp.CFrame = OldCFrame
                        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("VoidHideRestore")
                    end)
                else
                    Instances.TrueDepressedLoner:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                end
            else
                Instances.TrueDepressedLoner:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
            end
            
            if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Enabled then
                local Origin = Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                
                if Origin and (Table.Ragebot.Target and Utility.IsAlive(Table.Ragebot.Target)) then
                    if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Resolver.SpawnProtection then
                        if Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    
                    local OldCFrame = Hrp.CFrame
                    local PredictedCFrame = nil
                    local CurrentPosition = Origin.CFrame.Position
                    local LastPosition = PreviousPositions[Table.Ragebot.Target] or CurrentPosition
                    local EstimatedVelocity = (CurrentPosition - LastPosition) / DeltaTime
                    local Prediction = PredictionValue or 0
                    local Alpha = 0.5
                    
                    CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] = (CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] or Vector3.zero) * Alpha + EstimatedVelocity * (1 - Alpha)
                    PreviousPositions[Table.Ragebot.Target] = CurrentPosition
                    
                    local IsTargetKnocked = Settings.Ragebot.Other.AutoStomping.Enabled and DaHood.IsKnocked(Table.Ragebot.Target, Settings.Ragebot.Other.AutoStomping.Health)
                    
                    if IsTargetKnocked then
                        local StompPosition = CFrame.new(Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.UpperTorso.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                        Hrp.CFrame = StompPosition
                        
                        if Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Enabled then
                            Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(StompPosition)
                            Instances.Dabadoo.FillColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                            Instances.Dabadoo.FillTransparency = 0
                            Instances.Dabadoo.OutlineColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                            Instances.Dabadoo.OutlineTransparency = 0
                        else
                            Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                        end
                        
                        Utility.SetCenterPanelText("Stomping")
                        MainEvent:FireServer("Stomp")
                        
                        if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Desync then
                            RunService:BindToRenderStep("FollowTargetRestore", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                Hrp.CFrame = OldCFrame
                                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FollowTargetRestore")
                            end)
                        end
                    elseif not IsTargetKnocked then
                        if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Type == "Strafe" and Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Strafe.Enabled then
                            local Speed = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Strafe.Speed
                            local Distance = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Strafe.Distance
                            local Height = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Strafe.Height
                            local Angle = tick() * Speed
                            local Offset = NewVector3(
                                Cos(Angle) * Distance,
                                Height,
                                Sin(Angle) * Distance
                            )
                            local PredictedPosition = CurrentPosition + (CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] * Prediction) + Offset
                            PredictedCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(PredictedPosition, CurrentPosition)
                        elseif Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Type == "Spiral" and Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Spiral.Enabled then
                            local Speed = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Spiral.Speed
                            local Radius = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Spiral.Radius
                            local Height = Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Spiral.Height
                            local Time = tick() * Speed
                            -- Spiral motion: circular movement with rising/falling height
                            local Offset = NewVector3(
                                Cos(Time) * Radius,
                                Height + (Sin(Time * 0.5) * (Height * 0.5)), -- Oscillating height
                                Sin(Time) * Radius
                            )
                            local PredictedPosition = CurrentPosition + (CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] * Prediction) + Offset
                            PredictedCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(PredictedPosition, CurrentPosition)
                        elseif Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Type == "Random" then
                            local PredictedPosition = CurrentPosition + (CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] * Prediction) + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Randomization)
                            PredictedCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(PredictedPosition, CurrentPosition)
                        elseif Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Type == "Static" then
                            PredictedCFrame = Origin.CFrame + NewVector3(Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Static.X, Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Static.Y, Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Static.Z)
                        end
                        
                        Utility.SetCenterPanelText("Following")
                        Hrp.CFrame = PredictedCFrame

                        if Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Enabled then
                            Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(PredictedCFrame)
                            Instances.Dabadoo.FillColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                            Instances.Dabadoo.FillTransparency = 0
                            Instances.Dabadoo.OutlineColor = Settings.Visuals.Hud.ServerPositionIndicator.Color
                            Instances.Dabadoo.OutlineTransparency = 0

                            local ScreenPos, OnScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(PredictedCFrame.Position)
                            --ImageLabel.Visible = OnScreen
                            --ImageLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(ScreenPos.X, ScreenPos.Y)
                        else
                            Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                            --ImageLabel.Visible = false
                        end
                        
                        if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.DaHood.MagicBullet and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                            local PredictedAimingPosition = CurrentPosition + (CustomVelocities[Table.Ragebot.Target] * Prediction)
                            -- Spam Resolver override
                            if Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.Enabled then
                                local spamPos = Ragebot.GetSpamResolverPosition(PredictedAimingPosition)
                                if spamPos then PredictedAimingPosition = spamPos end
                            end
                            local TargetHead = Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.Head
                            
                            local Tools = {}
                            for _, Child in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                                if Child:IsA("Tool") and Child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
                                    table.insert(Tools, Child)
                                end
                            end
                            
                            if #Tools > 0 then
                                for _, Tool in ipairs(Tools) do
                                    if not Table.Ragebot.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") or Tool.Name == "[Rifle]" then
                                        local Handle = Tool.Handle
                                        local Args = {
                                            [1] = "ShootGun",
                                            [2] = Handle,
                                            [3] = Handle.Position,
                                            [4] = PredictedAimingPosition,
                                            [5] = TargetHead,
                                            [6] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                        }
                                        MainEvent:FireServer(unpack(Args))
                                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled then
                                            if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Type == "Beam" then
                                                Utility.CreateBeam(Handle.Position, PredictedAimingPosition, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Texture, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                                            else
                                                Utility.CreateDrawingBeam(Handle.Position, PredictedAimingPosition, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                                            end
                                            if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Enabled then
                                                Utility.CreateImpact(Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Size, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Duration, PredictedAimingPosition)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        
                        if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Desync then
                            RunService:BindToRenderStep("FollowTargetRestore", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                Hrp.CFrame = OldCFrame
                                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("FollowTargetRestore")
                            end)
                        end
                    else
                        Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                    end
                else
                    Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
                end
            else
                Instances.FollowTargetIndicator:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(NewCFrame(9999, 9999, 9999))
            end
            
            if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target then
                local TargetPlayer = Table.Ragebot.Target
                if TargetPlayer and TargetPlayer.Character and TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and not DaHood.IsKnocked(TargetPlayer, 10) then
                    local Tool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                    if Tool and TargetPlayer.Character and (not TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) then
            
                        local Distance = (TargetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        local Tool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            
                        if Tool then
                            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.MagicBullet then
                                if GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment" then
                                    local TargetPart = TargetPlayer.Character.SpecialParts and TargetPlayer.Character.SpecialParts:FindFirstChild("Head") or TargetPlayer.Character.Head
                                    local ForceField = TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField")
                            
                                    if TargetPart and not ForceField then
                                        local Args = {
                                            [1] = "Shoot",
                                            [2] = {
                                                [1] = {
                                                    [1] = {Normal = TargetPart.Position, Instance = TargetPart, Position = TargetPart.Position},
                                                    [2] = {Normal = TargetPart.Position, Instance = TargetPart, Position = TargetPart.Position},
                                                    [3] = {Normal = TargetPart.Position, Instance = TargetPart, Position = TargetPart.Position},
                                                    [4] = {Normal = TargetPart.Position, Instance = TargetPart, Position = TargetPart.Position},
                                                    [5] = {Normal = TargetPart.Position, Instance = TargetPart, Position = TargetPart.Position}
                                                },
                                                [2] = {
                                                    [1] = {thePart = TargetPart, theOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)},
                                                    [2] = {thePart = TargetPart, theOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)},
                                                    [3] = {thePart = TargetPart, theOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)},
                                                    [4] = {thePart = TargetPart, theOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)},
                                                    [5] = {thePart = TargetPart, theOffset = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0)}
                                                },
                                                [3] = Hrp.Position,
                                                [4] = Hrp.Position,
                                                [5] = workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                                            }
                                        }
                                        MainEvent:FireServer(unpack(Args))
                                    end
                                elseif Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.DaHood.MagicBullet then
                                    if Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Enabled == false then
                                        local TargetHead = TargetPlayer.Character.Head
                                        local AimPosition = TargetHead.Position
                                        -- Spam Resolver override
                                        if Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.Enabled then
                                            local spamPos = Ragebot.GetSpamResolverPosition(AimPosition)
                                            if spamPos then AimPosition = spamPos end
                                        end
                                        
                                        local Tools = {}
                                        for _, Child in pairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                                            if Child:IsA("Tool") and Child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
                                                table.insert(Tools, Child)
                                            end
                                        end
                                        
                                        if #Tools > 0 then
                                            for _, ToolInstance in ipairs(Tools) do
                                                if not TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") or ToolInstance.Name == "[Rifle]" then
                                                    local Handle = ToolInstance.Handle
                                                    local Args = {
                                                        [1] = "ShootGun",
                                                        [2] = Handle,
                                                        [3] = Handle.Position,
                                                        [4] = AimPosition,
                                                        [5] = TargetHead,
                                                        [6] = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                                    }
                                                    MainEvent:FireServer(unpack(Args))
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif DaHood.IsKnocked(TargetPlayer, Settings.Ragebot.Other.AutoStomping.Health) and Settings.Ragebot.Other.AutoStomping.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.Other.FollowTarget.Enabled == false then
                    SavedPosition = Hrp.CFrame
            
                    Hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(TargetPlayer.Character.UpperTorso.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                    
                    Utility.SetCenterPanelText("Stomping")
                    MainEvent:FireServer("Stomp")
                
                    RunService:BindToRenderStep("AutoStompingRestore", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                        Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("AutoStompingRestore")
                    end)
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled then
                if Settings.Misc.Players.Target ~= nil then
                    local Target = Settings.Misc.Players.Target
                
                    if Target and Target.Character and LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local Tools = {}
                        for _, Child in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
                            if Child:IsA("Tool") then
                                table.insert(Tools, Child)
                            end
                        end
                        
                        local TargetHRP  = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local TargetHead = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        local SavedPosition = Hrp.CFrame
                        
                        if #Tools > 0 and TargetHRP and TargetHead then
                            local Knocked = false
                            local BodyEffects = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                            if BodyEffects then
                                local KO = BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
                                Knocked = KO and KO.Value == true
                            end

                            -- Detect void hiding
                            local inVoid = (math.abs(TargetHRP.Position.X) + math.abs(TargetHRP.Position.Z)) >= 8955

                            -- Detect if target is reloading
                            local targetReloading = false
                            if BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("Reload") then
                                targetReloading = BodyEffects.Reload.Value
                            end
            
                            if not Knocked then
                                local HasForceField = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") ~= nil
            
                                if not HasForceField then
                                    -- Determine aim position with resolver
                                    local AimPos = TargetHead.Position
                                    local usingResolver = false

                                    if getgenv().ue_resolver_enabled then
                                        local resolved = AdvancedResolver.GetResolvedPosition(Target)
                                        if resolved then
                                            -- Offset resolved HRP position up to head height
                                            local headOffset = (TargetHead.Position - TargetHRP.Position).Y
                                            AimPos = resolved + NewVector3(0, headOffset, 0)
                                            usingResolver = true
                                        end
                                    end

                                    -- Spam Resolver override for AutoKill
                                    if Settings.Ragebot.General.SpamResolver.Enabled then
                                        local spamPos = Ragebot.GetSpamResolverPosition(TargetHead.Position)
                                        if spamPos then
                                            AimPos = spamPos
                                            usingResolver = true
                                        end
                                    end

                                    -- Set indicator text
                                    if inVoid then
                                        IndicatorText = "Void Hiding - Resolving.."
                                    elseif targetReloading then
                                        IndicatorText = "Target Reloading.."
                                    elseif usingResolver then
                                        IndicatorText = "Shooting (Resolved)"
                                    else
                                        IndicatorText = "Killing Target.."
                                    end

                                    for _, Tool in ipairs(Tools) do
                                        local Handle = Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                        if Handle then
                                            Hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(AimPos + NewVector3(Random(-15, 15), Random(-15, 15), Random(-15, 15)), AimPos)
                                            MainEvent:FireServer("ShootGun", Handle, Handle.Position, AimPos, TargetHead, NewVector3(0, 1, 0))
                                            if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled then
                                                if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Type == "Beam" then
                                                    Utility.CreateBeam(Handle.Position, AimPos, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Texture, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                                                else
                                                    Utility.CreateDrawingBeam(Handle.Position, AimPos, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                                                end
                                                if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Enabled then
                                                    Utility.CreateImpact(Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Size, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Duration, AimPos)
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                                        RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                            Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                                            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                                        end)
                                    end
                                else
                                    AutoKillReload = true
                                    Hrp.CFrame += NewVector3(Random(-80000, 80000), Random(0, 80000), Random(-80000, 80000))
                                    IndicatorText = "Spawn Protection - Waiting.."
                                    if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                                        RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                            Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                                            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                local IsDead = BodyEffects and BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("SDeath") and BodyEffects.SDeath.Value
                                if IsDead == false then
                                    if Target.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                                        Hrp.CFrame = NewCFrame(Target.Character.UpperTorso.Position + NewVector3(0,3,0))
                                        MainEvent:FireServer("Stomp")
                                        IndicatorText = "Stomping Target.."
                                        if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                                            RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                                Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                                                RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                                            end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                elseif IsDead == true then
                                    AutoKillReload = true
                                    Hrp.CFrame += NewVector3(Random(-80000, 80000), Random(0, 80000), Random(-80000, 80000))
                                    IndicatorText = "Target Dead - Waiting Respawn.."
                                    if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                                        RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                            Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                                            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                                        end)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            Hrp.CFrame += NewVector3(Random(-80000, 80000), Random(0, 80000), Random(-80000, 80000))
                            IndicatorText = "Equip Gun for Auto Kill!"
                            if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                                RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                                    Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                                    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                else
                    local SavedPosition = Hrp.CFrame 
                    Hrp.CFrame = NewCFrame(0, 500, 0)
                    IndicatorText = "No Target Selected."
                    if Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync then
                        RunService:BindToRenderStep("RestoreCFrame", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value - 1, function()
                            Hrp.CFrame = SavedPosition
                            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("RestoreCFrame")
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            
            if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Enabled then
                if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Type == "Underground" then
                    Hrp.Velocity = Hrp.Velocity + NewVector3(0, -Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Yaw, 0)
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Type == "Sky" then
                    Hrp.Velocity = Hrp.Velocity + NewVector3(0, Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Yaw, 0)
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Type == "Multiplier" then
                    Hrp.Velocity = Hrp.Velocity + NewVector3(Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Yaw, Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Pitch, Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Roll)
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Type == "Custom" then
                    Hrp.Velocity = Hrp.Velocity + NewVector3(Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Yaw, Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Pitch, Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Roll)
                elseif Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocitySpoofer.Type == "Prediction Breaker" then
                    Hrp.Velocity = Hrp.Velocity + NewVector3(0, 0, 0)
                end
                
                IsVelocitySpoofing = true
            end
            
			if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocityDesync.Enabled then
			    local Amount = Settings.Player.AntiAim.VelocityDesync.Amount * 1000
			    Hrp.Velocity = CustomMath.RandomVector3(Amount)
			    
			    IsVelocityDesyncing = true
			end
			
			if Table.Ragebot.Target and Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition() then
			    if Settings.Ragebot.Visualization.LookAt then
			        Hrp.CFrame = NewCFrame(Hrp.Position, NewVector3(Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition().X, Hrp.Position.Y, Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition().Z))
				end
			end
			
			if Table.ForceCFrame then
				Hrp.CFrame = Table.ForceCFrame
				Hrp.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 1.1, 0)
			end
			
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
			
			if IsVelocityDesyncing or IsVelocitySpoofing then
			    Hrp.Velocity = OldVelocity
			end
		end
		
		Crosshair.indicator = CenterPanelText
    end))
    
    -- Hood Customs Force Hit Connection
    if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") then
        Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.ForceHit.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting then
                -- Apply prefire delay if enabled
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.ForceHit.Prefire.Enabled then
                    local PrefireMS = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.ForceHit.Prefire.MS
                    
                    -- Auto calculate if enabled
                    if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.ForceHit.Prefire.AutoCalculate then
                        PrefireMS = Misc.CalculatePrefire(Table.Ragebot.Target)
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(PrefireMS / 1000) -- Convert MS to seconds
                end
                
                Misc.DBForceHit(Table.Ragebot.Target)
            end
        end))
    end
    
    if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment") then
        Utility.NewConnection(RunService.RenderStepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            local Tool = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.DaHood.RapidFire and Tool and Tool:FindFirstChild("GunScript") then 
                for _, v in ipairs(getconnections(Tool.Activated)) do
                    local funcinfo = debug.getinfo(v.Function)
                    for i = 1, funcinfo.nups do
                        local c, n = debug.getupvalue(v.Function, i)
                        if type(c) == "number" then 
                            debug.setupvalue(v.Function, i, 0.00000000000000000001)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
        
        local OldMathRandom
        OldMathRandom = hookfunction(Random, function(...)
            local Args = {...}
        
            if checkcaller() then
                return OldMathRandom(...)
            end
        
            if (#Args == 0) or 
               (Args[1] == -0.05 and Args[2] == 0.05) or 
               (Args[1] == -0.1) or
               (Args[1] == -0.05) then
        
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.DaHood.SpreadModifier.Enabled then
                    local Spread = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.DaHood.SpreadModifier.Amount
                    return OldMathRandom(...) * (Spread / 100)
                else
                    return OldMathRandom(...)
                end
            end
            
            return OldMathRandom(...)
        end)
    elseif (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") then
        Utility.NewConnection(RunService.RenderStepped, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.Anchored and Table.TargetAim.Target then
                if Table.TargetAim.Target.Character and Table.TargetAim.Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    Table.TargetAim.Target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * NewCFrame(0, 0, -6.5)
                end
            end
        end))
        
        local function BoostGun(Gun)
            if Gun and Gun:FindFirstChild('GunData') then
                local Module = require(Gun:FindFirstChild('GunData'))
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.RapidFire then
                    Module['slowdown_time'] = 0.0000000001
                    Module['cooldown'] = 0.0000000001
                    Module['is_automatic'] = true
                    if Module['fire_rate'] then 
                        Module['fire_rate'] = 1000000 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.SpreadModifier.Enabled then
                    if Module['spread'] then 
                        Module['spread'] = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.SpreadModifier.Amount 
                    end
                    if Module['max_spread'] then 
                        Module['max_spread'] = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.SpreadModifier.Amount 
                    end
                    if Module['bullet_spread'] then 
                        Module['bullet_spread'] = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.SpreadModifier.Amount 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.InfiniteRange then
                    if Module['range'] then 
                        Module['range'] = math.huge 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.InfiniteClip then
                    if Module['clip_size'] then 
                        Module['clip_size'] = 1e6 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.InstantReload then
                    if Module['reload_time'] then 
                        Module['reload_time'] = 0 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.AmmoModifier.Enabled then
                    if Module['ammo'] then 
                        Module['ammo'] = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.AmmoModifier.Ammo 
                    end
                    if Module['max_ammo'] then 
                        Module['max_ammo'] = Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.AmmoModifier.MaxAmmo 
                    end
                end
                
                if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.InstantHit then
                    if Module['accuracy'] then 
                        Module['accuracy'] = math.huge 
                    end
                end
                
                CurrentGun = Gun
            end
        end
        
        local function OnCharacterAdded(Char)
            BoostGun(Char:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool'))
            
            Char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(Child)
                if Child:IsA('Tool') then
                    BoostGun(Child)
                end
            end)
            
            -- Restart custom emote spam on respawn
            task.wait(1) -- Wait for character to fully load
            if Settings.Misc.EmoteSpam.Enabled and Settings.Misc.EmoteSpam.EmoteID ~= "" then
                Misc.StartCustomEmoteSpam(Settings.Misc.EmoteSpam.EmoteID)
            end
            
            -- Restart godmode on respawn (Hood Customs only)
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.HoodCustom.Godmode and GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment" then
                Misc.StartGodmode()
            end
        end
        
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            OnCharacterAdded(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        
        LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        if MainChannel then
            while task.wait(Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Delay) do
                if Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Enabled then
                    MainChannel:SendAsync(Misc.ChatSpam(Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Type, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Repeat, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Emojis, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ChatSpam.Symbols))
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            if Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Spam then
                Table.ShouldHaalfiDestroy = true
                task.wait(Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.In)
                
                Table.ShouldHaalfiDestroy = false
                task.wait(Settings.Player.AntiAim.VoidHide.Out)
            else
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end
    end)
    
    if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment") then
        local ChildAdded = Workspace.Ignored.Siren.Radius.ChildAdded:Connect(function(Object)
            if Object.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then
                local OwnerCharacterName = Object:GetAttribute("OwnerCharacter")
                if OwnerCharacterName and OwnerCharacterName == LocalPlayer.Name then
                    local StartPos = Object.CFrame.Position
                    local LookVector = Object.CFrame.LookVector
    
                    local RaycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
                    RaycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                    RaycastParams.IgnoreWater = true
                    RaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {LocalPlayer.Character}
    
                    local Ray = workspace:Raycast(StartPos, LookVector * 1000, RaycastParams)
                    if Ray then
                        local EndPos = Ray.Position
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled then
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Type == "Beam" then
                            Utility.CreateBeam(StartPos, EndPos, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Texture, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                        else
                            Utility.CreateDrawingBeam(StartPos, EndPos, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration)
                        end
                    end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Enabled then
                            Utility.CreateImpact(Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Size, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Duration, EndPos)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    
        RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled then
                for _,Child in pairs(workspace.Ignored.Siren.Radius:GetChildren()) do
                    if Child.Name == "BULLET_RAYS" then Child:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end)
    else
        local function SirenAdded(Obj)
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local RootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    
            local function VerifyBullet(Obj)
                return (Obj.Name == BulletName or Obj:FindFirstChild("Attachment") or Obj:FindFirstChild(BulletBeamName)) and Obj
            end
    
            local PlayerChecks = {PlayerGun = false}
            local BulletRay = VerifyBullet(Obj)
    
            if BulletRay and RootPart then
                local Mag = (RootPart.Position - BulletRay.Position).Magnitude
                if Mag <= 13 then PlayerChecks.PlayerGun = true end
    
                if PlayerChecks.PlayerGun then
                    local GunBeam = BulletRay:WaitForChild(BulletBeamName,1)
                    local Attachment1 = GunBeam and GunBeam.Attachment1
    
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled and Attachment1 then
                        GunBeam:Destroy()
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Type == "Beam" then
                            Utility.CreateBeam(BulletRay.Position, Attachment1.WorldCFrame.Position, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Texture, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Material)
                        else
                            Utility.CreateDrawingBeam(BulletRay.Position, Attachment1.WorldCFrame.Position, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletTracers.Neon)
                        end
                    end
    
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Enabled and Attachment1 then
                        Utility.CreateImpact(Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Size, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Duration, Settings.Visuals.Game.BulletImpacts.Duration, Attachment1.WorldPosition)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        if BulletPath then BulletPath.DescendantAdded:Connect(SirenAdded) end
    end
    
    function SetupHitMarker(Plr)
        Plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(Char)
            local OldHealth = Char:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Health
            local Connection
            Connection = Char:WaitForChild("Humanoid").HealthChanged:Connect(function(Health)
                if Health < OldHealth then 
                    local HealthFactor = Floor(OldHealth) - Floor(Health)
                    if Plr == Table.Ragebot.Target or Plr == Table.AimAssist.Target or Plr == Table.SilentAim.Target then
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitNotification.Enabled then
                            local CustomText = Settings.Visuals.Game.HitNotification.CustomText or "{damage} damage inflicted on {player} {health}/{maxhealth}"
                            local NotificationText = CustomText
                                :gsub("{damage}", tostring(Floor(OldHealth) - Floor(Health)))
                                :gsub("{player}", Char.Humanoid.DisplayName)
                                :gsub("{health}", tostring(Floor(Health)))
                                :gsub("{maxhealth}", tostring(Floor(Char.Humanoid.MaxHealth)))
                            Library:Notify(NotificationText)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.HitSkeleton(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Duration, Plr)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.HitEffect(Plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.WhichEffect, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.Color)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.HitMarker2D(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Duration)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.HitMarker3D(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Duration, Plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.HitChams(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Material, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Duration, Plr)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Enabled then
                            HitDetection.DamageNumber(Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Color, HealthFactor, Plr.Character)
                        end
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Enabled then
                            Utility.PlaySound(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Volume, Table.Sounds[Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Value])
                        end
                        -- Hit Screen Effect - Stacks with each hit on Ragebot target
                        if Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Plr == Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting then
                            HitScreenIntensity = Clamp(HitScreenIntensity + 0.15, 0, 1) -- Stack 15% per hit, max 100%
                            Utility.CreateHitEffect(Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Color, HitScreenIntensity, Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Duration)
                            
                            -- Reset intensity after 2 seconds of no hits
                            task.delay(2, function()
                                HitScreenIntensity = Clamp(HitScreenIntensity - 0.15, 0, 1)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                OldHealth = Health
                if Health == 0 then 
                    -- Reset hit intensity on death
                    HitScreenIntensity = 0
                    Connection:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
    
    for I, Plr in pairs(Players:GetChildren()) do 
        local Char = Plr.Character or Plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local Hum = Char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        local OldHealth = Hum.Health
        local Connection
        
        Connection = Char:WaitForChild("Humanoid").HealthChanged:Connect(function(Health)
            if Health < OldHealth then 
                HealthFactor = Floor(OldHealth) - Floor(Health)
                if Plr == Table.Ragebot.Target or Plr == Table.AimAssist.Target or Plr == Table.SilentAim.Target then
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitNotification.Enabled then
                        local CustomText = Settings.Visuals.Game.HitNotification.CustomText or "{damage} damage inflicted on {player} {health}/{maxhealth}"
                        local NotificationText = CustomText
                            :gsub("{damage}", tostring(Floor(OldHealth) - Floor(Health)))
                            :gsub("{player}", Char.Humanoid.DisplayName)
                            :gsub("{health}", tostring(Floor(Health)))
                            :gsub("{maxhealth}", tostring(Floor(Char.Humanoid.MaxHealth)))
                        Library:Notify(NotificationText)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.HitSkeleton(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSkeleton.Duration, Plr)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.HitEffect(Plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.WhichEffect, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitEffect.Color)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.HitMarker2D(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker2D.Duration)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.HitMarker3D(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitMarker3D.Duration, Plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.HitChams(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Color, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Material, Settings.Visuals.Game.HitChams.Duration, Plr)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Enabled then
                        HitDetection.DamageNumber(Settings.Visuals.Game.DamageNumber.Color, HealthFactor, Plr.Character)
                    end
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Enabled then
                        Utility.PlaySound(Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Volume, Table.Sounds[Settings.Visuals.Game.HitSound.Value])
                    end
                    -- Hit Screen Effect - Stacks with each hit on Ragebot target
                    if Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Enabled and Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Plr == Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting then
                        HitScreenIntensity = Clamp(HitScreenIntensity + 0.15, 0, 1) -- Stack 15% per hit, max 100%
                        Utility.CreateHitEffect(Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Color, HitScreenIntensity, Settings.Visuals.Game.KillScreenEffect.Duration)
                        
                        -- Reset intensity after 2 seconds of no hits
                        task.delay(2, function()
                            HitScreenIntensity = Clamp(HitScreenIntensity - 0.15, 0, 1)
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            OldHealth = Health
            if Health == 0 then 
                -- Reset hit intensity on death
                HitScreenIntensity = 0
                Connection:Disconnect()
            end
        end)
    end
    
    function BulletTeleport(Character)
        local Equipped, EquippedTool, OldPos
    
        function ToolActivated()
            local EquippedTool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Tool')
            OldPos = EquippedTool.Grip
            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.BulletTeleport and Table.Ragebot.Target ~= nil and Table.Ragebot.Target.Character then
                EquippedTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand.Anchored = false
    
                EquippedTool.Grip = CustomMath.CFrameToOffset(LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand.CFrame, Table.Ragebot.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
    
                LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand.Anchored = true
                EquippedTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
    
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
    
                EquippedTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                LocalPlayer.Character.RightHand.Anchored = false
    
                EquippedTool.Grip = OldPos
                EquippedTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
            end
        end
    
        function ChildAdded(Tool)
            if Tool:IsA('Tool') and Tool.Name ~= 'Ragebot Tool' and Tool.Name ~= 'Aim Assist Tool' and Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.BulletTeleport then
                EquippedTool = Tool
                Equipped = Utility.NewConnection(Tool.Activated, function()
                    ToolActivated()
                end)
            end
        end
    
        function ChildRemoved(Tool)
            if Tool:IsA('Tool') then
                EquippedTool = nil
                if Equipped then
                    Equipped:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
    
        Utility.NewConnection(Character.ChildAdded, function(Tool)
            ChildAdded(Tool)
        end)
    
        Utility.NewConnection(Character.ChildRemoved, function(Tool)
            ChildRemoved(Tool)
        end)
    end
    
    pcall(BulletTeleport, LocalPlayer.Character)

    -- // Knife Connection
    do
        local KnifeConn_LocalChar = nil
        local KnifeConn_LocalRoot = nil

        local function KnifeConn_SetupChar(char)
            KnifeConn_LocalChar = char
            KnifeConn_LocalRoot = char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or nil
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then KnifeConn_SetupChar(LocalPlayer.Character) end
        LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(KnifeConn_SetupChar)

        local function KnifeConn_SetNetOwner(part)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function() part:SetNetworkOwner(LocalPlayer) end)
            end
        end

        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
            if not Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.KnifeConnection then return end
            if not KnifeConn_LocalChar or not KnifeConn_LocalRoot then return end

            local target = Table.Ragebot.Target
            if not target or not target.Character then return end
            local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not targetRoot then return end

            KnifeConn_SetNetOwner(KnifeConn_LocalRoot)
            KnifeConn_SetNetOwner(targetRoot)

            pcall(function()
                sethiddenproperty(KnifeConn_LocalRoot, "PhysicsRepRootPart", targetRoot)
                KnifeConn_LocalRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
                KnifeConn_LocalRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                KnifeConn_LocalRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end))
    end

    local SetupTool = function(Object)
        if not Object:IsA("Tool") then return end
    
        if not Table.Connections.Gun[Object] then
            Table.Connections.Gun[Object] = {}
        end
    
        if not Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalParts then
            Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalParts = {}
            for _, Part in ipairs(Object:GetChildren()) do
                if Part:IsA("BasePart") or Part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalParts[Part] = {
                        Material = Part.Material,
                        Color = Part.Color,
                        TextureID = Part:IsA("MeshPart") and Part.TextureID or nil
                    }
                end
            end
        end
    
        for Part, Original in pairs(Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalParts) do
            if Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.MaterialTools.Enabled then
                Part.Material = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.MaterialTools.Material
                Part.Color = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.MaterialTools.Color
                if Part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    Part.TextureID = ""
                end
            else
                Part.Material = Original.Material
                Part.Color = Original.Color
                if Part:IsA("MeshPart") and Original.TextureID then
                    Part.TextureID = Original.TextureID
                end
            end
        end
    
        pcall(function()
            for _, Conn in ipairs(getconnections(Object:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Grip"))) do
                Conn:Disable()
            end
        end)
    
        if Table.Connections.Gun[Object].Activated then
            Table.Connections.Gun[Object].Activated:Disconnect()
        end
        Table.Connections.Gun[Object].Activated = Utility.NewConnection(Object.Activated, function()
            Combat.CalculateAimViewerEndPoint()
        end)
    
        if Table.Connections.Gun[Object].ToolRotation then
            Table.Connections.Gun[Object].ToolRotation:Disconnect()
            Table.Connections.Gun[Object].ToolRotation = nil
        end
    
        if Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.AnimatedTools.Enabled then
            if not Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalGrip then
                Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalGrip = Object.Grip
            end
    
            local OriginalGrip = Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalGrip
            local Angle = 0
            local Tool = Object
    
            Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(DeltaTime)
                if not Tool or not Tool.Parent then
                    if Table.Connections.Gun[Tool] and Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation then
                        Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation:Disconnect()
                        Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation = nil
                    end
                    return
                end
    
                if not Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.AnimatedTools.Enabled then
                    Tool.Grip = OriginalGrip
                    if Table.Connections.Gun[Tool] and Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation then
                        Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation:Disconnect()
                        Table.Connections.Gun[Tool].ToolRotation = nil
                    end
                    return
                end
    
                local RotationSpeed = math.rad(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.AnimatedTools.Speed or 90)
                Angle = Angle + (RotationSpeed * DeltaTime)
    
                local Axes = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.AnimatedTools.Axes or {"Y"}
                local XRotation, YRotation, ZRotation = 0, 0, 0
