                for _, Axis in ipairs(Axes) do
                    if Axis == "X" then XRotation = Angle
                    elseif Axis == "Y" then YRotation = Angle
                    elseif Axis == "Z" then ZRotation = Angle
                    end
                end
    
                Tool.Grip = OriginalGrip * CFrame.Angles(XRotation, YRotation, ZRotation)
            end))
        end
    
        if not Table.Gun.CurrentTools then
            Table.Gun.CurrentTools = {}
        end
        Table.Gun.CurrentTools[Object] = true
        Table.Gun.CurrentTool = Object
    end
    
    local AddCharacter = function(Character)
        if not Table.Connections.Gun then Table.Connections.Gun = {} end
        if not Table.Gun.CurrentTools then Table.Gun.CurrentTools = {} end
    
        Utility.NewConnection(Character.ChildAdded, function(Object)
            if Object:IsA("Tool") then
                SetupTool(Object)
            end
        end)
    
        Utility.NewConnection(Character.ChildRemoved, function(Object)
            if Table.Gun.CurrentTools and Table.Gun.CurrentTools[Object] then
                if Object and Object.Parent and Table.Connections.Gun[Object] and Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalGrip then
                    Object.Grip = Table.Connections.Gun[Object].OriginalGrip
                end
                for _, Connection in pairs(Table.Connections.Gun[Object]) do
                    if typeof(Connection) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                        pcall(function()
                            Connection:Disconnect()
                        end)
                    end
                end
                Table.Connections.Gun[Object] = nil
                Table.Gun.CurrentTools[Object] = nil
    
                local LastTool = nil
                for Tool, _ in pairs(Table.Gun.CurrentTools) do LastTool = Tool end
                Table.Gun.CurrentTool = LastTool
    
                if not next(Table.Gun.CurrentTools) then
                    Table.Gun.PreviousAmmo = 999
                end
            end
        end)
    
        for _, Object in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
            if Object:IsA("Tool") then
                SetupTool(Object)
            end
        end
    end
    
    Utility.NewConnection(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(Character)
        Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        
        for EmoteName in pairs(Table.Emotes) do
            Misc.LoadEmoteTrack(Character, EmoteName)
        end
        
        if next(OriginalAnimations) == nil then
            Misc.SaveOriginalAnimations(Character)
        end
        
        if Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.Enabled then
            Misc.ApplyAnimationPack(Settings.Player.General.Character.AnimationPlayer.WhichAnimation)
        end
        
        if Settings.Player.Movement.NoJumpCooldown then 
            LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.UseJumpPower = false 
        end 
        
        local Headless = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Headless
        if Headless then
            local Head = Character:WaitForChild("Head")
            local Decal = Head:WaitForChild("face", 1)
            if Decal then
                Decal.Transparency = 1
            end
            Head.Transparency = 1
        end
        
        local Korblox = Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Korblox
        if Korblox then
            local RightLowerLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
            local RightUpperLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            local RightFoot = Character:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
            if RightLowerLeg then
                RightLowerLeg.MeshId = "902942093"
                RightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1
            end
            if RightUpperLeg then
                RightUpperLeg.MeshId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=902942096"
                RightUpperLeg.TextureID = "http://roblox.com/asset/?id=902843398"
            end
            if RightFoot then
                RightFoot.MeshId = "902942089"
                RightFoot.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
        
        AddCharacter(Character)
        Features.HighlightBody(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.HighlightBody.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.HighlightBody.Color, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.HighlightBody.Outline)
        Features.ForcefieldBody(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ForcefieldBody.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ForcefieldBody.Color)
        Features.ForcefieldHats(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ForcefieldHats.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.ForcefieldHats.Color)
        Features.Trail(Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Trail.Enabled, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Trail.Color, Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Trail.Lifetime)
    end)
    
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        for EmoteName in pairs(Table.Emotes) do
            Misc.LoadEmoteTrack(LocalPlayer.Character, EmoteName)
        end
        
        if next(OriginalAnimations) == nil then
            Misc.SaveOriginalAnimations(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
        
        if Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.Enabled then
            Misc.ApplyAnimationPack(Settings.Player.General.Character.AnimationPlayer.WhichAnimation)
        end
        
        AddCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
    
    for _, Plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
            if Plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                SetupHitMarker(Plr)
                Misc.ModDetection(Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Enabled, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Method, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Rank, Plr)
            end
        end))
    end
    
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(Plr)
        coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
            if Plr ~= LocalPlayer then
                SetupHitMarker(Plr)
                Misc.ModDetection(Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Enabled, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Method, Settings.Misc.Utilities.ModDetection.Rank, Plr)
            end
        end))
    end)
    
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(Plr)
        if Table.Ragebot.Target and Table.Ragebot.Target == Plr then
            Table.Ragebot.Target = nil
            Library.Notify("Ragebot target cleared due to player leaving")
        end
        
        if Table.AimAssist.Target and Table.AimAssist.Target == Plr then
            Table.AimAssist.Target = nil
            Library.Notify("Aim assist target cleared due to player leaving")
        end
    end)
    
    -- * Metamethod Hooks
    
    if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") then
        local Environment
        local Global
        
        for _, Instance in getnilinstances() do
            if Instance.ClassName ~= "LocalScript" or Instance.Name ~= "LocalScript" then continue end
    
            local ScriptEnvironment = getsenv(Instance)
            if type(ScriptEnvironment) ~= "table" or type(ScriptEnvironment.mobileShiftLockMousePos) ~= "function" then continue end
            Instance.Name = "Guns Framework"
            Environment = ScriptEnvironment
            Global = ScriptEnvironment._G
            break
        end
        
        Environment._G = setmetatable({}, {
            __index = Global,
            __newindex = function(Self, Key, Value)
                Global[Key] = Value
                if Key == "MOUSE_POSITION" then
                    if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target then
                        Global[Key] = Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition()
                    end
                    if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and CanShoot then
                        Global[Key] = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
                    end
                end
            end
        })
    
        Utility.NewConnection(RunService.PostSimulation, function(DeltaTime)
            if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target then
                Global["MOUSE_POSITION"] = Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition()
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and CanShoot then
                Global["MOUSE_POSITION"] = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
            end
        end)
    end
    
    if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment") then
        local GunModule = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("GunHandler"))
        local OldGetAim = GunModule.getAim
    
        GunModule.getAim = function(Origin, Range)
            if not Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and not Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled then
                return OldGetAim(Origin, Range)
            end
    
            local TargetPosition
            
            if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target then
                TargetPosition = Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition()
            end
    
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and CanShoot then
                TargetPosition = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
            end
    
            if TargetPosition then
                local Direction = (TargetPosition - Origin).Unit
                local Distance = (TargetPosition - Origin).Magnitude
                return Direction, Distance
            end
    
            return OldGetAim(Origin, Range)
        end
    end
    
    local OldIndex = nil
    OldIndex = hookmetamethod(LocalPlayer:GetMouse(), '__index', LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Self, Index)
        if Self == Mouse and string.lower(Index) == 'hit' then
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiAimViewer and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Method == "Index" then
                return OldIndex(Self, Index)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Method == "Index" and CanShoot then
                local Position = NewCFrame(SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition())
                return Position
            elseif Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Ragebot.General.Method == "Index" then
                local Position = NewCFrame(Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
                return Position
            end
        end
        return OldIndex(Self, Index)
    end))
    
    local OldNamecall = nil
    OldNamecall = hookmetamethod(Game, "__namecall", LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Self, ...)
        local Args, Method = {...}, tostring(getnamecallmethod())
    
        if not checkcaller() and Method == "FireServer" then
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                for I, Arg in pairs(Args) do
                    if typeof(Arg) == "Vector3" then
                        Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemote = Self
                        Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemoteFound = true
                        Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemoteArgs = Args
                        Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemotePositionIndex = I
                        if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Method == "Namecall" and CanShoot and not Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiAimViewer then
                            Args[I] = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
                        end
                        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Ragebot.General.Method == "Namecall" then
                            Args[I] = Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition()
                        end
                    elseif type(Arg) == "table" then
                        for Index, Element in ipairs(Arg) do
                            if typeof(Element) == "Vector3" then
                                Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemote = Self
                                Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemoteFound = true
                                Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemoteArgs = Arg
                                Table.AntiAimViewer.MouseRemotePositionIndex = Index
                                if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Enabled and Table.SilentAim.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Method == "Namecall" and CanShoot and not Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiAimViewer then
                                    Arg[Index] = SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition()
                                end
                                if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and not (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment" or  GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment") and Settings.Ragebot.General.Method == "Namecall" then
                                    Arg[Index] = Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition()
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            return OldNamecall(Self, unpack(Args))
        end
    
        return OldNamecall(Self, ...)
    end))
    
    local NewIndex = nil
    NewIndex = hookmetamethod(Game, "__newindex", LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(Self, Index, Value)
        if not checkcaller() then
            if Self == Camera then
                if getcallingscript().Name == "Framework" then
                    if Index == "CFrame" and Settings.Visuals.View.NoRecoil then
                        return
                    end
                elseif Index == "CFrame" and Settings.Visuals.View.AspectRatio.Enabled then
                    local StretchRatio = Settings.Visuals.View.AspectRatio.Amount / 100
                    return NewIndex(Self, Index, Value * NewCFrame(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, StretchRatio, 0, 0, 0, 1))
                elseif Index == "FieldOfView" then
                    Table.World.Fov = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.View.FieldOfView.Enabled then
                        return
                    end
                end
            elseif Self == Lighting then
                if Index == "ClockTime" then
                    Table.World.ClockTime = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.ClockTime.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "FogColor" then
                    Table.World.FogColor = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Fog.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "FogStart" then
                    Table.World.FogStart = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Fog.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "FogEnd" then
                    Table.World.FogEnd = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Fog.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "Ambient" then
                    Table.World.Ambient = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Ambient.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "OutdoorAmbient" then
                    Table.World.OutdoorAmbient = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Ambient.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "ExposureCompensation" then
                    Table.World.ExposureCompensation = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Exposure.Enabled then return end
                elseif Index == "Brightness" then
                    Table.World.Brightness = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.World.Brightness.Enabled then return end
                end
            elseif typeof(Self) == "Instance" then
                if Self.Name == "PepperSprayBlur" and Settings.Visuals.View.NoBlur then
                    if Value then
                        return
                    end
                elseif Self == LocalPlayer and Index == "CameraMaxZoomDistance" then
                    Table.World.MaxZoomDistance = Value
                    if Settings.Visuals.View.InfiniteZoom then return end
                end
            end
        end
        return NewIndex(Self, Index, Value)
    end))
    
    -- * Menu Setup
    
  local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

-- // freemium.lol CFG Configuration System
-- Full config system: saves every registered Toggle/ColorPicker/KeyPicker/
-- Dropdown/Slider/Input value and restores them through the normal callbacks.
local ConfigFolder = "freemium.lol"
local ConfigName = "default"
local ConfigList = nil
local ConfigNameInput = nil
local ConfigDropdown = nil
local ConfigVersion = 2

if not isfolder(ConfigFolder) then
    makefolder(ConfigFolder)
end

local function SanitizeConfigName(Name)
    Name = tostring(Name or "")
    Name = Name:gsub("[%c<>:\"/\\|%?%*]", "")
    Name = Name:gsub("%.cfg$", "")
    Name = Name:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if Name == "" then
        Name = "default"
    end
    return Name:sub(1, 64)
end

local function GetConfigPath(Name)
    return ConfigFolder .. "/" .. SanitizeConfigName(Name) .. ".cfg"
end

local function EncodeValue(value, seen)
    local valueType = typeof(value)

    if valueType == "Color3" then
        return {
            __type = "Color3",
            R = value.R,
            G = value.G,
            B = value.B
        }
    elseif valueType == "EnumItem" then
        return {
            __type = "EnumItem",
            EnumType = tostring(value.EnumType),
            Name = value.Name
        }
    elseif valueType == "Vector2" then
        return {
            __type = "Vector2",
            X = value.X,
            Y = value.Y
        }
    elseif valueType == "Vector3" then
        return {
            __type = "Vector3",
            X = value.X,
            Y = value.Y,
            Z = value.Z
        }
    elseif type(value) == "table" then
        seen = seen or {}
        if seen[value] then
            return nil
        end
        seen[value] = true

        local result = {}
        for k, v in pairs(value) do
            local encoded = EncodeValue(v, seen)
            if encoded ~= nil then
                result[tostring(k)] = encoded
            end
        end
        seen[value] = nil
        return result
    end

    if type(value) == "number" then
        if value ~= value or value == math.huge or value == -math.huge then
            return 0
        end
    end

    return value
end

local function DecodeValue(value)
    if type(value) ~= "table" then
        return value
    end

    if value.__type == "Color3" then
        return Color3.new(tonumber(value.R) or 0, tonumber(value.G) or 0, tonumber(value.B) or 0)
    elseif value.__type == "Vector2" then
        return Vector2.new(tonumber(value.X) or 0, tonumber(value.Y) or 0)
    elseif value.__type == "Vector3" then
        return Vector3.new(tonumber(value.X) or 0, tonumber(value.Y) or 0, tonumber(value.Z) or 0)
    elseif value.__type == "EnumItem" then
        local enumName = tostring(value.EnumType or ""):gsub("Enum%.", "")
        local enum = Enum[enumName]
        if enum and value.Name and enum[value.Name] then
            return enum[value.Name]
        end
        return nil
    end

    local result = {}
    for k, v in pairs(value) do
        result[k] = DecodeValue(v)
    end
    return result
end

local function SerializeCfgValue(value, depth)
    depth = depth or 0

    if value == nil then
        return "nil"
    elseif type(value) == "boolean" then
        return value and "true" or "false"
    elseif type(value) == "number" then
        if value ~= value then return "0" end
        if value == math.huge then return "math.huge" end
        if value == -math.huge then return "-math.huge" end
        return tostring(value)
    elseif type(value) == "string" then
        return string.format("%q", value)
    elseif type(value) == "table" then
        local Parts = {"{"}
        local Indent = string.rep("    ", depth + 1)
        local ClosingIndent = string.rep("    ", depth)

        for k, v in pairs(value) do
            local Key = tostring(k)
            if Key:match("^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$") then
                Key = Key
            else
                Key = "[" .. string.format("%q", Key) .. "]"
            end
            table.insert(Parts, Indent .. Key .. " = " .. SerializeCfgValue(v, depth + 1) .. ",")
        end

        table.insert(Parts, ClosingIndent .. "}")
        return table.concat(Parts, "\n")
    end

    return "nil"
end

local function BuildConfigData()
    local data = {
        __config_version = ConfigVersion,
        Options = {},
        Toggles = {},
        -- Save the actual runtime Settings table too. Some controls in this
        -- script intentionally do not have a UI callback, so saving only
        -- Options/Toggles cannot restore those values.
        Settings = EncodeValue(Settings)
    }

    -- Save every registered option. These are the actual UI values used by
    -- the callbacks, so loading them restores the underlying script state too.
    for id, option in pairs(Options) do
        if option and option.Type then
            local saved

            if option.Type == "ColorPicker" then
                saved = {
                    Type = "ColorPicker",
                    Value = EncodeValue(option.Value),
                    Transparency = option.Transparency
                }
            elseif option.Type == "KeyPicker" then
                -- KeyPicker:SetValue expects {Key, Mode}; the old config system
                -- incorrectly saved only the key string, which made keybinds fail.
                saved = {
                    Type = "KeyPicker",
                    Value = EncodeValue(option.Value),
                    Toggled = option.Toggled,
                    Mode = option.Mode
                }
            elseif option.Type == "Dropdown" then
                saved = {
                    Type = "Dropdown",
                    Value = EncodeValue(option.Value),
                    Multi = option.Multi == true
                }
            elseif option.Type == "Slider" then
                saved = {
                    Type = "Slider",
                    Value = option.Value
                }
            elseif option.Type == "Input" or option.Type == "Textbox" then
                saved = {
                    Type = "Input",
                    Value = option.Value
                }
            end

            -- Don't save the config manager's own temporary controls.
            if saved and id ~= "ConfigName" and id ~= "ConfigList" then
                data.Options[id] = saved
            end
        end
    end

    -- Toggles are stored separately because the library registers them in
    -- Toggles rather than Options.
    for id, toggle in pairs(Toggles) do
        if toggle and toggle.Type == "Toggle" then
            data.Toggles[id] = {
                Type = "Toggle",
                Value = toggle.Value == true
            }
        end
    end

    return data
end

local function ApplyConfigData(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    -- Support both the new format and the older flat format generated by the
    -- previous config system.
    local savedOptions = data.Options or data
    local savedToggles = data.Toggles or {}

    if type(savedOptions) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    -- First restore the UI controls. Their normal callbacks update the live
    -- Settings table, and this also updates dependency boxes/visual state.
    for id, saved in pairs(savedOptions) do
        if id ~= "__config_version" and id ~= "ConfigName" and id ~= "ConfigList" then
            local option = Options[id]

            if type(saved) == "table" and option then
                if saved.Type == "ColorPicker" then
                    local color = DecodeValue(saved.Value)
                    if color then
                        pcall(function()
                            option:SetValueRGB(color, tonumber(saved.Transparency) or 0)
                        end)
                    end

                elseif saved.Type == "KeyPicker" then
                    local key = DecodeValue(saved.Value)
                    local mode = saved.Mode or option.Mode or "Toggle"

                    if key ~= nil then
                        pcall(function()
                            option:SetValue({key, mode})
                        end)
                    end

                    if saved.Toggled ~= nil then
                        option.Toggled = saved.Toggled == true
                        pcall(function() option:Update() end)
                    end

                elseif saved.Type == "Dropdown" then
                    pcall(function() option:SetValue(DecodeValue(saved.Value)) end)

                elseif saved.Type == "Slider" then
                    pcall(function() option:SetValue(saved.Value) end)

                elseif saved.Type == "Input" or saved.Type == "Textbox" then
                    pcall(function() option:SetValue(saved.Value) end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for id, saved in pairs(savedToggles) do
        local toggle = Toggles[id]
        if type(saved) == "table" and toggle and saved.Type == "Toggle" then
            pcall(function() toggle:SetValue(saved.Value == true) end)
        end
    end

    -- Then restore the actual Settings table. This is the important part for
    -- controls that have no callback, and for any runtime setting whose value
    -- is represented outside the UI registry. Unsupported userdata/Instances
    -- are intentionally omitted by EncodeValue and therefore left untouched.
    local savedSettings = data.Settings
    if type(savedSettings) == "table" and type(Settings) == "table" then
        local function RestoreTable(target, source)
            for key, value in pairs(source) do
                local decoded = DecodeValue(value)

                if type(decoded) == "table" and type(target[key]) == "table" then
                    RestoreTable(target[key], decoded)
                elseif decoded ~= nil then
                    target[key] = decoded
                end
            end
        end

        RestoreTable(Settings, savedSettings)
    end

    return true
end

local SaveConfig

local function CreateConfig(Name, Silent)
    Name = SanitizeConfigName(Name)
    local Path = GetConfigPath(Name)

    if isfile(Path) then
        if not Silent then
            Library:Notify(Name .. ".cfg already exists. Use Overwrite to replace it.", 2)
        end
        return false
    end

    local Success = SaveConfig(Name, true)
    if Success and not Silent then
        Library:Notify("Created " .. Name .. ".cfg", 2)
    end
    return Success
end

SaveConfig = function(Name, Silent)
    Name = SanitizeConfigName(Name)
    local Path = GetConfigPath(Name)
    local Data = BuildConfigData()
    local Payload = "-- freemium.lol configuration v" .. tostring(ConfigVersion) .. "\nreturn " .. SerializeCfgValue(Data) .. "\n"

    local Success, Error = pcall(function()
        writefile(Path, Payload)
    end)

    if not Success then
        Library:Notify("Failed to save " .. Name .. ".cfg: " .. tostring(Error), 3)
        return false
    end

    ConfigName = Name
    if ConfigNameInput then
        ConfigNameInput:SetValue(Name)
    end
    if ConfigDropdown and ConfigList and table.find(ConfigList, Name) then
        ConfigDropdown:SetValue(Name)
    end

    if not Silent then
        Library:Notify("Saved " .. Name .. ".cfg", 2)
    end
    return true
end

local function LoadConfig(Name, Silent)
    Name = SanitizeConfigName(Name)
    local Path = GetConfigPath(Name)

    if not isfile(Path) then
        if not Silent then
            Library:Notify("No " .. Name .. ".cfg found.", 2)
        end
        return false
    end

    local Success, Data = pcall(function()
        local Source = readfile(Path)
        local Loader = loadstring(Source)
        if not Loader then
            error("Invalid cfg source")
        end
        return Loader()
    end)

    if not Success or type(Data) ~= "table" or not ApplyConfigData(Data) then
        if not Silent then
            Library:Notify("Invalid " .. Name .. ".cfg", 3)
        end
        return false
    end

    ConfigName = Name
    if ConfigNameInput then
        ConfigNameInput:SetValue(Name)
    end
    if ConfigDropdown and ConfigList and table.find(ConfigList, Name) then
        ConfigDropdown:SetValue(Name)
    end

    if not Silent then
        Library:Notify("Loaded " .. Name .. ".cfg", 2)
    end
    return true
end

local function DeleteConfig(Name)
    Name = SanitizeConfigName(Name)
    local Path = GetConfigPath(Name)

    if isfile(Path) then
        local Success, Error = pcall(function()
            delfile(Path)
        end)
        if Success then
            Library:Notify("Deleted " .. Name .. ".cfg", 2)
        else
            Library:Notify("Failed to delete " .. Name .. ".cfg: " .. tostring(Error), 3)
        end
    else
        Library:Notify("No " .. Name .. ".cfg found.", 2)
    end
end

local function RefreshConfigList()
    local Values = {}

    if listfiles then
        local Success, Files = pcall(function()
            return listfiles(ConfigFolder)
        end)

        if Success and type(Files) == "table" then
            for _, FilePath in ipairs(Files) do
                local FileName = tostring(FilePath):match("([^/\\]+)%.cfg$")
                if FileName then
                    table.insert(Values, FileName)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(Values, function(a, b)
        return a:lower() < b:lower()
    end)

    if #Values == 0 then
        Values = {"< no cfg files >"}
    end

    ConfigList = Values
    if ConfigDropdown then
        ConfigDropdown:SetValues(Values)
        if table.find(Values, ConfigName) then
            ConfigDropdown:SetValue(ConfigName)
        end
    end

    return Values
end

        -- // Aim Assist
        do
            local AimAssist = Sections.Legitbot.AimAssist:AddToggle("AimAssistEnabled", {Text = "Enabled", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Enabled = a
            end})
            if IsMobile then
                local AimAssistPopup = Sections.Legitbot.AimAssist:AddDependencyBox()
                AimAssistPopup:AddButton({Text = "Load Button", Func = function()
                    if AimAssistButtonAlreadyLoaded then
                        Library:Notify("Already loaded.")
                        return
                    end
                    AimAssistButtonAlreadyLoaded = true
                    
                    local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                    local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    local TextButton = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    local UITextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
                    SizeMultiply = 1.5
                    ScreenGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
                    ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
                    
                    Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                    Frame.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
                    Frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, Random(-128, 128), 0, 0)
                    Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90*SizeMultiply, 0, 32*SizeMultiply)
                    Frame.Active = true
                    
                    TextButton.Parent = Frame
                    TextButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    TextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26*SizeMultiply, 0, 26*SizeMultiply)
                    TextButton.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0, 0.5)
                    TextButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
                    TextButton.Image = "rbxassetid://133131442644680"
                    
                    TextLabel.Parent = Frame
                    TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52*SizeMultiply, 0, 26*SizeMultiply)
                    TextLabel.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0.5, 0.5)
                    TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.65, 0, 0.5, 0)
                    TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Arimo
                    TextLabel.Text = "Aim Assist"
                    TextLabel.TextColor3 = NewRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    TextLabel.TextScaled = true
                    
                    local UiCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", Frame)
                    UiCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
                    
                    local ButtonState = false
                    Utility.NewConnection(TextLabel.MouseButton1Down, function()
                        if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Enabled then
                            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Device", Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Size, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Flags)
                            
                            Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting or false
                            
                            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AutoSwitch and NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and Table.AimAssist.Target ~= NewTarget then
                                Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = true
                            end
                            
                            Table.AimAssist.Target = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and NewTarget or nil
                            
                            local Message = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and string.format("Targeting %s", Table.AimAssist.Target.DisplayName) or "Untargeting"
                            Library.Notify(Message)
                        end
                        
                        TextButton.Image = Table.AimAssist.Target and "rbxassetid://133131442644680" or "rbxassetid://133131442644680"
                    end)
                    
                    local Dragging = false
                    local DragInput, DragStart, StartPos
                    
                    local function Update(input)
                        if not DragStart then return end
                        local Delta = input.Position - DragStart
                        Frame.Position = UDim2.new(StartPos.X.Scale, StartPos.X.Offset + Delta.X, StartPos.Y.Scale, StartPos.Y.Offset + Delta.Y)
                    end
                    
                    Utility.NewConnection(Frame.InputBegan, function(input)
                        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            Dragging = true
                            DragStart = input.Position
                            StartPos = Frame.Position
                    
                            Utility.NewConnection(input.Changed, function()
                                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                                    Dragging = false
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Utility.NewConnection(Frame.InputChanged, function(input)
                        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            DragInput = input
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    Utility.NewConnection(game:GetService("UserInputService").InputChanged, function(input)
                        if Dragging and input == DragInput then
                            Update(input)
                        end
                    end)
                end})
                AimAssistPopup:AddButton({Text = "Load Tool", Func = function()
                    if AimAssistToolAlreadyLoaded then
                        Library:Notify("Already loaded.")
                        return
                    end
                    AimAssistToolAlreadyLoaded = true
                    local Tool = Instance.new("Tool")
                    Tool.RequiresHandle = false
                    Tool.Name = "Aim Assist Tool"
                    Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                    
                    local Player = LocalPlayer
                    
                    local function ConnectCharacterAdded()
                        Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)
                    end
                    
                    ConnectCharacterAdded()
                    
                    Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
                        Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                    end)
                    
                    Tool.Activated:Connect(function()
                        if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Enabled then
                            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Mouse", Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Size, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Flags)
                            
                            Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting or false
                            
                            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AutoSwitch and NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and Table.AimAssist.Target ~= NewTarget then
                                Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = true
                            end
                            
                            Table.AimAssist.Target = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and NewTarget or nil
                            
                            local Message = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and string.format("Targeting %s", Table.AimAssist.Target.DisplayName) or "Untargeting"
                            Library:Notify(Message)
                        end
                    end)
                end})
                AimAssistPopup:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["AimAssistEnabled"], true}})
            else
                AimAssist:AddKeyPicker("AimAssistKeybind", {Default = "None", NoUI = true, Text = "Aim Assist", Callback = function()
                    if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Enabled then
                        local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Mouse", Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.FieldOfView.Size, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Checks.Flags)
                    
                        Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting or false
                    
                        if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AutoSwitch and NewTarget and not Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and Table.AimAssist.Target ~= NewTarget then
                            Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting = true
                        end
                    
                        Table.AimAssist.Target = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and NewTarget or nil
                    
                        local Message = Table.AimAssist.IsTargetting and string.format("Targeting %s", Table.AimAssist.Target.DisplayName) or "Untargeting"
                        Library:Notify(Message)
                    end
                end})
            end

            end})
            TriggerbotFov:AddColorPicker("TriggerbotFovFromOutline", {Default = MainColor, Title = "From Outline", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.ShowFov.From.Outline = a
            end})
            TriggerbotFov:AddColorPicker("TriggerbotFovToOutline", {Default = MainColor, Title = "To Outline", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.ShowFov.To.Outline = a
            end})
            FieldOfView:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["TriggerbotFieldOfViewEnabled"], true}})
            local Spin = Sections.Legitbot.Triggerbot:AddDependencyBox()
            Spin:AddToggle("TriggerbotFovSpinEnabled", {Text = "Spin", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.ShowFov.Spin.Enabled = a
            end})
            Spin:AddSlider("TriggerbotFovSpinSpeed", {Text = "Speed", Default = 1, Min = 1, Max = 250, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "rps", Compact = true, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Legitbot.Triggerbot.FieldOfView.ShowFov.Spin.Speed = a
            end})
            Spin:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["ShowTriggerbotFov"], true}})
        end
    end
    
    -- // Mobile Ragebot floating target button
    local SpawnRagebotTargetButton
    SpawnRagebotTargetButton = function(Player)
        if not IsMobile or not Player then return end

        local Existing = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"):FindFirstChild("freemium.lol_RagebotTarget")
        if Existing then
            Existing:Destroy()
        end

        local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        ScreenGui.Name = "freemium.lol_RagebotTarget"
        ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

        local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
        Frame.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(255,255,255)
        Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1,-96,0,70)
        Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0,90,0,90)
        Frame.Active = true

        local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner", Frame)
        UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)

        local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame)
        UIStroke.Color = NewRGB(0,0,0)
        UIStroke.Thickness = 1

        local Logo = Instance.new("TextButton")
        Logo.Parent = Frame
        Logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Logo.Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,1,-8)
        Logo.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
        Logo.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0.5,0.5)
        Logo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        Logo.Text = "F"
        Logo.TextColor3 = NewRGB(0,0,0)
        Logo.TextScaled = true
        Logo.AutoButtonColor = false

        local UITextSizeConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
        UITextSizeConstraint.Parent = Logo
        UITextSizeConstraint.MaxTextSize = 56
        UITextSizeConstraint.MinTextSize = 20

        local LockedPlayer = Player
        Logo.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
            if not Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled then
                Library:Notify("Please turn on ragebot first!")
                return
            end

            if LockedPlayer and LockedPlayer.Character then
                Table.Ragebot.Target = LockedPlayer
                Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = true
                Library:Notify("Targeting " .. tostring(LockedPlayer.DisplayName or LockedPlayer.Name))
            else
                Table.Ragebot.Target = nil
                Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = false
                Library:Notify("Selected player is no longer valid.")
            end
        end)

        local InputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local Dragging = false
        local DragInput
        local DragStart
        local StartPos

        local function UpdateDrag(Input)
            if not DragStart then return end
            local Delta = Input.Position - DragStart
            Frame.Position = UDim2.new(StartPos.X.Scale, StartPos.X.Offset + Delta.X, StartPos.Y.Scale, StartPos.Y.Offset + Delta.Y)
        end

        Frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                Dragging = true
                DragStart = Input.Position
                StartPos = Frame.Position
                Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        Dragging = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)

        Frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
            if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                DragInput = Input
            end
        end)

        InputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
            if Dragging and Input == DragInput then
                UpdateDrag(Input)
            end
        end)
    end

    -- // Ragebot Tab
    do
        -- // General
        do
            local Ragebot = Sections.Ragebot.General:AddToggle("RagebotEnabled", {Text = "Enabled", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled = a
            end})
            if IsMobile then
                local RagebotPopup = Sections.Ragebot.General:AddDependencyBox()
                RagebotPopup:AddButton({Text = "Load Button", Func = function()
                    if RagebotButtonAlreadyLoaded then
                        Library:Notify("Already loaded.")
                        return
                    end
                    RagebotButtonAlreadyLoaded = true

                    -- Mobile lock-target button: same square frame, A logo only.
                    local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                    ScreenGui.Name = "freemium.lol_LockTarget"
                    ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
                    ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                    ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

                    local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    Frame.Parent = ScreenGui
                    Frame.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(255,255,255)
                    Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                    Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1,-96,0,-32)
                    Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0,90,0,90)
                    Frame.Active = true

                    local UICorner = Instance.new("UICorner", Frame)
                    UICorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)

                    local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", Frame)
                    UIStroke.Color = NewRGB(0,0,0)
                    UIStroke.Thickness = 1

                    local Logo = Instance.new("ImageButton")
                    Logo.Parent = Frame
                    Logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    Logo.Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,1,-8)
                    Logo.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
                    Logo.AnchorPoint = NewVector2(0.5,0.5)
                    Logo.Image = "rbxassetid://133131442644680"
                    Logo.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit

                        if not Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled then
                            Library:Notify("Please turn on ragebot first!")
                            return
                        end

                        Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = not Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting
                        if Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting then
                            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer(
                                "Device",
                                Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Enabled,
                                Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Size,
                                Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Enabled,
                                Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Flags
                            )

                            if NewTarget and NewTarget.Character then
                                Table.Ragebot.Target = NewTarget
                                Library:Notify("Targeting " .. tostring(NewTarget.DisplayName or NewTarget.Name))
                            else
                                Table.Ragebot.Target = nil
                                Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = false
                                Library:Notify("No valid target found.")
                            end
                        else
                            Table.Ragebot.Target = nil
                            Library:Notify("Untargeting")
                        end
                    end)

                    local InputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
                    local Dragging = false
                    local DragInput
                    local DragStart
                    local StartPos

                    local function UpdateDrag(Input)
                        if not DragStart then return end
                        local Delta = Input.Position - DragStart
                        Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                            StartPos.X.Scale,
                            StartPos.X.Offset + Delta.X,
                            StartPos.Y.Scale,
                            StartPos.Y.Offset + Delta.Y
                        )
                    end

                    Frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
                        if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            Dragging = true
                            DragStart = Input.Position
                            StartPos = Frame.Position
                            Input.Changed:Connect(function()
                                if Input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                                    Dragging = false
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)

                    Frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
                        if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            DragInput = Input
                        end
                    end)

                    InputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
                        if Dragging and Input == DragInput then
                            UpdateDrag(Input)
                        end
                    end)
                end})
                RagebotPopup:AddButton({Text = "Load Tool", Func = function()
                    if RagebotToolAlreadyLoaded then
                        Library:Notify("Already loaded.")
                        return
                    end
                    RagebotToolAlreadyLoaded = true
                    
                    local Tool = Instance.new("Tool")
                    Tool.RequiresHandle = false
                    Tool.Name = "Ragebot Tool"
                    Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                    
                    local Player = LocalPlayer
                    
                    local function ConnectCharacterAdded()
                        Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)
                    end
                    
                    ConnectCharacterAdded()
                    
                    Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
                        Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
                    end)
                    
                    Tool.Activated:Connect(function()
                        if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled then
                            Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = not Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting
                            
                            local NewTarget = Combat.GetClosestPlayer("Mouse", Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Enabled, Settings.Ragebot.General.FieldOfView.Size, Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Enabled, Settings.Ragebot.General.Checks.Flags)
                            Table.Ragebot.Target = Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and NewTarget.Character and NewTarget or nil
                            
                            local Message = Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and string.format("Targeting %s", Table.Ragebot.Target.DisplayName) or "Untargeting"
                            Library:Notify(Message)
                        end
                    end)
                end})
                RagebotPopup:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["RagebotEnabled"], true}})
            else
                Ragebot:AddKeyPicker("RagebotKeybind", {Default = "None", NoUI = true, Text = "Ragebot", Callback = function()
                    if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled then
                        Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = not Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting

                        Table.Ragebot.Target = Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting and NewTarget.Character and NewTarget or nil

        do

                            return
                        end

                            if Settings.Ragebot.Exploits.MultipleWeapons then
                                local Backpack = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack")
                                for _, Tool in ipairs(Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                                    if Tool:IsA("Tool") and Tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo") then
                                        Tool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
                                    end
                                end
                            end

            Sections.Visuals.SelfESP:AddToggle("Headless", {Text = "Headless", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Headless = a
                
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if Character then
                   local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                   if Head then
                        local Decal = Head:FindFirstChildOfClass("Decal")
                        if Decal then
                            Decal.Transparency = a and 1 or 0
                        end
                        Head.Transparency = a and 1 or 0
                    end
                end

            local OriginalLegMeshes = {}
            Sections.Visuals.SelfESP:AddToggle("Korblox", {Text = "Korblox", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.SelfESP.Korblox = a
                
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if Character then
                    local RightLowerLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                    local RightUpperLeg = Character:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
                    local RightFoot = Character:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                
                    if not OriginalLegMeshes.Cached then
                        if RightLowerLeg then
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightLowerLegMeshId = RightLowerLeg.MeshId
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightLowerLegTransparency = RightLowerLeg.Transparency
                        end
                        if RightUpperLeg then
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightUpperLegMeshId = RightUpperLeg.MeshId
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightUpperLegTextureID = RightUpperLeg.TextureID
                        end
                        if RightFoot then
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightFootMeshId = RightFoot.MeshId
                            OriginalLegMeshes.RightFootTransparency = RightFoot.Transparency
                        end
                        OriginalLegMeshes.Cached = true
                    end
                
                    if a then
                        if RightLowerLeg then
                            RightLowerLeg.MeshId = "902942093"
                            RightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1
                        end
                        if RightUpperLeg then
                            RightUpperLeg.MeshId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=902942096"
                            RightUpperLeg.TextureID = "http://roblox.com/asset/?id=902843398"
                        end
                        if RightFoot then
                            RightFoot.MeshId = "902942089"
                            RightFoot.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    else
                        if RightLowerLeg then
                            RightLowerLeg.MeshId = OriginalLegMeshes.RightLowerLegMeshId or RightLowerLeg.MeshId
                            RightLowerLeg.Transparency = OriginalLegMeshes.RightLowerLegTransparency or 0
                        end
                        if RightUpperLeg then
                            RightUpperLeg.MeshId = OriginalLegMeshes.RightUpperLegMeshId or RightUpperLeg.MeshId
                            RightUpperLeg.TextureID = OriginalLegMeshes.RightUpperLegTextureID or RightUpperLeg.TextureID
                        end
                        if RightFoot then
                            RightFoot.MeshId = OriginalLegMeshes.RightFootMeshId or RightFoot.MeshId
                            RightFoot.Transparency = OriginalLegMeshes.RightFootTransparency or 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end})

            -- // Verified Badge
            Sections.Visuals.SelfESP:AddButton({Text = "Apply Verified Badge", Func = function()
                local function addOverheadBadge()
                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    if not char then return end
                    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not head then return end
                    if head:FindFirstChild("VerifiedBadgeBillboard") then return end

                    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                    billboard.Name = "VerifiedBadgeBillboard"
                    billboard.Adornee = head
                    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 20)
                    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
                    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
                    billboard.Parent = head

                    local container = Instance.new("Frame")
                    container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    container.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
                    container.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
                    container.Parent = billboard

                    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                    nameLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                    nameLabel.Text = LocalPlayer.DisplayName
                    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                    nameLabel.TextSize = 11
                    nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    nameLabel.Parent = container

                    local badge = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    badge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
                    badge.Image = "rbxassetid://76191174048129"
                    badge.Parent = container

                    local function updatePositions()
                        local totalWidth = nameLabel.AbsoluteSize.X + 4 + badge.AbsoluteSize.X
                        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -totalWidth / 2, 0.5, 0)
                        badge.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -totalWidth / 2 + nameLabel.AbsoluteSize.X + 4, 0.5, 0)
                    end

                    nameLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updatePositions)
                    badge:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updatePositions)
                    task.defer(updatePositions)

                    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if head and head.Parent then
                            local distance = (Camera.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude
                            billboard.Enabled = distance <= 50
                        end
                    end)

                    Library:Notify("Verified Badge applied successfully")
                end

                if LocalPlayer.Character then
                    addOverheadBadge()
                end

                LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.wait(1)
                    addOverheadBadge()
                end)
            end})

            Sections.Visuals.SelfESP:AddButton({Text = "Remove Verified Badge", Func = function()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if char then
                    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if head and head:FindFirstChild("VerifiedBadgeBillboard") then
                        head.VerifiedBadgeBillboard:Destroy()
                        Library:Notify("Verified Badge removed")
                    else
                        Library:Notify("No Verified Badge found to remove")
                    end
                end
            end})
        end

            local DefaultSky = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky")
            local CustomSkyInstance = nil
            local LightingSettings = {}
            if DefaultSky then
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxBk = DefaultSky.SkyboxBk
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxDn = DefaultSky.SkyboxDn
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxFt = DefaultSky.SkyboxFt
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxLf = DefaultSky.SkyboxLf
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxRt = DefaultSky.SkyboxRt
                LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxUp = DefaultSky.SkyboxUp
            end
            local function ApplyCustomSkybox(name)
                -- Remove existing custom sky
                if CustomSkyInstance then
                    CustomSkyInstance:Destroy()
                    CustomSkyInstance = nil
                end
                
                -- Hide default sky
                if DefaultSky then
                    DefaultSky.Parent = nil
                end
                
                local SelectedSkybox = Skybox[name]
                if not SelectedSkybox then return end
                
                -- Create new custom sky
                CustomSkyInstance = Instance.new("Sky")
                CustomSkyInstance.Name = "CustomLockedSky"
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxLf = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxLf
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxBk = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxBk
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxDn = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxDn
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxFt = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxFt
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxRt = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxRt
                CustomSkyInstance.SkyboxUp = SelectedSkybox.SkyboxUp
                CustomSkyInstance.Parent = Lighting
            end
            local function RestoreDefaultSkybox()
                if CustomSkyInstance then
                    CustomSkyInstance:Destroy()
                    CustomSkyInstance = nil
                end
                if DefaultSky then
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxBk = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxBk
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxDn = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxDn
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxFt = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxFt
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxLf = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxLf
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxRt = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxRt
                    DefaultSky.SkyboxUp = LightingSettings.DefaultSkyboxUp
                    DefaultSky.Parent = Lighting
                end
            end
            Sections.Visuals.World:AddToggle("CustomSkyboxEnabled", {Text = "Custom Skybox", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.World.CustomSkybox.Enabled = a
                if a then
                    ApplyCustomSkybox(Settings.Visuals.World.CustomSkybox.WhichSkybox)
                else
                    RestoreDefaultSkybox()
                end
            end})
            local CustomSkybox = Sections.Visuals.World:AddDependencyBox()
            CustomSkybox:AddDropdown("WhichCustomSkybox", {Values = SkyboxOptions, Default = "Default", Text = "Which Skybox", Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.World.CustomSkybox.WhichSkybox = a
                if Settings.Visuals.World.CustomSkybox.Enabled then
                    ApplyCustomSkybox(a)
                end
            end})
            CustomSkybox:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["CustomSkyboxEnabled"], true}})
