            -- // Custom Money Text
            local CustomMoneyTextConnection  = nil
            local CustomMoneyTextLiveConn    = nil  -- heartbeat for live money value updates

            local function GetMainScreenGui()
                local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                return pg and pg:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
            end

            -- Resolves what the label should display.
            -- If custom text is set, supports the token {money} which gets replaced
            -- with the real in-game money value read from the MoneyText label.
            local function ResolveDisplayText(rawMoneyText)
                local cmt = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText
                if not cmt.UseCustomText or cmt.CustomText == "" then
                    return nil  -- nil = leave the game's own text untouched
                end
                -- Replace {money} token with the actual value
                local result = cmt.CustomText:gsub("{money}", rawMoneyText or "")
                return result
            end

            local function UpdateCustomMoneyText(selected)
                local MainScreenGui = GetMainScreenGui()
                if not MainScreenGui then return end

                local style = selected or Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value
                local text  = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("MoneyText")
                if not text then return end

                text.TextColor3       = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled and Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Color or NewRGB(95, 255, 87)
                text.TextTransparency = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled and (Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Transparency or 0) or 0

                -- Apply custom text label if set (keep before children clear so we read raw text first)
                local rawText = text.Text
                local display = ResolveDisplayText(rawText)
                if display then
                    text.Text = display
                end

                -- Clear old injected children (image labels etc.)
                for _, child in ipairs(text:GetChildren()) do
                    child:Destroy()
                end

                local promoteButton = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Promote")
                if promoteButton then
                    promoteButton.Visible = true
                    if Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then
                        promoteButton.Visible = not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.HideButton
                    end
                end

                if style == "Default" then
                    text.Font                   = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    text.TextSize               = 25
                    text.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                    text.Position               = UDim2.new(1, -210, 1, -60)
                    text.AnchorPoint            = Vector2.new(0, 0)
                    text.AutomaticSize          = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
                    text.Size                   = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
                elseif style == "Simple" then
                    text.FontFace               = Font.new("rbxasset://fonts/families/SourceSansPro.json", Enum.FontWeight.Bold, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
                    text.TextSize               = 24
                    text.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                    text.AnchorPoint            = Vector2.new(1, 1)
                    text.AutomaticSize          = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                    text.Position               = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
                    text.Size                   = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20)

                    local imageValue = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Image
                    local imageId    = ""
                    if imageValue == "My Melody" then
                        imageId = "rbxassetid://18401067020"
                    elseif imageValue == "Custom" then
                        imageId = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.CustomImage or ""
                    end

                    local img                   = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    img.Name                    = "MoneyIcon"
                    img.Image                   = imageId
                    img.ResampleMode            = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated
                    img.AnchorPoint             = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
                    img.BackgroundTransparency  = 1
                    img.BorderColor3            = NewRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    img.Position                = UDim2.new(0, -28, 0.5, 0)
                    img.Size                    = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
                    img.Parent                  = text
                end
            end

            -- Live heartbeat: keeps the custom text format in sync as money changes in-game
            local function StartMoneyTextLiveUpdate()
                if CustomMoneyTextLiveConn then
                    CustomMoneyTextLiveConn:Disconnect()
                    CustomMoneyTextLiveConn = nil
                end
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.UseCustomText then return end
                if Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.CustomText == "" then return end

                local lastRaw = ""
                CustomMoneyTextLiveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()
                    if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                    local MainScreenGui = GetMainScreenGui()
                    if not MainScreenGui then return end
                    local text = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("MoneyText")
                    if not text then return end

                    -- The game constantly rewrites text; detect numeric-only content as "raw money"
                    local currentText = text.Text
                    -- If the game just reset the label back to a plain number, reapply our format
                    if currentText:match("^%$?[%d,]+$") and currentText ~= lastRaw then
                        lastRaw = currentText
                        local display = ResolveDisplayText(currentText:gsub("%$", ""):gsub(",", ""))
                        if display then
                            text.Text = display
                        end
                    end
                end))
            end

            -- Helper: refresh visibility of all sub-option rows
            local function RefreshSubOptionVisibility(enabled)
                local isEnabled = enabled
                local isSimple  = isEnabled and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value == "Simple"
                local isCustomImg = isSimple and Options.CustomMoneyTextImageStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextImageStyle.Value == "Custom"

                if Toggles["HideMoneyButton"] and Toggles["HideMoneyButton"].GUI then 
                    Toggles["HideMoneyButton"].GUI.Visible = isEnabled 
                end
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextStyle"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextStyle"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextStyle"].GUI.Visible = isEnabled 
                end
                if Toggles["CustomMoneyTextCustomText"] and Toggles["CustomMoneyTextCustomText"].GUI then 
                    Toggles["CustomMoneyTextCustomText"].GUI.Visible = isEnabled 
                end
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"].GUI.Visible = isEnabled and Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.UseCustomText 
                end
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"].GUI.Visible = isSimple 
                end
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI.Visible = isCustomImg 
                end
            end

            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddToggle("CustomMoneyTextEnabled", {Text = "Custom Money Text", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled = a
                RefreshSubOptionVisibility(a)

                if CustomMoneyTextConnection then
                    CustomMoneyTextConnection:Disconnect()
                    CustomMoneyTextConnection = nil
                end
                if a then
                    CustomMoneyTextConnection = Utility.NewConnection(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
                        task.wait(1)
                        UpdateCustomMoneyText()
                        StartMoneyTextLiveUpdate()
                    end)
                    StartMoneyTextLiveUpdate()
                else
                    if CustomMoneyTextLiveConn then
                        CustomMoneyTextLiveConn:Disconnect()
                        CustomMoneyTextLiveConn = nil
                    end
                end
                task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, a and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value or "Default")
            end}):AddColorPicker("CustomMoneyTextColor", {Default = NewRGB(95, 255, 87), Title = "Color", Transparency = 0, Callback = function(a, t)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Color        = a
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Transparency = t or 0
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                local MainScreenGui = GetMainScreenGui()
                if not MainScreenGui then return end
                local text = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("MoneyText")
                if text then
                    text.TextColor3       = a
                    text.TextTransparency = t or 0
                end
            end})

            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddToggle("HideMoneyButton", {Text = "Hide Money Button", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.HideButton = a
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                local MainScreenGui = GetMainScreenGui()
                if not MainScreenGui then return end
                local p = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Promote")
                if p then p.Visible = not a end
            end})

            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddDropdown("CustomMoneyTextStyle", {Values = {"Default", "Simple"}, Default = "Default", Text = "Style", Multi = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Style = a
                local isSimple = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled and a == "Simple"
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextImageStyle"].GUI.Visible = isSimple 
                end
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI.Visible = isSimple and Options.CustomMoneyTextImageStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextImageStyle.Value == "Custom" 
                end
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, a)
            end})

            -- // Custom Text toggle + input
            -- Toggle to enable/disable the custom label text feature
            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddToggle("CustomMoneyTextCustomText", {Text = "Custom Text", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.UseCustomText = a
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"].GUI then
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextInput"].GUI.Visible = a and Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled
                end
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                if a then
                    StartMoneyTextLiveUpdate()
                else
                    if CustomMoneyTextLiveConn then
                        CustomMoneyTextLiveConn:Disconnect()
                        CustomMoneyTextLiveConn = nil
                    end
                    -- Revert to the game's live money text (just let the next game tick restore it)
                end
                task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value or "Default")
            end})

            -- Text input for the custom label. Supports {money} token for the live value.
            -- Examples: "Cash: {money}" → "Cash: 500"  |  "💰 {money}" → "💰 500"
            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddInput("CustomMoneyTextInput", {
                Default     = "",
                Numeric     = false,
                Finished    = false,
                Text        = "Label ({money} = value)",
                Placeholder = "e.g. 💰 {money} coins",
                Callback    = function(a)
                    Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.CustomText = a
                    if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                    if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.UseCustomText then return end
                    -- Restart live updater so it picks up the new format immediately
                    StartMoneyTextLiveUpdate()
                    task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value or "Default")
                end
            })

            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddDropdown("CustomMoneyTextImageStyle", {Values = {"None", "My Melody", "Custom"}, Default = "None", Text = "Image", Multi = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Image = a
                local isCustom = Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value == "Simple" and a == "Custom"
                if Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"] and Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI then 
                    Options["CustomMoneyTextImageInput"].GUI.Visible = isCustom 
                end
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value or "Default")
            end})

            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddInput("CustomMoneyTextImageInput", {Default = "", Numeric = false, Finished = false, Text = "Custom Image ID", Placeholder = "rbxassetid://...", Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.CustomImage = a
                if not Settings.Visuals.Hud.CustomMoneyText.Enabled then return end
                task.defer(UpdateCustomMoneyText, Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle and Options.CustomMoneyTextStyle.Value or "Default")
            end})

            -- Hide all sub-options on load (revealed when main toggle turns on)
            task.defer(function()
                RefreshSubOptionVisibility(false)
            end)
        end

        -- // Radar
        do
            Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddToggle("RadarEnabled", {Text = "Radar", Default = false, Tooltip = "Displays a minimap radar showing nearby players", Callback = function(a)

            local RadarPopup = Sections.Visuals.Hud:AddDependencyBox()
            RadarPopup:AddToggle("RadarTeamCheck", {Text = "Team Colors", Default = false, Tooltip = "Color players based on team (team/enemy)", Callback = function(a)

                RadarInfo.Health_Color = a

            RadarPopup:AddSlider("RadarScale", {Text = "Scale", Default = 1, Min = 0.5, Max = 5, Rounding = 1, Compact = true, Callback = function(a)
                RadarInfo.Scale = a

            RadarPopup:AddLabel("Colors"):AddColorPicker("RadarBackgroundColor", {Default = NewRGB(10, 10, 10), Title = "Background", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                RadarInfo.RadarBack = a

            RadarPopup:AddLabel("Border Color"):AddColorPicker("RadarBorderColor", {Default = NewRGB(75, 75, 75), Title = "Border", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                RadarInfo.RadarBorder = a
            end})
            RadarPopup:AddLabel("Enemy Color"):AddColorPicker("RadarEnemyColor", {Default = NewRGB(255, 0, 0), Title = "Enemy", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                RadarInfo.Enemy = a
            end})
            RadarPopup:AddLabel("Team Color"):AddColorPicker("RadarTeamColor", {Default = NewRGB(0, 255, 0), Title = "Team", Transparency = nil, Callback = function(a)
                RadarInfo.Team = a

                RadarInfo.LocalPlayerDot = a

        -- // View
        do
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("UnlockMaxZoomDistance", {Text = "Unlock Max Zoom Distance", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.View.InfiniteZoom = a
                LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = a and 9e9 or Table.World.MaxZoomDistance
            end})
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("RemovePepperSprayEffect", {Text = "Remove Pepper Spray Effect", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.View.NoBlur = a
            end})
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("SuppressFlashbangEffect", {Text = "Suppress Flashbang Effect", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.View.NoFlashbang = a
            end})
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("RemoveRecoil", {Text = "Remove Recoil", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.View.NoRecoil = a
            end})
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("HideGunCrosshair", {Text = "Hide Gun Crosshair", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Visuals.View.HideCrosshair = a
                
                -- Hide crosshair from gun
                task.spawn(function()
                    while Settings.Visuals.View.HideCrosshair do
                        local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                        if PlayerGui then
                            local MainScreenGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
                            if MainScreenGui then
                                local CrossHair = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Aim") or MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("CrossHair")
                                if CrossHair then
                                    CrossHair.Visible = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    -- Restore crosshair when disabled
                    local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                    if PlayerGui then
                        local MainScreenGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainScreenGui")
                        if MainScreenGui then
                            local CrossHair = MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Aim") or MainScreenGui:FindFirstChild("CrossHair")
                            if CrossHair then
                                CrossHair.Visible = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end})
            Sections.Visuals.View:AddToggle("FieldOfViewEnabled", {Text = "Field Of View", Default = false, Callback = function(a)

                Settings.Visuals.View.FieldOfView.Amount = a

            FieldOfView:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["FieldOfViewEnabled"], true}})

            end})
            local AspectRatio = Sections.Visuals.View:AddDependencyBox()

    -- // Player
    do

                Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.Enabled = a
                
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                
                if a then
                    if next(OriginalAnimations) == nil then
                        Misc.SaveOriginalAnimations(Character)
                    end
                    Misc.ApplyAnimationPack(Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.WhichAnimation)
                else
                    Misc.RestoreOriginalAnimations()
                end
            end})
            local AnimationPlayer = Sections.Player.Character:AddDependencyBox()
            AnimationPlayer:AddDropdown("WhichAnimationPlayer", {Values = AnimationPlayerOptions, Default = "Default", Text = "Which Animation", Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.WhichAnimation = a
                
                if Settings.Player.Character.AnimationPlayer.Enabled then
                    Misc.ApplyAnimationPack(a)
                end
            end})

            local EmoteSpam = Sections.Player.Character:AddToggle("EmoteSpamEnabled", {Text = "Emote Spam", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled = a
                local EmoteName = Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.WhichEmote
                local Track = EmoteTracks[EmoteName]
                
                if a and Track then
                    if CurrentPlaying and EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying] then
                        EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying]:Stop()
                    end
                    CurrentPlaying = EmoteName
                    Misc.PlayEmote(Track, EmoteName)
                elseif not a and Track then
                    Track:Stop()
                    CurrentPlaying = nil
                    if EmoteCoroutine then
                        coroutine.close(EmoteCoroutine)
                        EmoteCoroutine = nil
                    end
                end
            end})
            local EmoteSpamPopup = Sections.Player.Character:AddDependencyBox()
            if IsMobile then
                EmoteSpamPopup:AddButton({Text = "Load Button", Func = function()
                    if EmoteSpamAlreadyLoaded then
                        Library.Notify("Already loaded.")
                        return
                    end
                    
                    EmoteSpamAlreadyLoaded = true
                    
                    Utility.CreateButton("Emote Spam", function()
                        Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled = not Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled
                        Library:Notify("Emote Spam is now " .. (Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled and "ON" or "OFF"))
                    end)
                end})
            else
                EmoteSpam:AddKeyPicker("EmoteSpamKeybind", {Default = "None", NoUI = true, Text = "Emote Spam", Callback = function()
                    Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled = not Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled
                    Library:Notify("Emote Spam is now " .. (Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled and "ON" or "OFF"))
                end})
            end
            EmoteSpamPopup:AddDropdown("WhichEmoteSpam", {Values = EmoteSpamOptions, Default = "Floss", Text = "Which Emote", Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.WhichEmote = a
                if Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled then
                    if CurrentPlaying and EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying] then
                        EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying]:Stop()
                    end
                    
                    local Track = EmoteTracks[a]
                    if Track then
                        CurrentPlaying = a
                        Misc.PlayEmote(Track, a)
                    end
                end
            end})
            EmoteSpamPopup:AddSlider("EmoteSpamAnimationSpeed", {Text = "Playback Speed", Default = 1, Min = 0.1, Max = 5, Rounding = 1, Suffix = "x", Compact = true, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.PlaybackSpeed = a
                if Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled and CurrentPlaying then
                    local Track = EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying]
                    if Track then
                        Track:Stop()
                        Misc.PlayEmote(Track, CurrentPlaying)
                    end
                end
            end})
            EmoteSpamPopup:AddSlider("EmoteSpamFramePerSecond", {Text = "Frame Per Second", Default = 30, Min = 1, Max = 60, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "fps", Compact = true, Callback = function(a)
                Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.FramePerSecond = a
                if Settings.Player.Character.EmoteSpam.Enabled and CurrentPlaying then
                    local Track = EmoteTracks[CurrentPlaying]
                    if Track then
                        Track:Stop()
                        Misc.PlayEmote(Track, CurrentPlaying)
                    end
                end
            end})

                    if v:FindFirstChild("DataFolder") and v.DataFolder:FindFirstChild("Information") and v.DataFolder.Information:FindFirstChild("Codes") then
                        local Codes2 = HttpService:JSONDecode(v.DataFolder.Information.Codes.Value)
                        for i2, v2 in pairs(Codes2) do
                            if not Find(Codes, i2) then
                                table.insert(Codes, i2)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                local Eq = 0.1 * #Codes
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = #Codes .. " Codes Found", -- Required
                    Text = "ETA: " .. Floor(Eq) .. "s", -- Required
                })
                for i, v in pairs(Codes) do
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    print("[TRYING " .. v .. "]: " .. i .. "/" .. #Codes)
                    MainEvent:FireServer("EnterPromoCode", v)
                end
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = "Completed", -- Required
                    Text = "Server hop to try to find new codes.",
                })

            -- // Ping Changer (Hood Customs only)

                local PingChangerThread = nil
                local PingChangerActive = false

                Sections.Misc.Utilities:AddToggle("PingChangerEnabled", {Text = "Ping Changer", Default = false, Tooltip = "Spoofs your displayed ping in Hood Customs", Callback = function(a)
                    PingChangerActive = a
                    if not a then
                        if PingChangerThread then
                            task.cancel(PingChangerThread)
                            PingChangerThread = nil
                        end
                    else
                        local replicated = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                        local main = replicated:WaitForChild("MainEvent")
                        local mt = getrawmetatable(game)
                        setreadonly(mt, false)

                        if not getgenv()._PingChangerHooked then
                            getgenv()._PingChangerHooked = true
                            getgenv()._PingChangerBlock = true
                            local old = mt.__namecall
                            mt.__namecall = newcclosure(function(self, ...)
                                local args = {...}
                                local method = getnamecallmethod()
                                if method == "FireServer" and self == main and args[1] == "GetPing" then
                                    if getgenv()._PingChangerBlock and not checkcaller() then
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                                return old(self, ...)
                            end)
                        end

                        PingChangerThread = task.spawn(function()
                            while PingChangerActive do
                                local currentPing = (Settings.Misc.Utilities.PingChanger.Amount or 99) / 1000
                                getgenv()._PingChangerBlock = true
                                getgenv()._PingChangerBlock = false
                                main:FireServer("GetPing", currentPing)
                                getgenv()._PingChangerBlock = true
                                task.wait(currentPing)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end})

                local PingChangerBox = Sections.Misc.Utilities:AddDependencyBox()
                PingChangerBox:AddSlider("PingChangerAmount", {Text = "Ping (ms)", Default = 99, Min = 0, Max = 3000, Rounding = 0, Suffix = "ms", Compact = true, Callback = function(a)
                    Settings.Misc.Utilities.PingChanger.Amount = a
                end})

            end

            -- // Auto Reload HC (Hood Customs only)
            if GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment" then
                local AutoReloadHCConnection = nil
                local AutoReloadHCLast = {}
                local AutoReloadHCCooldown = 1
                local AutoReloadHCTools = {
                    "[Revolver]",
                    "[DoubleBarrel]",
                    "[TacticalShotgun]",
                    "[SMG]",
                    "[Shotgun]",
                    "[Silencer]"
                }
                local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

                Sections.Misc.Other:AddToggle("AutoReloadHCEnabled", {Text = "Auto Reload HC", Default = false, Tooltip = "Automatically reloads supported Hood Customs weapons when empty", Callback = function(a)
                    Settings.Misc.Other.AutoReloadHC = a
                    if AutoReloadHCConnection then
                        AutoReloadHCConnection:Disconnect()
                        AutoReloadHCConnection = nil
                    end
                    if a then
                        AutoReloadHCLast = {}
                        AutoReloadHCConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                            if not char then return end
                            for _, toolName in ipairs(AutoReloadHCTools) do
                                local tool = char:FindFirstChild(toolName)
                                if tool and (not AutoReloadHCLast[toolName] or tick() - AutoReloadHCLast[toolName] >= AutoReloadHCCooldown) then
                                    local s = tool:FindFirstChild("Script")
                                    local ammo = s and s:FindFirstChild("Ammo")
                                    if ammo and ammo:IsA("IntValue") and ammo.Value == 0 then
                                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.R, false, nil)
                                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.R, false, nil)
                                        AutoReloadHCLast[toolName] = tick()
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end})
            end
        end

                end
            end})
            Sections.Misc.Players:AddButton({Text = "Clear Ragebot Target", Func = function()
                if Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled then
                    if Table.Ragebot.Target then
                        local PreviousTarget = Table.Ragebot.Target.DisplayName or Table.Ragebot.Target.Name
                        Table.Ragebot.Target = nil
                        Table.Ragebot.IsTargetting = false

                        Library:Notify("No target to clear.")

            if (GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Da Hood Entertainment") then
                local SavedCFrame = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                Sections.Misc.Players:AddToggle("AutoKillEnabled", {Text = "Auto Kill", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                    if a == true then
                        SavedCFrame = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                    end
                    Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled = a
                    if a == false then
                        LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = SavedCFrame
                    end
                end})
                local AutoKill = Sections.Misc.Players:AddDependencyBox()
                AutoKill:AddToggle("AutoKillDesyncPosition", {Text = "Desync Position", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                    Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Desync = a
                end})
                AutoKill:AddToggle("AutoKillIndicator", {Text = "Indicator", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                    Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Indicator = a
                end})
               -- one of them is causing an error btw AutoKill:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["AutoKillEnabled"], true}})
                Sections.Misc.Players:AddButton({Text = "Knock", Func = function()
                    local OriginalCFrame = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").CFrame
                    while task.wait() do
                        local Target = SelectedPlayer
                        if not LocalPlayer or not LocalPlayer.Character then continue end
                    
                        local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local Tool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                    
                        if SelectedPlayer ~= nil then
                            if Target and Target.Character and HumanoidRootPart and Tool and Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
                                local TargetHRP = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                local TargetHead = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local TargetPart = Target.Character.SpecialParts and Target.Character.SpecialParts:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                local TargetPosition = TargetHRP and TargetHRP.Position
                                local Handle = Tool and Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                                local Origin = (Handle and Handle.CFrame.Position) or (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") and LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position)
                                local Direction = (TargetPosition and Origin) and (TargetPosition - Origin).Unit or NewVector3()
                    
                                local Knocked = false
                                local BodyEffects = Target.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
                                if BodyEffects then
                                    local KO = BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("K.O")
                                    Knocked = KO and KO.Value == true
                                end
                    
                                if not Knocked and TargetHead and TargetHRP then
                                    if (not Target.Character:FindFirstChild("ForceField")) or (Tool.Name == "[Rifle]") then
                                        HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = NewCFrame(TargetHRP.Position + NewVector3(Random(-25, 25), Random(-25, 25), Random(-25, 25)))
                                        task.wait()
                    
                                        MainEvent:FireServer("ShootGun", Handle, Handle.Position, TargetHead.Position, TargetHead, NewVector3(0, 1, 0))
                    
                                        if Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Enabled then
                                            if Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Type == "Beam" then
                                                Utility.CreateBeam(
                                                    Handle.Position,
                                                    TargetHead.Position,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Texture,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Duration,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration
                                                )
                                            else
                                                Utility.CreateDrawingBeam(
                                                    Handle.Position,
                                                    TargetHead.Position,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient1,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Color.Gradient2,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Duration,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Enabled,
                                                    Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletTracers.Fade.Duration
                                                )
                                            end
                                        end
                    
                                        if Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Enabled then
                                            Utility.CreateImpact(
                                                Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Color,
                                                Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Size,
                                                Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Enabled,
                                                Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Fade.Duration,
                                                Settings.Visuals.General.Game.BulletImpacts.Duration,
                                                TargetHead.Position
                                            )
                                        end
                                    else
                                        HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = NewCFrame(50000, 10000, 0) + NewVector3(Random(-25, 25), Random(-25, 0), Random(-25, 25))
                                        Library:Notify("Waiting.. Target has spawn protection.")
                                        MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", Tool)
                                    end
                                else
                                    if Target.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") and Target.Character.BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("SDeath") and Target.Character.BodyEffects.SDeath.Value == false then
                                        if Target.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Target.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                                            HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = NewCFrame(Target.Character.UpperTorso.Position + NewVector3(0,3,0))
                                            MainEvent:FireServer("Stomp")
                                        end
                                    elseif Target.Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") and Target.Character.BodyEffects:FindFirstChild("SDeath") and Target.Character.BodyEffects.SDeath.Value == true then
                                        task.wait(1)
                                        Library:Notify("Target has been killed.")
                                        HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = OriginalCFrame
                                        return
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                Library:Notify("Please equip your gun before using 'Knock Target'.")
                                return
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end})
                Sections.Misc.Players:AddButton({Text = "Stomp", Func = function()
                    
                end})
                Sections.Misc.Players:AddButton({Text = "Bring", Func = function()
                    
                end})
                Sections.Misc.Players:AddButton({Text = "Void", Func = function()
                    
                end})
