    -- // Settings
    do
        -- // Menu
        do
            if not IsMobile then
                Sections.Settings.Menu:AddToggle("KeybindsListEnabled", {Text = "Keybinds List", Default = false, Tooltip = nil, Callback = function(a)
                    Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = a
                end})
            end
            Sections.Settings.Menu:AddToggle("WatermarkEnabled", {Text = "Watermark", Default = false, Tooltip = nil, Callback = function(a)
                Library:SetWatermarkVisibility(a)
            end})
            if IsMobile then
                local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
                local MainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                local ToggleButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

                ScreenGui.Name = "freemium.lolMobileUI"
                ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
                ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

                MainFrame.Parent = ScreenGui
                MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 0)
                MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)

                ToggleButton.Parent = MainFrame
                ToggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = NewRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ToggleButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0
                ToggleButton.BorderColor3 = NewRGB(25, 25, 25)
                ToggleButton.BorderSizePixel = 2
                ToggleButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                ToggleButton.Text = "F"
                ToggleButton.TextColor3 = NewRGB(0, 0, 0)
                ToggleButton.TextSize = 28
                ToggleButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                ToggleButton.AutoButtonColor = true


                local dragging = false
                local dragInput, dragStart, startPos

                local function update(input)
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
                        startPos.X.Scale,
                        startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                        startPos.Y.Scale,
                        startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                    )
                end

                ToggleButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        dragging = true
                        dragStart = input.Position
                        startPos = MainFrame.Position

                        input.Changed:Connect(function()
                            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                                dragging = false
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)

                ToggleButton.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                        dragInput = input
                    end
                end)

                UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                    if input == dragInput and dragging then
                        update(input)
                    end
                end)

                Utility.NewConnection(ToggleButton.MouseButton1Down, function()
                    task.spawn(Library.Toggle)
                end)

                local function OnCharacterAdded()
                    ScreenGui.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
                end

                LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(OnCharacterAdded)

                Utility.NewConnection(LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
                    ScreenGui.Parent = nil
                end)
            else
                Sections.Settings.Menu:AddLabel("Menu bind"):AddKeyPicker("MenuKeybind", {Default = "End", NoUI = true, Text = "Menu keybind"})
                Library.ToggleKeybind = Options.MenuKeybind
            end
            Sections.Settings.Menu:AddButton("Unload Menu", function()
                Library:Unload()
            end)
            Sections.Settings.Menu:AddButton("Unload Cheat", function()
                
            end)
        end
        
        -- // Server
        do
            Sections.Settings.Server:AddButton("Rejoin Server", function()
                LocalPlayer:Kick("Rejoining")
                wait()
                TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, LocalPlayer)
            end)
            Sections.Settings.Server:AddButton("Server Hop", function()
                local Sigma = {}
            	for _, v in ipairs(HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGetAsync("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")).data) do
            		if type(v) == "table" and v.maxPlayers > v.playing and v.id ~= game.JobId then
            			Sigma[#Sigma + 1] = v.id
            		end
            	end
            	if #Sigma > 0 then
            		TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, Sigma[Random(1, #Sigma)])
            	else
            		Library:Notify("Failed to find server.")
            	end
            end)
            Sections.Settings.Server:AddButton("Copy Job ID", function()
                if setclipboard then
                    setclipboard(game.JobId)
                    Library:Notify("Copied Job ID to clipboard.")
                else
                    Library:Notify("Clipboard not supported.")
                end
            end)
            Sections.Settings.Server:AddInput("ServerJobID", {Default = nil, Numeric = false, Finished = false, Text = "Job ID", Placeholder = "Enter Job ID", Callback = function(a)
                getgenv().JobID = a
            end})
            Sections.Settings.Server:AddButton("Join Server", function()
                if getgenv().JobID and getgenv().JobID ~= "" then
                    TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, getgenv().JobID, LocalPlayer)
                else
                    Library:Notify("Please input a valid Job ID.")
                end
            end)
        end
        
        end
        
        -- // XoraSA Silent Aim Tab
        do
            -- ── Config ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            getgenv().SilentAim = {
                Accuracy = 100,
                BodyPart = "HumanoidRootPart",
                Prediction = {
                    Enabled = false,
                    Method = "Manual",
                    Value = 0.14366,
                    Automatic = {
                        Add = 0,
                        Multiply = 1,
                        Divide = 800,
                    },
                },
                Offset = {
                    Enabled = false,
                    Jumping = -1.75,
                    Falling = -1,
                },
                Resovler = {
                    Enabled = false,
                    Method = "recalculatevelocity",
                },
                Ignore = {
                    Enabled = false,
                    OffScreen = true,
                    Dead = true,
                    Knocked = true,
                    Grabbed = true,
                    BehindWall = true,
                    SpawnProtection = true,
                    Friends = false,
                },
                FOVCircle = {
                    Visible = false,
                    Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
                    Transparency = 1,
                    Filled = false,
                    Radius = 120,
                    Position = "Center",
                },
            }

            local XSA = getgenv().SilentAim

            -- ── Services & locals (scoped, no globals polluted) ────────────────────
            local XSA_RunService    = RunService
            local XSA_Players       = Players
            local XSA_LocalPlayer   = LocalPlayer
            local XSA_GuiService    = game:GetService("GuiService")
            local XSA_GuiInset      = XSA_GuiService:GetGuiInset()

            -- Ping
            local XSA_LocalPing = 0
            local XSA_DataPing  = nil
            pcall(function()
                local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
                local Net   = Stats:FindFirstChild("Network") or Stats:WaitForChild("Network", 10)
                local SSI   = Net and (Net:FindFirstChild("ServerStatsItem") or Net:WaitForChild("ServerStatsItem", 10))
                local DP    = SSI and (SSI:FindFirstChild("Data Ping") or SSI:WaitForChild("Data Ping", 10))
                XSA_DataPing = DP
            end)

            -- Character locals (updated on CharacterAdded/Removing)
            local XSA_LocalCharacter = XSA_LocalPlayer.Character or XSA_LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            local XSA_LocalHumanoid  = XSA_LocalCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or XSA_LocalCharacter:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 15)
            local XSA_LocalRootPart  = XSA_LocalHumanoid and (XSA_LocalHumanoid.RootPart or XSA_LocalCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15))
            local XSA_LocalHead      = XSA_LocalCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head") or XSA_LocalCharacter:WaitForChild("Head", 15)
            local XSA_LocalGui       = XSA_LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or XSA_LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 15)
            local XSA_LocalBackpack  = XSA_LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") or XSA_LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack", 15)

            -- Camera
            local XSA_Camera     = Camera
            local XSA_ScreenSize = XSA_Camera.ViewportSize

            -- State
            local XSA_TargetPlayer = nil
            local XSA_PlayersList  = {}
            local XSA_Entities     = {}
            local XSA_Variables    = {}

            -- ── FOV Circle ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            local XSA_FOVCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
            XSA_FOVCircle.Visible      = XSA.FOVCircle.Visible
            XSA_FOVCircle.Transparency = XSA.FOVCircle.Transparency
            XSA_FOVCircle.Color        = XSA.FOVCircle.Color
            XSA_FOVCircle.Filled       = XSA.FOVCircle.Filled
            XSA_FOVCircle.Radius       = XSA.FOVCircle.Radius
            XSA_FOVCircle.Position     = XSA_ScreenSize / 2

            -- ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            local function XSA_GetCharacterStats(Character)
                return Character:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects") or Character:FindFirstChild("BD")
            end

            local function XSA_IsPlayerBehindWall(Player, Origin)
                if not (Player and Player.Character and XSA_LocalHumanoid and XSA_LocalRootPart) then return true end
                local Character = Player.Character
                local Humanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local RootPart  = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
                local Head      = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if not (Humanoid and RootPart and Head) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
                local RayParams = RaycastParams.new()
                RayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                RayParams.IgnoreWater = true
                RayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {XSA_LocalCharacter, XSA_Camera}
                local IsBehindWall = true
                Origin = Origin or (XSA_LocalHead and XSA_LocalHead.Position) or RootPart.Position
                for _, Part in {Head, RootPart} do
                    if not IsBehindWall then break end
                    local RayResults = workspace:Raycast(Origin, (Part.Position - Origin).Unit * 10000, RayParams)
                    if not (RayResults and RayResults.Instance) then continue end
                    IsBehindWall = not RayResults.Instance:IsDescendantOf(Character)
                end
                return IsBehindWall
            end

            local function XSA_IsPlayerDead(Player)
                if not (Player and Player.Character) then return true end
                local Character = Player.Character
                local Humanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local RootPart  = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
                if not (Humanoid and RootPart) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
                local IsDead = Character:GetAttribute("Dead")
                if IsDead == true then return true end
                local Stats = XSA_GetCharacterStats(Character)
                local DeadVal = Stats and Stats:FindFirstChild("Dead")
                if DeadVal and DeadVal.ClassName == "BoolValue" and DeadVal.Value then return true end
                return false
            end

            local function XSA_IsPlayerKnocked(Player)
                if not (Player and Player.Character) then return true end
                local Character = Player.Character
                local Humanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local RootPart  = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
                if not (Humanoid and RootPart) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
                local IsKnocked = Character:GetAttribute("Knocked")
                if IsKnocked == true then return true end
                local Stats = XSA_GetCharacterStats(Character)
                local KnockVal = Stats and (Stats:FindFirstChild("Knocked") or Stats:FindFirstChild("K.O"))
                if KnockVal and KnockVal.ClassName == "BoolValue" and KnockVal.Value then return true end
                return false
            end

            local function XSA_IsPlayerGrabbed(Player)
                if not (Player and Player.Character) then return true end
                local Character = Player.Character
                local Humanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local RootPart  = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
                if not (Humanoid and RootPart) or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then return true end
                for _, Child in Character:GetChildren() do
                    local Name = Child.Name:lower()
                    if Child.ClassName ~= "Weld" or Name ~= "grabbing_constraint" then continue end
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end

            local function XSA_GetTargetPlayer()
                if not (XSA_LocalHumanoid and XSA_LocalRootPart) then return nil end
                if XSA_IsPlayerKnocked(XSA_LocalPlayer) or XSA_IsPlayerDead(XSA_LocalPlayer) or XSA_IsPlayerGrabbed(XSA_LocalPlayer) then return nil end
                local Best = { ScreenDistance = math.huge }

                for _, Player in XSA_PlayersList do
                    if Player == XSA_LocalPlayer or not Player.Character then continue end
                    local Character = Player.Character
                    local Humanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local RootPart  = Humanoid and Humanoid.RootPart
                    local Head      = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if not (RootPart and Head) then continue end
                    local Tool = XSA_LocalCharacter and XSA_LocalCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

                    if XSA.Ignore.Enabled then
                        if XSA.Ignore.BehindWall      and XSA_IsPlayerBehindWall(Player, Tool and Tool:GetPivot().Position) then continue end
                        if XSA.Ignore.SpawnProtection  and Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")                    then continue end
                        if XSA.Ignore.Friends          and XSA_LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(Player.UserId)                      then continue end
                        if XSA.Ignore.Dead             and XSA_IsPlayerDead(Player)                                          then continue end
                        if XSA.Ignore.Knocked          and XSA_IsPlayerKnocked(Player)                                       then continue end
                        if XSA.Ignore.Grabbed          and XSA_IsPlayerGrabbed(Player)                                       then continue end
                    end

                    for _, Child in Character:GetChildren() do
                        if not Child:IsA("BasePart") or Child.Transparency == 1 then continue end
                        local ScreenPos, OnScreen = XSA_Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(Child.Position)
                        if XSA.Ignore.OffScreen and not OnScreen then continue end
                        local SP   = Vector2.new(ScreenPos.X, ScreenPos.Y)
                        local Dist = (SP - XSA_FOVCircle.Position).Magnitude
                        if Dist > Best.ScreenDistance or Dist > XSA_FOVCircle.Radius then continue end
                        Best.Self            = Player
                        Best.Character       = Character
                        Best.Humanoid        = Humanoid
                        Best.RootPart        = RootPart
                        Best.Head            = Head
                        Best.ClosestBodyPart = Child
                        Best.ScreenDistance  = Dist
                    end
                end

                return Best.Self and Best or nil
            end

            -- ── Main Heartbeat ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            XSA_RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(DeltaTime)
                -- Ping update
                task.spawn(function()
                    if XSA_DataPing then
                        XSA_LocalPing = XSA_DataPing:GetValue()
                    end
                end)

                -- FOV circle position
                task.spawn(function()
                    local Pos = XSA.FOVCircle.Position:lower():gsub("%s+", "")
                    if Pos == "crosshair" and XSA_LocalGui then
                        for _, Child in XSA_LocalGui:GetChildren() do
                            if Child.ClassName ~= "ScreenGui" then continue end
                            local Crosshair  = Child:FindFirstChild("Aim", true)
                            local IsCrosshair = Crosshair and (
                                Crosshair:FindFirstChild("CursorImage") or
                                Crosshair:FindFirstChild("Bottom") or
                                Crosshair:FindFirstChild("Top") or
                                Crosshair:FindFirstChild("Left") or
                                Crosshair:FindFirstChild("Right")
                            )
                            if not (Crosshair and IsCrosshair) then continue end
                            XSA_FOVCircle.Position = Crosshair.AbsolutePosition + Vector2.new(
                                Crosshair.AbsoluteSize.X / 2,
                                XSA_GuiInset.Y + Crosshair.AbsoluteSize.Y / 2
                            )
                            break
                        end
                    else
                        XSA_FOVCircle.Position = XSA_Camera.ViewportSize / 2
                    end
                end)

                -- Target resolution + prediction
                task.spawn(function()
                    XSA_TargetPlayer = XSA_GetTargetPlayer()
                    if not XSA_TargetPlayer then return end

                    local Prediction = XSA.Prediction
                    local Offset     = XSA.Offset
                    local Resolver   = XSA.Resovler

                    -- Resolve body part
                    local BPName = XSA.BodyPart:lower():gsub("%s+", "")
                    local AimPart =
                        (BPName == "head") and XSA_TargetPlayer.Head or
                        (BPName == "humanoidrootpart" or BPName == "rootpart" or BPName == "rp" or BPName == "hrp") and XSA_TargetPlayer.RootPart or
                        (BPName == "closest" or BPName == "closesttocrosshair" or BPName == "closestbodypart") and XSA_TargetPlayer.ClosestBodyPart or
                        XSA_TargetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(XSA.BodyPart) or XSA_TargetPlayer.RootPart

                    XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPart     = AimPart
                    XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition = AimPart.Position

                    local Velocity = AimPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity

                    if Prediction.Enabled then
                        if Resolver.Enabled then
                            local Method       = Resolver.Method:lower():gsub("%s+", "")
                            local LastPosition = XSA_Variables["LTPAPP"] or Vector3.zero
                            local DeltaPos     = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPart.Position - LastPosition
                            local RecalcVel    = DeltaPos / DeltaTime
                            Velocity = Method == "recalculatevelocity" and RecalcVel
                                or (Method == "humanoidvelocity" and XSA_TargetPlayer.Humanoid.MoveDirection * Vector3.new(RecalcVel.X, 0, RecalcVel.Z).Magnitude)
                                or RecalcVel
                            XSA_Variables["LTPAPP"] = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPart.Position
                        end

                        local Method = Prediction.Method:lower():gsub("%s+", "")
                        if Method == "manual" then
                            XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition + (Velocity * Prediction.Value)
                        elseif Method == "automatic" then
                            local PF = (XSA_LocalPing / Prediction.Automatic.Divide * Prediction.Automatic.Multiply + Prediction.Automatic.Add)
                            XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition + (Velocity * PF)
                        end
                    end

                    if Offset.Enabled then
                        if Velocity.Y >= 10 and XSA_TargetPlayer.Humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air then
                            XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition + Vector3.new(0, Offset.Jumping, 0)
                        elseif XSA_TargetPlayer.Humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
                            and XSA_TargetPlayer.Humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
                            and Velocity.Y <= -10 then
                            XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition + Vector3.new(0, Offset.Falling, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end)

            -- ── Character Added / Removing ────────────────────────────────────────
            XSA_LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(Character)
                XSA_LocalCharacter = Character
                XSA_LocalHumanoid  = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 15)
                XSA_LocalRootPart  = XSA_LocalHumanoid.RootPart or Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15)
                XSA_LocalHead      = Character:FindFirstChild("Head") or Character:WaitForChild("Head", 15)
                XSA_LocalBackpack  = XSA_LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") or XSA_LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("Backpack", 15)
                XSA_LocalGui       = XSA_LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or XSA_LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 15)
            end)

            XSA_LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
                XSA_LocalCharacter = nil
                XSA_LocalHumanoid  = nil
                XSA_LocalRootPart  = nil
                XSA_LocalHead      = nil
                XSA_LocalBackpack  = nil
                XSA_LocalGui       = nil
            end)

            -- ── Player list tracking ──────────────────────────────────────────────
            for _, Player in XSA_Players:GetPlayers() do
                table.insert(XSA_PlayersList, Player)
            end
            XSA_Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(Player)
                table.insert(XSA_PlayersList, Player)
            end)
            XSA_Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(Player)
                local Idx = table.find(XSA_PlayersList, Player)
                if Idx then table.remove(XSA_PlayersList, Idx) end
            end)

            -- ── Entity tracking ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
            for _, Desc in workspace:GetDescendants() do
                if Desc.ClassName ~= "Model" then continue end
                local Hum  = Desc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local Root = Hum and Hum.RootPart or Desc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not (Hum and Root) or Hum.Health <= 0 then continue end
                table.insert(XSA_Entities, Desc)
            end
            workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(Desc)
                if Desc.ClassName ~= "Model" then return end
                local Hum  = Desc:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or Desc:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                local Root = Hum and Hum.RootPart or Desc:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                if not (Hum and Root) or Hum.Health <= 0 then return end
                table.insert(XSA_Entities, Desc)
            end)
            workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(Desc)
                local Idx = table.find(XSA_Entities, Desc)
                if Idx then table.remove(XSA_Entities, Idx) end
            end)

            -- ── Hooks ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
            if GameInformation.Creator.Name == "Hood Customs Entertainment" then
                local HCEnv, HCGlobal
                for _, Inst in getnilinstances() do
                    if Inst.ClassName ~= "LocalScript" or Inst.Name ~= "LocalScript" then continue end
                    local Env = getsenv(Inst)
                    if type(Env) ~= "table" or type(Env.mobileShiftLockMousePos) ~= "function" then continue end
                    Inst.Name = "Guns Framework"
                    HCEnv    = Env
                    HCGlobal = Env._G
                    break
                end
                if HCEnv and HCGlobal then
                    HCEnv._G = setmetatable({}, {
                        __index    = HCGlobal,
                        __newindex = function(Self, Key, Value)
                            HCGlobal[Key] = Value
                            if Key == "MOUSE_POSITION" and XSA_TargetPlayer and math.random(100) <= XSA.Accuracy then
                                HCGlobal[Key] = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition
                            end
                        end
                    })
                    XSA_RunService.PostSimulation:Connect(function()
                        HCGlobal["MOUSE_POSITION"] = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                end
            else
                -- Mouse.Hit hook
                local XSA_Mouse = XSA_LocalPlayer:GetMouse()
                local XSA_MouseHook
                XSA_MouseHook = hookmetamethod(XSA_Mouse, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, key)
                    if key == "Hit" and XSA_TargetPlayer and XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition and math.random(100) <= XSA.Accuracy then
                        return CFrame.new(XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition)
                    end
                    return XSA_MouseHook(self, key)
                end))

                -- Ray.new hook
                local XSA_RayNew
                XSA_RayNew = hookfunction(Ray.new, newcclosure(function(Origin, Direction)
                    if checkcaller() then return XSA_RayNew(Origin, Direction) end
                    local CS = getcallingscript()
                    if CS and CS.Name == "CameraModule" then return XSA_RayNew(Origin, Direction) end
                    if not XSA_TargetPlayer or math.random(100) > XSA.Accuracy then return XSA_RayNew(Origin, Direction) end
                    local Name = debug.info(5, "n")
                    if Name == "CastRay" then
                        Direction = (XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition - Origin).Unit * 10000
                    end
                    return XSA_RayNew(Origin, Direction)
                end))

                -- Namecall hook
                local XSA_NameCall
                XSA_NameCall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(Self, ...)
                    if checkcaller() then return XSA_NameCall(Self, ...) end
                    local Arguments = {...}
                    local Method    = getnamecallmethod()

                    if XSA_TargetPlayer and XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition and math.random(100) <= XSA.Accuracy then
                        if Method == "FireServer" and Self.ClassName == "RemoteEvent" then
                            if type(Arguments[1]) == "string" then
                                if typeof(Arguments[2]) == "Vector3" then
                                    Arguments[2] = XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition
                                elseif typeof(Arguments[2]) == "table" then
                                    for Index, Value in Arguments[2] do
                                        Arguments[2][Index] = typeof(Value) == "CFrame" and CFrame.new(XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition)
                                            or typeof(Value) == "Vector3" and XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition or Value
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            return XSA_NameCall(Self, unpack(Arguments))
                        end

                        if Self == workspace and Method:lower():find("cast") then
                            local M = Method:lower()
                            if     M == "raycast"    then Arguments[2] = (XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition - Arguments[1]).Unit * 10000
                            elseif M == "shapecast"  then Arguments[2] = (XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition - Arguments[1].Position).Unit * 10000
                            elseif M == "spherecast" then Arguments[3] = (XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition - Arguments[1]).Unit * 10000
                            elseif M == "blockcast"  then Arguments[3] = (XSA_TargetPlayer.AimPosition - Arguments[1].Position).Unit * 10000
                            end
                            return XSA_NameCall(Self, unpack(Arguments))
                        end
                    end

                    return XSA_NameCall(Self, ...)
                end))
            end

            -- ── UI ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

            -- General (left)
            Sections.XoraSA.General:AddToggle("XoraSAEnabled", {Text = "Enabled", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.Enabled = a
            end})
            Sections.XoraSA.General:AddDropdown("XoraSABodyPart", {
                Values = {"HumanoidRootPart", "Head", "Closest"},
                Default = "HumanoidRootPart", Multi = false, Text = "Body Part",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.BodyPart = a end
            })
            Sections.XoraSA.General:AddSlider("XoraSAAccuracy", {
                Text = "Accuracy", Default = 100, Min = 1, Max = 100,
                Rounding = 0, Suffix = "%", Compact = true,
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Accuracy = a end
            })

            -- Jump / Fall Offset
            Sections.XoraSA.General:AddToggle("XoraSAOffsetEnabled", {Text = "Jump / Fall Offset", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.Offset.Enabled = a
            end})
            local XSA_OffsetBox = Sections.XoraSA.General:AddDependencyBox()
            XSA_OffsetBox:AddInput("XoraSAOffsetJumping", {
                Default = "-1.75", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Jumping Offset", Placeholder = "-1.75",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Offset.Jumping = tonumber(a) or XSA.Offset.Jumping end
            })
            XSA_OffsetBox:AddInput("XoraSAOffsetFalling", {
                Default = "-1", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Falling Offset", Placeholder = "-1",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Offset.Falling = tonumber(a) or XSA.Offset.Falling end
            })
            XSA_OffsetBox:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["XoraSAOffsetEnabled"], true}})

            -- Velocity Resolver
            Sections.XoraSA.General:AddToggle("XoraSAResolverEnabled", {Text = "Velocity Resolver", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.Resovler.Enabled = a
            end})
            local XSA_ResolverBox = Sections.XoraSA.General:AddDependencyBox()
            XSA_ResolverBox:AddDropdown("XoraSAResolverMethod", {
                Values = {"recalculatevelocity", "humanoidvelocity"},
                Default = "recalculatevelocity", Multi = false, Text = "Method",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Resovler.Method = a end
            })
            XSA_ResolverBox:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["XoraSAResolverEnabled"], true}})

            -- Prediction (right)
            Sections.XoraSA.Prediction:AddToggle("XoraSAPredictionEnabled", {Text = "Prediction", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.Prediction.Enabled = a
            end})
            local XSA_PredBox = Sections.XoraSA.Prediction:AddDependencyBox()
            XSA_PredBox:AddDropdown("XoraSAPredictionMethod", {
                Values = {"Manual", "Automatic"},
                Default = "Manual", Multi = false, Text = "Method",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Prediction.Method = a end
            })
            XSA_PredBox:AddInput("XoraSAPredictionValue", {
                Default = "0.14366", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Prediction Value", Placeholder = "0.14366",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Prediction.Value = tonumber(a) or XSA.Prediction.Value end
            })
            XSA_PredBox:AddLabel("Automatic Settings")
            XSA_PredBox:AddInput("XoraSAPredictionAutoDivide", {
                Default = "800", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Divide (ping)", Placeholder = "800",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Divide = tonumber(a) or XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Divide end
            })
            XSA_PredBox:AddInput("XoraSAPredictionAutoMultiply", {
                Default = "1", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Multiply", Placeholder = "1",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Multiply = tonumber(a) or XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Multiply end
            })
            XSA_PredBox:AddInput("XoraSAPredictionAutoAdd", {
                Default = "0", Numeric = true, Finished = true,
                Text = "Add", Placeholder = "0",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Add = tonumber(a) or XSA.Prediction.Automatic.Add end
            })
            XSA_PredBox:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["XoraSAPredictionEnabled"], true}})

            -- Ignore Filters (right)
            Sections.XoraSA.Ignore:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreEnabled", {Text = "Ignore Filters", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.Ignore.Enabled = a
            end})
            local XSA_IgnoreBox = Sections.XoraSA.Ignore:AddDependencyBox()
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreOffScreen",  {Text = "Off Screen",       Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.OffScreen       = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreDead",       {Text = "Dead",             Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.Dead            = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreKnocked",    {Text = "Knocked",          Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.Knocked         = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreGrabbed",    {Text = "Grabbed",          Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.Grabbed         = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreBehindWall", {Text = "Behind Wall",      Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.BehindWall      = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreSpawnProt",  {Text = "Spawn Protection", Default = false,  Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.SpawnProtection = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:AddToggle("XoraSAIgnoreFriends",    {Text = "Friends",          Default = false, Callback = function(a) XSA.Ignore.Friends         = a end})
            XSA_IgnoreBox:SetupDependencies({{Toggles["XoraSAIgnoreEnabled"], true}})

            -- FOV Circle (left bottom)
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddToggle("XoraSAFOVVisible", {Text = "Visible", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.FOVCircle.Visible  = a
                XSA_FOVCircle.Visible  = a
            end})
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddToggle("XoraSAFOVFilled", {Text = "Filled", Default = false, Callback = function(a)
                XSA.FOVCircle.Filled  = a
                XSA_FOVCircle.Filled  = a
            end})
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddSlider("XoraSAFOVRadius", {
                Text = "Radius", Default = 120, Min = 10, Max = 500,
                Rounding = 0, Compact = true,
                Callback = function(a)
                    XSA.FOVCircle.Radius  = a
                    XSA_FOVCircle.Radius  = a
                end
            })
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddSlider("XoraSAFOVTransparency", {
                Text = "Transparency", Default = 1, Min = 0, Max = 1,
                Rounding = 2, Compact = true,
                Callback = function(a)
                    XSA.FOVCircle.Transparency  = a
                    XSA_FOVCircle.Transparency  = a
                end
            })
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddLabel("FOV Color"):AddColorPicker("XoraSAFOVColor", {
                Default = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Title = "FOV Circle Color", Transparency = nil,
                Callback = function(a)
                    XSA.FOVCircle.Color  = a
                    XSA_FOVCircle.Color  = a
                end
            })
            Sections.XoraSA.FOV:AddDropdown("XoraSAFOVPosition", {
                Values = {"Center", "Crosshair"},
                Default = "Center", Multi = false, Text = "FOV Position",
                Callback = function(a) XSA.FOVCircle.Position = a end
            })
        end


       -- // freemium.lol CFG Configuration
do
    local ConfigSection = Tabs.Settings:AddLeftGroupbox("Configuration")

    ConfigNameInput = ConfigSection:AddInput("ConfigName", {
        Default = ConfigName,
        Numeric = false,
        Finished = true,
        Text = "Name config",
        Placeholder = "config name",
        Callback = function(Value)
            local Clean = SanitizeConfigName(Value)
            if Clean ~= "" then
                ConfigName = Clean
            end
        end
    })

    ConfigDropdown = ConfigSection:AddDropdown("ConfigList", {
        Values = {"< no cfg files >"},
        Default = "< no cfg files >",
        Multi = false,
        Text = "Config list",
        Callback = function(Value)
            if Value and Value ~= "< no cfg files >" then
                ConfigName = SanitizeConfigName(Value)
                if ConfigNameInput then
                    ConfigNameInput:SetValue(ConfigName)
                end
            end
        end
    })

    ConfigSection:AddButton("Create cfg", function()
        local Name = SanitizeConfigName(ConfigNameInput and ConfigNameInput.Value or ConfigName)
        ConfigName = Name
        if CreateConfig(Name) then
            RefreshConfigList()
        end
    end)

    ConfigSection:AddButton("Overwrite", function()
        SaveConfig(ConfigName)
        RefreshConfigList()
    end)

    ConfigSection:AddButton("Delete", function()
        DeleteConfig(ConfigName)
        RefreshConfigList()
    end)

    ConfigSection:AddButton("Refresh config list", function()
        RefreshConfigList()
        Library:Notify("Config list refreshed.", 2)
    end)

    ConfigSection:AddButton("Load cfg", function()
        LoadConfig(ConfigName)
    end)

    RefreshConfigList()
end
